- [ ] **Step 4: Write the account schemas**

`packages/contracts/src/accounts.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AccountId, ProjectId } from './ids';

type ValueOf<T> = T[keyof T];

/** Who pays for a project's sessions (accounts spec §4). The Codex kinds are reserved until the Codex adapter exists. */
export const AccountKind = {
  ClaudeSubscription: 'claude_subscription',
  AnthropicApiKey: 'anthropic_api_key',
  Bedrock: 'bedrock',
  Gateway: 'gateway',
  OpenaiApiKey: 'openai_api_key',
  CodexSignin: 'codex_signin',
} as const;
export type AccountKind = ValueOf<typeof AccountKind>;

/** The kinds 10db can run a session on today. */
export const SUPPORTED_ACCOUNT_KINDS = [
  AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription,
  AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey,
  AccountKind.Bedrock,
  AccountKind.Gateway,
] as const;
export type SupportedAccountKind = (typeof SUPPORTED_ACCOUNT_KINDS)[number];

const METERED: readonly AccountKind[] = [
  AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey,
  AccountKind.Bedrock,
  AccountKind.Gateway,
];

/** Billed per token: the SDK's dollars are real, and a monthly cap applies. */
export function isMetered(kind: AccountKind): boolean {
  return METERED.includes(kind);
}

/** Why a session on an account was refused, or why the account can't start one yet. */
export const AccountProblem = {
  Unconfirmed: 'unconfirmed',
  IdentityMismatch: 'identity_mismatch',
  WrongCredentials: 'wrong_credentials',
  SignedOut: 'signed_out',
  SecretMissing: 'secret_missing',
  SecretChanged: 'secret_changed',
  CannotBill: 'cannot_bill',
} as const;
export type AccountProblem = ValueOf<typeof AccountProblem>;

/** Why an account's lanes are paused. */
export const AccountPauseReason = {
  UsageLimit: 'usage_limit',
  MonthlyCap: 'monthly_cap',
  Refused: 'refused',
} as const;
export type AccountPauseReason = ValueOf<typeof AccountPauseReason>;

export const DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LANE_CAP = 4;
/** The seeded account's label: what it is, the builder's Claude plan. Who pays for it is `payer`. */
export const DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LABEL = 'Claude plan';

/** How pickers and card options name an account: its label, and its recorded identity beside it. */
export function accountDisplayName(account: {
  readonly label: string;
  readonly expectedIdentity: string | null;
}): string {
  return account.expectedIdentity === null
    ? account.label
    : `${account.label} · ${account.expectedIdentity}`;
}
const MAX_ACCOUNT_LANES = 16;
const MAX_MONTHLY_CAP_USD = 1_000_000;

/**
 * A Claude Code model alias as the router names profiles: `opus`, `sonnet`, `haiku`, `fable`. A model ID
 * never matches it, which is how the model check tells a requested alias from a mapped ID.
 */
export const MODEL_ALIAS_PATTERN = /^[a-z]+$/;
const Alias = z.string().regex(MODEL_ALIAS_PATTERN, 'a lowercase model alias');
const modelChoice = {
  /** The channel's model ID per alias, sent through the SDK's `model` option instead of the alias. */
  modelMap: z.record(Alias, z.string().trim().min(1).max(200)).optional(),
  /** The aliases the account offers; the router considers only these. Absent means every alias. */
  aliases: z.array(Alias).min(1).optional(),
};
const AwsRegion = z.string().regex(/^[a-z]{2}(-[a-z]+)+-\d+$/, 'an AWS region such as us-west-2');
const AwsProfile = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64}$/, 'an AWS profile name');
/** A gateway's endpoint. Credentials in it would land in the database, so it may carry none. */
const BaseUrl = z.url({ protocol: /^https$/ }).refine(
  (url) => {
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    return parsed.username === '' && parsed.password === '' && parsed.search === '';
  },
  { message: 'an https URL with no credentials and no query' },
);

export const SubscriptionSettings = z.strictObject({ ...modelChoice });
export const ApiKeySettings = z.strictObject({ ...modelChoice });
export const BedrockSettings = z.strictObject({
  ...modelChoice,
  region: AwsRegion,
  /** The AWS profile the session uses; null means a bearer token from the Keychain. */
  awsProfile: AwsProfile.nullable(),
});
export const GatewaySettings = z.strictObject({ ...modelChoice, baseUrl: BaseUrl });
export type SubscriptionSettings = z.infer<typeof SubscriptionSettings>;
export type ApiKeySettings = z.infer<typeof ApiKeySettings>;
export type BedrockSettings = z.infer<typeof BedrockSettings>;
export type GatewaySettings = z.infer<typeof GatewaySettings>;

export const AccountSettings = z.union([BedrockSettings, GatewaySettings, SubscriptionSettings]);
export type AccountSettings = z.infer<typeof AccountSettings>;

const SETTINGS_BY_KIND = {
  [AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription]: SubscriptionSettings,
  [AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey]: ApiKeySettings,
  [AccountKind.Bedrock]: BedrockSettings,
  [AccountKind.Gateway]: GatewaySettings,
} as const satisfies Record<SupportedAccountKind, z.ZodType>;

/** Stored or submitted settings, checked against the account's own kind. */
export function parseAccountSettings(kind: SupportedAccountKind, raw: unknown): AccountSettings {
  return SETTINGS_BY_KIND[kind].parse(raw);
}

/** Whether the account's credential is a Keychain secret: an API key, a gateway token or a Bedrock bearer token. */
export function needsSecret(kind: SupportedAccountKind, settings: AccountSettings): boolean {
  switch (kind) {
    case AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription:
      return false;
    case AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey:
    case AccountKind.Gateway:
      return true;
    case AccountKind.Bedrock:
      return 'awsProfile' in settings && settings.awsProfile === null;
  }
}

const Label = z.string().trim().min(1).max(80);
const Payer = z.string().trim().max(200);
const LaneCap = z.int().min(1).max(MAX_ACCOUNT_LANES);
const MonthlyCapUsd = z.number().positive().max(MAX_MONTHLY_CAP_USD);
/** An organization `accountInfo()` reports for a keyed account (spec §11: unverified for API keys and Bedrock). */
const Organization = z.string().trim().min(1).max(200);
const Timestamp = z.iso.datetime();

const common = {
  label: Label,
  payer: Payer.default(''),
  laneCap: LaneCap.default(DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LANE_CAP),
};
const metered = { monthlyCapUsd: MonthlyCapUsd.nullable().default(null) };
const organization = { expectedIdentity: Organization.nullable().default(null) };

/**
 * `POST /accounts`. A subscription's identity is confirmed after it signs in, so it takes none here;
 * a gateway is identified by its key's fingerprint, so it never takes one.
 */
export const AccountInput = z.discriminatedUnion(
  'kind',
  [
    z.strictObject({
      kind: z.literal(AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription),
      ...common,
      settings: SubscriptionSettings.default({}),
    }),
    z.strictObject({
      kind: z.literal(AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey),
      ...common,
      ...metered,
      ...organization,
      settings: ApiKeySettings.default({}),
    }),
    z.strictObject({
      kind: z.literal(AccountKind.Bedrock),
      ...common,
      ...metered,
      ...organization,
      settings: BedrockSettings,
    }),
    z.strictObject({
      kind: z.literal(AccountKind.Gateway),
      ...common,
      ...metered,
      settings: GatewaySettings,
    }),
  ],
  {
    error:
      'Choose claude_subscription, anthropic_api_key, bedrock or gateway; openai_api_key and codex_signin are reserved until the Codex adapter exists',
  },
);
export type AccountInput = z.input<typeof AccountInput>;
export type AccountConfig = z.output<typeof AccountInput>;

/** `PATCH /accounts/:id`. The kind never changes; settings and identity are checked against it by the daemon. */
export const AccountPatch = z
  .strictObject({
    label: Label,
    payer: Payer,
    laneCap: LaneCap,
    monthlyCapUsd: MonthlyCapUsd.nullable(),
    /** The email (subscription) or organization (API key, Bedrock) a session must report; null clears it. */
    expectedIdentity: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200).nullable(),
    /** The kind's whole settings object. */
    settings: z.record(z.string(), z.json()),
  })
  .partial()
  .refine((p) => Object.keys(p).length > 0, { message: 'Change at least one field' });
export type AccountPatch = z.infer<typeof AccountPatch>;

export const AccountDto = z.object({
  id: AccountId,
  kind: z.enum(SUPPORTED_ACCOUNT_KINDS),
  label: z.string(),
  payer: z.string(),
  expectedIdentity: z.string().nullable(),
  /**
   * The Claude Code config directory its sessions run on: 10db's own directory for every account but
   * the default account, which runs on the builder's login (null is Claude Code's default directory).
   */
  configDir: z.string().nullable(),
  settings: AccountSettings,
  laneCap: z.int(),
  monthlyCapUsd: z.number().nullable(),
  archived: z.boolean(),
  /** New projects bind to it when they name no account. */
  isDefault: z.boolean(),
  /** Whether 10db has recorded a secret for it. The secret itself never leaves the daemon. */
  secretSet: z.boolean(),
  createdAt: Timestamp,
  updatedAt: Timestamp,
});
export type AccountDto = z.infer<typeof AccountDto>;

/** How to sign a subscription account in with the unmodified `claude` (spec §5). */
export const AccountSignInDto = z.object({
  accountId: AccountId,
  configDir: z.string().nullable(),
  command: z.string(),
});
export type AccountSignInDto = z.infer<typeof AccountSignInDto>;

/** `POST /accounts`: the account, and its sign-in steps when it is a subscription. */
export const AccountCreatedDto = z.object({
  account: AccountDto,
  signIn: AccountSignInDto.nullable(),
});
export type AccountCreatedDto = z.infer<typeof AccountCreatedDto>;

/**
 * `PUT /accounts/:id/secret`. The secret reaches the Keychain inside double quotes on `security`'s
 * standard input, so it may hold no whitespace, quote or backslash.
 */
export const AccountSecretInput = z.strictObject({
  secret: z
    .string()
    .min(8)
    .max(8192)
    .regex(
      /^[\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e]+$/,
      'printable ASCII with no spaces, double quotes or backslashes',
    ),
});
export type AccountSecretInput = z.infer<typeof AccountSecretInput>;

/** Why a session on an account was refused. `seenIdentity` is what Claude Code reported, when it matters. */
export const AccountRefusal = z.object({
  problem: z.enum(AccountProblem),
  /** Names the account and what differed; never an email, organization or secret. */
  message: z.string(),
  seenIdentity: z.string().nullable(),
});
export type AccountRefusal = z.infer<typeof AccountRefusal>;

/** `GET /accounts/:id/identity`: what Claude Code reports for the account now, and the refusal a session would meet. */
export const AccountIdentityDto = z.object({
  accountId: AccountId,
  email: z.string().nullable(),
  organization: z.string().nullable(),
  apiProvider: z.string().nullable(),
  apiKeySource: z.string().nullable(),
  tokenSource: z.string().nullable(),
  /** Whether it reported a subscription plan; the plan's name is never compared or shown. */
  subscription: z.boolean(),
  refusal: AccountRefusal.nullable(),
});
export type AccountIdentityDto = z.infer<typeof AccountIdentityDto>;

export const Month = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/, 'a month as YYYY-MM');

/** Tokens and dollars over some sessions. `costUsd` is null for a subscription. */
export const UsageTotals = z.object({
  tokensIn: z.int().min(0),
  tokensOut: z.int().min(0),
  tokensCached: z.int().min(0),
  costUsd: z.number().min(0).nullable(),
});
export type UsageTotals = z.infer<typeof UsageTotals>;

/** `GET /accounts/:id/usage?month=YYYY-MM`: the month's sessions on the account, in total and per project. */
export const AccountUsageDto = z.object({
  accountId: AccountId,
  month: Month,
  total: UsageTotals,
  monthlyCapUsd: z.number().nullable(),
  byProject: z.array(UsageTotals.extend({ projectId: ProjectId, projectName: z.string() })),
});
export type AccountUsageDto = z.infer<typeof AccountUsageDto>;

/** The `detail` key of a card about an account. */
export const ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL = 'accountCard';

export const AccountCardDetail = z.object({
  accountId: AccountId,
  reason: z.enum(AccountPauseReason).nullable(),
  problem: z.enum(AccountProblem).nullable(),
  /** The identity a Confirm answer would record. */
  seenIdentity: z.string().nullable(),
});
export type AccountCardDetail = z.infer<typeof AccountCardDetail>;

/** The options on an account card: confirm a seen identity, try the account again after a fix, or wait. */
export const AccountCardChoice = { Confirm: 'Confirm', Resume: 'Resume', Later: 'Later' } as const;
export type AccountCardChoice = ValueOf<typeof AccountCardChoice>;
```

If zod's `discriminatedUnion` doesn't apply the `error` option to a discriminator no option matches, the reserved-kind rows fail on the message only: put the same text on the `kind` path with a `z.preprocess` that leaves the value unchanged, or check the two reserved kinds first with `.superRefine` on a wrapping `z.object({ kind: z.string() }).loose()`. The test's message rows decide.

### Task 2: The accounts table, the project and session columns, and usage

**Files:**

- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/entities.ts` (`accountId` on `ProjectInput`, `ProjectDto`, `ProjectPatch`)
- Modify: `packages/db/src/schema.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/projects.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/sessions.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/workstreams.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/test-support.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/index.ts`, `packages/db/src/index.ts`
- Create: `packages/db/src/store/accounts.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/usage.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/accounts.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts` (seed the default account on a fresh database, so `POST /projects` without an account binds to it)
- Modify (tests the new required column touches): `packages/db/src/schema.test.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/projects-tasks.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/events/events.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/src/test/fixtures.ts`
- Generated: `packages/db/drizzle/` (baseline regenerated), `packages/contracts/generated/*.schema.json`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's `AccountInput`, `AccountPatch`, `AccountDto`, `AccountId`, `AccountKind`, `SupportedAccountKind`, `AccountSettings`, `parseAccountSettings`, `needsSecret`, `isMetered`, `DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LABEL`, `DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LANE_CAP`, `UsageTotals`, `AccountUsageDto`, `EventType.AccountSaved`.
- Produces:
  - `ProjectInput.accountId?: AccountId` (absent means the default account); `ProjectDto.accountId: AccountId`; `ProjectPatch.accountId?: AccountId`.
  - `packages/db` (exported from the package entry):
    - `type AccountRow` (the row: `secretRef`, `secretFingerprint`, `settings: AccountSettings`, …)
    - `accountSecretRef(id: AccountId): string` → `account:<id>`
    - `insertAccount(ctx: WriteContext, input: AccountInput, opts: { configDirFor(id: AccountId): string }): AccountDto` (every kind gets `configDirFor(id)`; the caller creates the directory)
    - `getAccount(db: DbReader, id: AccountId): AccountDto`, `getAccountRow(db: DbReader, id: AccountId): AccountRow`
    - `listAccounts(db: DbReader, opts?: { includeArchived?: boolean }): AccountDto[]` (by label)
    - `patchAccount(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId, patch: AccountPatch): AccountDto`
    - `archiveAccount(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId): AccountDto`
    - `recordAccountSecret(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId, fingerprint: string): AccountDto`
    - `defaultAccountId(db: DbReader): AccountId | null`
    - `seedDefaultAccount(ctx: WriteContext, opts: { expectedIdentity: string | null; configDir?: string | null }): AccountDto | null` (null when an account already exists; `configDir` is the login shell's own Claude Code directory, and absent or null means Claude Code's default)
    - `interface UsageWindow { month: string; start: string; end: string }` (ISO instants, `start` inclusive, `end` exclusive)
    - `accountSpendUsd(db: DbReader, id: AccountId, window: UsageWindow): number`
    - `accountUsage(db: DbReader, id: AccountId, window: UsageWindow): AccountUsageDto`
    - `SessionRow` gains `accountId`, `cachedTokens`, and `costUsd: number | null`. `insertSession` records the workstream's project's account and starts `costUsd` at 0 (metered) or null. `updateSessionUsage` takes `cachedTokens` and never sets a cost on a session whose cost is null.
    - `LaneCounts.byAccount: ReadonlyMap<AccountId, number>`, beside `byProvider` (Task 4 removes `byProvider`).
    - `patchProject` takes `accountId`: `NotFoundError` for an unknown account, `InvalidError` for an archived one. Work in flight doesn't stop a move (Task 9 makes each lane's next session follow it).
    - Test support: `TEST_ACCOUNT_EMAIL = 'builder@example.com'`; `seedProject` first seeds the default account ("Claude plan", identity `TEST_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`) when there is none; `seedAccount(store: Store, input: AccountInput): AccountDto`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`packages/db/src/store/accounts.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ConflictError,
  EventType,
  InvalidError,
  NotFoundError,
  mintId,
  type AccountId,
  type ProjectDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  accountSecretRef,
  accountSpendUsd,
  accountUsage,
  archiveAccount,
  countLanes,
  createTestStore,
  defaultAccountId,
  getAccount,
  getAccountRow,
  getProject,
  insertSession,
  insertTask,
  insertWorkstream,
  listAccounts,
  listEventsAfter,
  patchAccount,
  patchProject,
  recordAccountSecret,
  seedAccount,
  seedDefaultAccount,
  seedProject,
  TEST_ACCOUNT_EMAIL,
  transitionWorkstream,
  updateSessionUsage,
  type Store,
} from './index';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let now: Date;
beforeEach(() => {
  now = new Date(2026, 9, 15, 12, 0);
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => now));
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

const OCTOBER = {
  month: '2026-10',
  start: new Date(2026, 9, 1).toISOString(),
  end: new Date(2026, 10, 1).toISOString(),
};
const apiKey = (label = 'Client key', monthlyCapUsd: number | null = 500) =>
  seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label, monthlyCapUsd });

/** One session of `usage` on a fresh task in `project`, started at `at`. */
function spend(
  project: ProjectDto,
  usage: { inputTokens: number; outputTokens: number; cachedTokens: number; costUsd: number },
  at: Date,
) {
  now = at;
  return store.write((ctx) => {
    const task = insertTask(ctx, project.id, {
      title: `t-${String(at.getTime())}`,
      acceptanceCriteria: ['x'],
      areas: ['api'],
    });
    const ws = insertWorkstream(ctx, task.id);
    const session = insertSession(ctx, {
      workstreamId: ws.id,
      provider: 'claude',
      model: 'sonnet',
      effort: 'medium',
      mode: 'managed',
      mcpTokenHash: `hash-${String(Math.random())}`,
    });
    updateSessionUsage(ctx, session.id, usage);
    return session;
  }).value;
}

describe('accounts', () => {
  it("seeds the default account once, as Claude plan, on the login's own directory", () => {
    const seeded = store.write((ctx) =>
      seedDefaultAccount(ctx, { expectedIdentity: null, configDir: '/Users/b/.claude-work' }),
    ).value;
    expect(seeded).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'claude_subscription',
      label: 'Claude plan',
      configDir: '/Users/b/.claude-work',
      expectedIdentity: null,
      laneCap: 4,
      isDefault: true,
      secretSet: false,
      archived: false,
    });
    expect(
      store.write((ctx) => seedDefaultAccount(ctx, { expectedIdentity: null })).value,
    ).toBeNull();
    expect(defaultAccountId(store.db)).toBe(seeded?.id);
  });

  it('gives every new account its own config directory, and a keyed account a Keychain item', () => {
    const defaultAccount = store.write((ctx) =>
      seedDefaultAccount(ctx, { expectedIdentity: null }),
    ).value;
    expect(defaultAccount?.configDir).toBeNull();
    const created = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Company A' });
    expect(created.configDir).toBe(`/tmp/10db-test-accounts/${created.id}/claude`);
    expect(created.isDefault).toBe(false);
    const key = apiKey();
    expect(getAccountRow(store.db, key.id)).toMatchObject({
      configDir: `/tmp/10db-test-accounts/${key.id}/claude`,
      secretRef: accountSecretRef(key.id),
      secretFingerprint: null,
    });
    expect(listAccounts(store.db).map((a) => a.label)).toEqual([
      'Claude plan',
      'Client key',
      'Company A',
    ]);
  });

  it('lets the builder rename the default account, and keeps labels unique', () => {
    const seeded = store.write((ctx) => seedDefaultAccount(ctx, { expectedIdentity: null })).value;
    if (!seeded) throw new Error('the store already had an account');
    expect(
      store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, seeded.id, { label: 'Work plan' })).value,
    ).toMatchObject({ label: 'Work plan', isDefault: true });
    apiKey('Client key');
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, seeded.id, { label: 'Client key' })),
    ).toThrow(ConflictError);
  });

  it('writes account.saved with the changed fields and never a value', () => {
    const key = apiKey();
    const after = listEventsAfter(store.db, 0, 100).at(-1)?.seq ?? 0;
    store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, key.id, { monthlyCapUsd: 250, payer: 'Client B' }));
    store.write((ctx) => recordAccountSecret(ctx, key.id, 'sha256:0123456789abcdef'));
    const events = listEventsAfter(store.db, after, 100);
    expect(events.map((e) => [e.type, e.entityType, e.data])).toEqual([
      [EventType.AccountSaved, 'account', { fields: ['monthlyCapUsd', 'payer'] }],
      [EventType.AccountSaved, 'account', { fields: ['secret'] }],
    ]);
    expect(JSON.stringify(events)).not.toContain('0123456789abcdef');
    expect(getAccount(store.db, key.id).secretSet).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each<{
    name: string;
    act: (id: AccountId) => void;
    error: typeof InvalidError | typeof ConflictError;
  }>([
    {
      name: 'a monthly cap on a subscription',
      act: (id) => store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, id, { monthlyCapUsd: 10 })),
      error: InvalidError,
    },
    {
      name: 'an expected identity that is not an email on a subscription',
      act: (id) => store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, id, { expectedIdentity: 'org-1' })),
      error: InvalidError,
    },
    {
      name: 'gateway settings on a subscription',
      act: (id) =>
        store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, id, { settings: { baseUrl: 'https://g.example' } })),
      error: InvalidError,
    },
    {
      name: 'a secret on a subscription',
      act: (id) => store.write((ctx) => recordAccountSecret(ctx, id, 'sha256:0000000000000000')),
      error: InvalidError,
    },
    {
      name: 'a label another account has',
      act: (id) => {
        apiKey('Taken');
        store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, id, { label: 'Taken' }));
      },
      error: ConflictError,
    },
  ])('refuses $name', ({ act, error }) => {
    const sub = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Company A' });
    expect(() => {
      act(sub.id);
    }).toThrow(error);
  });

  it('turns a Bedrock account to a bearer token and back, keeping the secret only while it needs one', () => {
    const bedrock = seedAccount(store, {
      kind: 'bedrock',
      label: 'Client C',
      settings: { region: 'us-west-2', awsProfile: 'client-c' },
    });
    expect(getAccountRow(store.db, bedrock.id).secretRef).toBeNull();
    store.write((ctx) =>
      patchAccount(ctx, bedrock.id, { settings: { region: 'us-west-2', awsProfile: null } }),
    );
    store.write((ctx) => recordAccountSecret(ctx, bedrock.id, 'sha256:1111111111111111'));
    expect(getAccount(store.db, bedrock.id).secretSet).toBe(true);
    store.write((ctx) =>
      patchAccount(ctx, bedrock.id, { settings: { region: 'us-west-2', awsProfile: 'p' } }),
    );
    expect(getAccountRow(store.db, bedrock.id)).toMatchObject({
      secretRef: null,
      secretFingerprint: null,
    });
  });

  it('archives an unused account, but not the default or one a project binds', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const defaultId = defaultAccountId(store.db);
    if (!defaultId) throw new Error('seedProject seeds the default account');
    expect(() => store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, defaultId))).toThrow(ConflictError);
    const key = apiKey();
    store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: key.id }));
    expect(() => store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, key.id))).toThrow(/demo/);
    const spare = apiKey('Spare');
    expect(store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, spare.id)).value.archived).toBe(true);
    expect(listAccounts(store.db).map((a) => a.label)).not.toContain('Spare');
    expect(listAccounts(store.db, { includeArchived: true }).map((a) => a.label)).toContain(
      'Spare',
    );
    expect(() => store.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, spare.id, { payer: 'x' }))).toThrow(
      ConflictError,
    );
  });

  it('throws NotFoundError for an unknown account', () => {
    expect(() => getAccount(store.db, mintId('Account'))).toThrow(NotFoundError);
  });
});

describe('projects on accounts', () => {
  it('binds a new project to the default account, or the one it names', () => {
    const plain = seedProject(store);
    expect(plain.accountId).toBe(defaultAccountId(store.db));
    const key = apiKey();
    expect(seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id }).accountId).toBe(key.id);
  });

  it('moves a project with work in flight, but never to an archived or unknown account', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const key = apiKey();
    const spare = apiKey('Spare');
    store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, spare.id));
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: spare.id })),
    ).toThrow(InvalidError);
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: mintId('Account') })),
    ).toThrow(NotFoundError);
    store.write((ctx) => {
      const task = insertTask(ctx, project.id, {
        title: 't',
        acceptanceCriteria: ['x'],
        areas: ['api'],
      });
      const ws = insertWorkstream(ctx, task.id);
      transitionWorkstream(ctx, ws.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
    });
    store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: key.id }));
    expect(getProject(store.db, project.id).accountId).toBe(key.id);
  });

  it('counts lanes per account', () => {
    const key = apiKey();
    const a = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id });
    const b = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id });
    for (const project of [a, b]) {
      store.write((ctx) => {
        const task = insertTask(ctx, project.id, {
          title: 't',
          acceptanceCriteria: ['x'],
          areas: ['api'],
        });
        const ws = insertWorkstream(ctx, task.id);
        transitionWorkstream(ctx, ws.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
      });
    }
    expect(countLanes(store.db).byAccount.get(key.id)).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe('usage', () => {
  it('records the account a session ran on, and no dollars on a subscription', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const session = spend(
      project,
      { inputTokens: 100, outputTokens: 10, cachedTokens: 50, costUsd: 0.4 },
      new Date(2026, 9, 2),
    );
    expect(session.accountId).toBe(project.accountId);
    const usage = accountUsage(store.db, project.accountId, OCTOBER);
    expect(usage.total).toEqual({ tokensIn: 100, tokensOut: 10, tokensCached: 50, costUsd: null });
  });

  it('sums a metered account in the month the sessions started, in total and per project', () => {
    const key = apiKey();
    const a = seedProject(store, { name: 'alpha', accountId: key.id });
    const b = seedProject(store, { name: 'beta', accountId: key.id });
    const u = (n: number) => ({
      inputTokens: n,
      outputTokens: n,
      cachedTokens: n,
      costUsd: n / 100,
    });
    spend(a, u(100), new Date(2026, 9, 1, 0, 0));
    spend(a, u(50), new Date(2026, 9, 31, 23, 59));
    spend(b, u(10), new Date(2026, 9, 10));
    spend(b, u(999), new Date(2026, 8, 30, 23, 59));
    spend(b, u(999), new Date(2026, 10, 1, 0, 0));
    const usage = accountUsage(store.db, key.id, OCTOBER);
    expect(usage.month).toBe('2026-10');
    expect(usage.monthlyCapUsd).toBe(500);
    expect(usage.total.tokensIn).toBe(160);
    expect(usage.total.costUsd).toBeCloseTo(1.6);
    expect(usage.byProject.map((p) => [p.projectName, p.tokensIn])).toEqual([
      ['alpha', 150],
      ['beta', 10],
    ]);
    expect(accountSpendUsd(store.db, key.id, OCTOBER)).toBeCloseTo(1.6);
  });

  it('counts a session under the account it ran on, even after the project moved', () => {
    const key = apiKey();
    const other = apiKey('Other');
    const project = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id });
    spend(
      project,
      { inputTokens: 1, outputTokens: 1, cachedTokens: 0, costUsd: 2 },
      new Date(2026, 9, 3),
    );
    store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: other.id }));
    expect(accountSpendUsd(store.db, key.id, OCTOBER)).toBe(2);
    expect(accountSpendUsd(store.db, other.id, OCTOBER)).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the table and columns**

In `packages/db/src/schema.ts`, import `AccountId`, `AccountSettings` and `SupportedAccountKind` (types) from `@10db/contracts`, and add before `projects`:

```ts
export const accounts = sqliteTable(
  'accounts',
  {
    id: text('id').$type<AccountId>().primaryKey(),
    kind: text('kind').$type<SupportedAccountKind>().notNull(),
    label: text('label').notNull(),
    payer: text('payer').notNull(),
    /** The email (subscription) or organization (keyed) a session must report; null when none is compared. */
    expectedIdentity: text('expected_identity'),
    /** The Claude Code config directory its sessions run on; null is Claude Code's default (the default account only). */
    configDir: text('config_dir'),
    /** The Keychain account (service `10db`) of the secret, for kinds that have one. */
    secretRef: text('secret_ref'),
    /** `sha256:` and 16 hex digits of the secret 10db last stored: the account's identity for keyed kinds. */
    secretFingerprint: text('secret_fingerprint'),
    settings: text('settings', { mode: 'json' }).$type<AccountSettings>().notNull(),
    laneCap: integer('lane_cap').notNull(),
    monthlyCapUsd: real('monthly_cap_usd'),
    archived: integer('archived', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
    updatedAt: updatedAt(),
  },
  (t) => [uniqueIndex('accounts_label_idx').on(t.label)],
);
```

```ts
    .$type<AccountId>()
    .notNull()
```

```ts
import {
  AccountId,
  AccountInput,
  AccountKind,
  AccountPatch,
  ConflictError,
  DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LABEL,
  DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LANE_CAP,
  EventType,
  InvalidError,
  isMetered,
  mintId,
  needsSecret,
  NotFoundError,
  parseAccountSettings,
  type AccountDto,
  type AccountSettings,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { and, asc, eq, ne } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { z } from 'zod';
import * as schema from '../schema';
import { getAppSetting, setAppSetting } from './settings';
import type { DbReader, WriteContext } from './store';

export type AccountRow = typeof schema.accounts.$inferSelect;

/** The app setting naming the account new projects bind to when they name none. */
const DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_KEY = 'accounts.default';
const Email = z.email();

/** The Keychain account (service `10db`) that holds an account's secret. */
export function accountSecretRef(id: AccountId): string {
  return `account:${id}`;
}

export function defaultAccountId(db: DbReader): AccountId | null {
  const parsed = AccountId.safeParse(getAppSetting(db, DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_KEY));
  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
}

function toDto(db: DbReader, row: AccountRow): AccountDto {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    kind: row.kind,
    label: row.label,
    payer: row.payer,
    expectedIdentity: row.expectedIdentity,
    configDir: row.configDir,
    // Stored JSON is parsed for its kind on the way out, so a drifted row fails loudly here.
    settings: parseAccountSettings(row.kind, row.settings),
    laneCap: row.laneCap,
    monthlyCapUsd: row.monthlyCapUsd,
    archived: row.archived,
    isDefault: defaultAccountId(db) === row.id,
    secretSet: row.secretFingerprint !== null,
    createdAt: row.createdAt,
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt,
  };
}

/** `fields` names what changed; a value never goes into the event log. */
function saved(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId, fields: readonly string[]): void {
  ctx.emit({
    type: EventType.AccountSaved,
    entityType: 'account',
    entityId: id,
    data: { fields: [...fields] },
  });
}

function refuseTakenLabel(db: DbReader, label: string, except: AccountId | null): void {
  const taken = db
    .select({ id: schema.accounts.id })
    .from(schema.accounts)
    .where(
      except
        ? and(eq(schema.accounts.label, label), ne(schema.accounts.id, except))
        : eq(schema.accounts.label, label),
    )
    .get();
  if (taken) throw new ConflictError(`An account is already called "${label}"`);
}

export function getAccountRow(db: DbReader, id: AccountId): AccountRow {
  const row = db.select().from(schema.accounts).where(eq(schema.accounts.id, id)).get();
  if (!row) throw new NotFoundError('account', id);
  return row;
}

export function getAccount(db: DbReader, id: AccountId): AccountDto {
  return toDto(db, getAccountRow(db, id));
}

export function listAccounts(
  db: DbReader,
  opts: { readonly includeArchived?: boolean } = {},
): AccountDto[] {
  const rows = db.select().from(schema.accounts).orderBy(asc(schema.accounts.label)).all();
  return rows.filter((r) => opts.includeArchived === true || !r.archived).map((r) => toDto(db, r));
}

export function insertAccount(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  input: AccountInput,
  opts: { configDirFor(id: AccountId): string },
): AccountDto {
  const config = AccountInput.parse(input);
  refuseTakenLabel(ctx.tx, config.label, null);
  const id = mintId('Account');
  const row: AccountRow = {
    id,
    kind: config.kind,
    label: config.label,
    payer: config.payer,
    expectedIdentity: 'expectedIdentity' in config ? config.expectedIdentity : null,
    // Keyed kinds too: a session on the default account's directory would read its saved login and config.
    configDir: opts.configDirFor(id),
    secretRef: needsSecret(config.kind, config.settings) ? accountSecretRef(id) : null,
    secretFingerprint: null,
    settings: config.settings,
    laneCap: config.laneCap,
    monthlyCapUsd: 'monthlyCapUsd' in config ? config.monthlyCapUsd : null,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: ctx.now,
    updatedAt: ctx.now,
  };
  ctx.tx.insert(schema.accounts).values(row).run();
  saved(ctx, id, ['created']);
  return toDto(ctx.tx, row);
}

/** Settings checked against the account's own kind; a mismatch is the caller's mistake (400). */
function settingsFor(row: AccountRow, raw: unknown): AccountSettings {
  try {
    return parseAccountSettings(row.kind, raw);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof z.ZodError)
      throw new InvalidError(
        `Those settings don't fit a ${row.kind} account: ${error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join('; ')}`,
      );
    throw error;
  }
}

export function patchAccount(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId, patch: AccountPatch): AccountDto {
  const changes = AccountPatch.parse(patch);
  const row = getAccountRow(ctx.tx, id);
  if (row.archived) throw new ConflictError(`Account "${row.label}" is archived`);
  if (changes.label !== undefined) refuseTakenLabel(ctx.tx, changes.label, id);
  if (typeof changes.monthlyCapUsd === 'number' && !isMetered(row.kind))
    throw new InvalidError('Only API-key, Bedrock and gateway accounts take a monthly dollar cap');
  if (typeof changes.expectedIdentity === 'string') {
    if (row.kind === AccountKind.Gateway)
      throw new InvalidError(
        'A gateway account is identified by its key, not an expected identity',
      );
    if (
      row.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription &&
      !Email.safeParse(changes.expectedIdentity).success
    )
      throw new InvalidError("A subscription's expected identity is the email it signs in as");
  }
  const settings = changes.settings === undefined ? undefined : settingsFor(row, changes.settings);
  // A Bedrock account moving between a profile and a bearer token gains or loses its secret.
  const secretRef =
    settings === undefined
      ? row.secretRef
      : needsSecret(row.kind, settings)
        ? accountSecretRef(id)
        : null;
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.accounts)
    .set({
      ...(changes.label !== undefined ? { label: changes.label } : {}),
      ...(changes.payer !== undefined ? { payer: changes.payer } : {}),
      ...(changes.laneCap !== undefined ? { laneCap: changes.laneCap } : {}),
      ...(changes.monthlyCapUsd !== undefined ? { monthlyCapUsd: changes.monthlyCapUsd } : {}),
      ...(changes.expectedIdentity !== undefined
        ? { expectedIdentity: changes.expectedIdentity }
        : {}),
      ...(settings !== undefined
        ? {
            settings,
            secretRef,
            secretFingerprint: secretRef === null ? null : row.secretFingerprint,
          }
        : {}),
      updatedAt: ctx.now,
    })
    .where(eq(schema.accounts.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, Object.keys(changes).sort());
  return getAccount(ctx.tx, id);
}

/** Archiving is refused while a project binds the account, and for the default account. */
export function archiveAccount(ctx: WriteContext, id: AccountId): AccountDto {
  const row = getAccountRow(ctx.tx, id);
  if (row.archived) return getAccount(ctx.tx, id);
  if (defaultAccountId(ctx.tx) === id)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `"${row.label}" is the default account for new projects, so it can't be archived`,
    );
  const bound = ctx.tx
    .select({ name: schema.projects.name })
    .from(schema.projects)
    .where(eq(schema.projects.accountId, id))
    .all();
  if (bound.length > 0)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `Move ${bound.map((p) => `"${p.name}"`).join(', ')} to another account before archiving "${row.label}"`,
    );
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.accounts)
    .set({ archived: true, updatedAt: ctx.now })
    .where(eq(schema.accounts.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, ['archived']);
  return getAccount(ctx.tx, id);
}

/** Records the fingerprint of the secret just stored in the Keychain; the secret itself is never passed here. */
export function recordAccountSecret(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: AccountId,
  fingerprint: string,
): AccountDto {
  const row = getAccountRow(ctx.tx, id);
  if (row.archived) throw new ConflictError(`Account "${row.label}" is archived`);
  if (row.secretRef === null) throw new InvalidError(`Account "${row.label}" takes no secret`);
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.accounts)
    .set({ secretFingerprint: fingerprint, updatedAt: ctx.now })
    .where(eq(schema.accounts.id, id))
    .run();
  saved(ctx, id, ['secret']);
  return getAccount(ctx.tx, id);
}

/**
 * On a fresh database: the builder's current login as "Claude plan", the default for new projects
 * (accounts spec §3 "accounts start fresh"). `configDir` is where that login lives: the login shell's
 * own Claude Code directory, or null for Claude Code's default.
 */
export function seedDefaultAccount(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  opts: { readonly expectedIdentity: string | null; readonly configDir?: string | null },
): AccountDto | null {
  if (ctx.tx.select({ id: schema.accounts.id }).from(schema.accounts).get()) return null;
  const row: AccountRow = {
    id: mintId('Account'),
    kind: AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription,
    label: DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LABEL,
    payer: '',
    expectedIdentity: opts.expectedIdentity,
    configDir: opts.configDir ?? null,
    secretRef: null,
    secretFingerprint: null,
    settings: {},
    laneCap: DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_LANE_CAP,
    monthlyCapUsd: null,
    archived: false,
    createdAt: ctx.now,
    updatedAt: ctx.now,
  };
  ctx.tx.insert(schema.accounts).values(row).run();
  setAppSetting(ctx, DEFAULT_ACCOUNT_KEY, row.id);
  saved(ctx, row.id, ['created']);
  return toDto(ctx.tx, row);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the usage queries**

`packages/db/src/store/usage.ts`:

```ts
import { isMetered, type AccountId, type AccountUsageDto, type UsageTotals } from '@10db/contracts';
import { and, asc, eq, gte, lt, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import * as schema from '../schema';
import { getAccountRow } from './accounts';
import type { DbReader } from './store';

/** A calendar month as ISO instants: `start` inclusive, `end` exclusive. The daemon computes it in local time. */
export interface UsageWindow {
  readonly month: string;
  readonly start: string;
  readonly end: string;
}

/** Sessions on the account that started in the window (accounts spec §6: usage is a query, not a ledger). */
function inWindow(id: AccountId, window: UsageWindow) {
  return and(
    eq(schema.sessions.accountId, id),
    gte(schema.sessions.startedAt, window.start),
    lt(schema.sessions.startedAt, window.end),
  );
}

export function accountSpendUsd(db: DbReader, id: AccountId, window: UsageWindow): number {
  const row = db
    .select({ total: sql<number | null>`sum(${schema.sessions.costUsd})` })
    .from(schema.sessions)
    .where(inWindow(id, window))
    .get();
  return row?.total ?? 0;
}

export function accountUsage(db: DbReader, id: AccountId, window: UsageWindow): AccountUsageDto {
  const account = getAccountRow(db, id);
  const metered = isMetered(account.kind);
  const rows = db
    .select({
      projectId: schema.projects.id,
      projectName: schema.projects.name,
      tokensIn: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${schema.sessions.inputTokens}), 0)`,
      tokensOut: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${schema.sessions.outputTokens}), 0)`,
      tokensCached: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${schema.sessions.cachedTokens}), 0)`,
      costUsd: sql<number | null>`sum(${schema.sessions.costUsd})`,
    })
    .from(schema.sessions)
    .innerJoin(schema.workstreams, eq(schema.workstreams.id, schema.sessions.workstreamId))
    .innerJoin(schema.projects, eq(schema.projects.id, schema.workstreams.projectId))
    .where(inWindow(id, window))
    .groupBy(schema.projects.id)
    .orderBy(asc(schema.projects.name))
    .all();
  const byProject = rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, costUsd: metered ? (r.costUsd ?? 0) : null }));
  const zero: UsageTotals = {
    tokensIn: 0,
    tokensOut: 0,
    tokensCached: 0,
    costUsd: metered ? 0 : null,
  };
  const total = byProject.reduce<UsageTotals>(
    (sum, r) => ({
      tokensIn: sum.tokensIn + r.tokensIn,
      tokensOut: sum.tokensOut + r.tokensOut,
      tokensCached: sum.tokensCached + r.tokensCached,
      costUsd: sum.costUsd === null || r.costUsd === null ? null : sum.costUsd + r.costUsd,
    }),
    zero,
  );
  return {
    accountId: id,
    month: window.month,
    total,
    monthlyCapUsd: account.monthlyCapUsd,
    byProject,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Bind projects and sessions to accounts, and count lanes per account**

In `packages/db/src/store/projects.ts` (import `type AccountId` from `@10db/contracts`, and `defaultAccountId`, `getAccountRow` from `./accounts`):

```ts
/** The account a new project binds to: the one it names, else the default; never an archived one. */
function accountFor(ctx: WriteContext, named: AccountId | undefined): AccountId {
  const id = named ?? defaultAccountId(ctx.tx);
  if (!id) throw new InvalidError('There is no account to bind the project to; create one first');
  const account = getAccountRow(ctx.tx, id);
  if (account.archived) throw new InvalidError(`Account "${account.label}" is archived`);
  return id;
}
```

In `insertProject`, compute `const { accountId: named, ...rest } = config;` and build the row from `...rest` with `accountId: accountFor(ctx, named)`.

In `patchProject`, before the update:

```ts
if (changes.accountId !== undefined && changes.accountId !== current.accountId) {
  // Work in flight moves too: each lane's next session runs on the new account (accounts spec §6).
  const account = getAccountRow(ctx.tx, changes.accountId);
  if (account.archived) throw new InvalidError(`Account "${account.label}" is archived`);
}
```

In `packages/db/src/store/sessions.ts` (import `isMetered` from `@10db/contracts`, `sql` from `drizzle-orm`):

- `insertSession` looks up the owner before building the row:

```ts
const owner = ctx.tx
  .select({ accountId: schema.projects.accountId, kind: schema.accounts.kind })
  .from(schema.workstreams)
  .innerJoin(schema.projects, eq(schema.projects.id, schema.workstreams.projectId))
  .innerJoin(schema.accounts, eq(schema.accounts.id, schema.projects.accountId))
  .where(eq(schema.workstreams.id, input.workstreamId))
  .get();
if (!owner) throw new NotFoundError('workstream', input.workstreamId);
```

and the row gains `accountId: owner.accountId`, `cachedTokens: 0`, and `costUsd: isMetered(owner.kind) ? 0 : null` (the comment: "A subscription's dollars are not real money, so its cost stays null (accounts spec §4).").

```ts
export function seedProject(store: Store, overrides: Partial<ProjectInput> = {}): ProjectDto {

  }).value;
}
```

### Task 3: The Keychain, the launcher and the Claude Code version warning

`environment/` gains the two pieces every later task launches through: a `Keychain` that reads and writes items without putting a secret on a command line, and the launcher, the one module that names account variables (accounts spec §3). Nothing calls the launcher yet; Task 5 switches the runner to it.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/environment/keychain.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/keychain.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/launch.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/launch.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/environment/session-env.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/session-env.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/system-check.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/system-check.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/environment/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/main.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts` (`noSystemCheck`)
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/system.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/system.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/desktop/src/shell/system-alert.tsx`, `apps/desktop/src/test/daemon.tsx`, and the shell test that renders `SystemAlert` (`apps/desktop/src/shell/*.test.tsx`; create `system-alert.test.tsx` if none renders it)
- Generated: `packages/contracts/generated/system-check-dto.schema.json`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `AccountRow`; Task 1's `AccountProblem`, `AccountKind`, `BedrockSettings`, `GatewaySettings`, `parseAccountSettings`, `SupportedAccountKind`.
- Produces (exported from `environment/index.ts`):
  - `KEYCHAIN_SERVICE = '10db'`, `AA_KEY_ACCOUNT = 'artificial-analysis-api-key'`.
  - `interface Keychain { read(account: string): string | null; write(account: string, secret: string): void }`; `class KeychainError`; `keychainWith(exec: KeychainExec): Keychain`; `systemKeychain: Keychain`; `unavailableKeychain: Keychain` (reads nothing, refuses writes); `secretFingerprint(secret: string): string`.
  - `stripBillingSwitches(base: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): Record<string, string>`; `BILLING_SWITCH_VARS` gains `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` and `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`.
  - `interface AccountExpectation { accountId: AccountId; label: string; kind: SupportedAccountKind; identity: string | null }`.
  - `interface SessionLaunch { env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; model: string; expect: AccountExpectation }`; `interface ProbeLaunch { env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; expect: AccountExpectation }`.
  - `class AccountLaunchError extends Error { accountId; problem: AccountProblem }`.
  - `interface Launcher { projectEnv(): Record<string, string>; session(account: AccountRow, alias: string): SessionLaunch; probe(account: AccountRow): ProbeLaunch; loginConfigDir(): string | null; loginConfigDirWarning(): string | null }`; `createLauncher(opts: { base: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; clientApp: string; keychain: Keychain }): Launcher`. Every account with a `configDir` gets `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` set to it, whatever its kind; `loginConfigDir()` is the login shell's own absolute `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, or null (Task 5 seeds the default account on it; Task 7 guards it). `loginConfigDirWarning(): string | null` says, naming the variable and never its value, that a non-empty relative one was ignored.
  - `SECRET_VARIABLES: readonly string[]`, `signInCommand(configDir: string | null): string`, `ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON = '2.1.302'`.
  - `claudeCodeWarning(version: string | null): string | null`; `SystemCheckDto.claudeCode: { verifiedOn: string; warning: string | null }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`apps/daemon/src/environment/keychain.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ExecFileSyncOptionsWithStringEncoding } from 'node:child_process';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { KeychainError, keychainWith, secretFingerprint, unavailableKeychain } from './index';

type Call = { args: readonly string[]; input: unknown };

/** A Keychain double at the `security` boundary: `-i` writes go to `items`, reads come from it. */
function securityDouble(opts: { mangle?: (s: string) => string } = {}) {
  const items = new Map<string, string>();
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const exec = (
    _cmd: string,
    args: readonly string[],
    options: ExecFileSyncOptionsWithStringEncoding,
  ): string => {
    calls.push({ args, input: options.input });
    if (args[0] === '-i') {
      const line = String(options.input);
      const match = /-a "([^"]+)" -w "([^"]*)"/.exec(line);
      if (match?.[1] !== undefined && match[2] !== undefined)
        items.set(match[1], opts.mangle ? opts.mangle(match[2]) : match[2]);
      return '';
    }
    const account = args[args.indexOf('-a') + 1] ?? '';
    const value = items.get(account);
    if (value === undefined) throw Object.assign(new Error('not found'), { status: 44 });
    return `${value}\n`;
  };
  return { exec, items, calls };
}

describe('keychainWith', () => {
  it('writes through security -i on standard input, never on the command line, and reads it back', () => {
    const security = securityDouble();
    const keychain = keychainWith(security.exec);
    keychain.write('account:acc_1', 'sk-ant-SECRET-1234');
    expect(keychain.read('account:acc_1')).toBe('sk-ant-SECRET-1234');
    const write = security.calls[0];
    expect(write?.args).toEqual(['-i']);
    expect(String(write?.input)).toContain(
      'add-generic-password -U -s 10db -a "account:acc_1" -w "sk-ant-SECRET-1234"',
    );
    for (const call of security.calls) expect(call.args.join(' ')).not.toContain('SECRET');
  });

  it('refuses a secret security -i would split or unquote, before running anything', () => {
    const security = securityDouble();
    const keychain = keychainWith(security.exec);
    for (const bad of ['has space', 'quote"d', 'back\\slash', 'new\nline'])
      expect(() => {
        keychain.write('account:acc_1', bad);
      }).toThrow(KeychainError);
    expect(security.calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('fails a write the Keychain did not store as given, naming no value', () => {
    const keychain = keychainWith(securityDouble({ mangle: (s) => s.slice(1) }).exec);
    const write = () => {
      keychain.write('account:acc_1', 'sk-ant-SECRET-1234');
    };
    expect(write).toThrow(KeychainError);
    expect(write).not.toThrow(/SECRET/);
  });

  it('reads nothing for a missing or empty item', () => {
    const security = securityDouble();
    security.items.set('account:empty', '');
    const keychain = keychainWith(security.exec);
    expect(keychain.read('account:none')).toBeNull();
    expect(keychain.read('account:empty')).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    ['user denied (status 51)', { status: 51 }, /status 51/],
    ['a locked Keychain (status 36)', { status: 36 }, /status 36/],
    ['a timeout', { code: 'ETIMEDOUT', status: null, signal: 'SIGTERM' }, /timed out/],
    ['security missing', { code: 'ENOENT' }, /ENOENT/],
  ])('throws a KeychainError for %s, naming no output', (_name, props, message) => {
    const keychain = keychainWith(() => {
      throw Object.assign(new Error('Command failed: security ... sk-ant-LEAK'), props);
    });
    const read = () => keychain.read('account:acc_1');
    expect(read).toThrow(KeychainError);
    expect(read).toThrow(message);
    expect(read).not.toThrow(/LEAK/);
  });

  it('has an unavailable Keychain that reads nothing and refuses writes', () => {
    expect(unavailableKeychain.read('anything')).toBeNull();
    expect(() => {
      unavailableKeychain.write('account:acc_1', 'sk-ant-SECRET-1234');
    }).toThrow(KeychainError);
  });

  it('fingerprints a secret without exposing it', () => {
    const print = secretFingerprint('sk-ant-SECRET-1234');
    expect(print).toMatch(/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
    expect(secretFingerprint('sk-ant-SECRET-1234')).toBe(print);
    expect(secretFingerprint('sk-ant-SECRET-1235')).not.toBe(print);
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/environment/keychain.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync, type ExecFileSyncOptionsWithStringEncoding } from 'node:child_process';

export const KEYCHAIN_SERVICE = '10db';
/** The Keychain account of the builder's Artificial Analysis key. */
export const AA_KEY_ACCOUNT = 'artificial-analysis-api-key';
const KEYCHAIN_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;
/** `security` exits with this status when the Keychain has no such item. */
const ITEM_NOT_FOUND = 44;
/** The secret reaches `security -i` inside double quotes, so it can hold no whitespace, quote or backslash. */
const SAFE_SECRET = /^[\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e]+$/;
const SAFE_ACCOUNT = /^[A-Za-z0-9:_.-]+$/;
const FINGERPRINT_HEX = 16;

export type KeychainExec = (
  command: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  options: ExecFileSyncOptionsWithStringEncoding,
) => string;

/** The Keychain could not be read or written. The message names the item and the status, never a value. */
export class KeychainError extends Error {
  override readonly name = 'KeychainError';
}

/** Items in the login Keychain under service `10db`. Secrets are read when needed and never cached. */
export interface Keychain {
  /** The secret, or null when the item is missing or empty. */
  read(account: string): string | null;
  /** Adds or replaces the item, then reads it back. */
  write(account: string, secret: string): void;
}

function failure(error: unknown): string {
  // Execution errors carry the command line and its output, which can hold a value; only the codes are used.
  const e = error as { status?: number | null; code?: string; signal?: string | null };
  if (e.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') return `timed out after ${String(KEYCHAIN_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)} s`;
  if (typeof e.status === 'number') return `security exit status ${String(e.status)}`;
  return e.code ?? e.signal ?? 'unknown error';
}

/**
 * A Keychain over `exec`. Only "item not found" means there is no secret; a timeout, a locked or
 * unreachable Keychain or a denied prompt throws, because telling the builder to add a secret that
 * exists would send them the wrong way.
 */
export function keychainWith(exec: KeychainExec): Keychain {
  const read = (account: string): string | null => {
    try {
      const value = exec(
        '/usr/bin/security',
        ['find-generic-password', '-s', KEYCHAIN_SERVICE, '-a', account, '-w'],
        { encoding: 'utf8', stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'], timeout: KEYCHAIN_TIMEOUT_MS },
      ).trim();
      return value.length > 0 ? value : null;
    } catch (error) {
      // A thrown exec error has a numeric `status` when security ran and exited non-zero.
      if ((error as { status?: unknown }).status === ITEM_NOT_FOUND) return null;
      throw new KeychainError(`Reading the Keychain item ${account} failed (${failure(error)})`);
    }
  };
  return {
    read,
    write(account, secret) {
      if (!SAFE_ACCOUNT.test(account))
        throw new KeychainError(`${account} is not a Keychain account name 10db writes`);
      if (!SAFE_SECRET.test(secret))
        throw new KeychainError(
          'The secret must be printable ASCII with no spaces, double quotes or backslashes',
        );
      try {
        // Interactive mode reads the command from standard input, so the secret is never on a command line.
        exec('/usr/bin/security', ['-i'], {
          encoding: 'utf8',
          input: `add-generic-password -U -s ${KEYCHAIN_SERVICE} -a "${account}" -w "${secret}"\n`,
          stdio: ['pipe', 'ignore', 'ignore'],
          timeout: KEYCHAIN_TIMEOUT_MS,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        throw new KeychainError(`Writing the Keychain item ${account} failed (${failure(error)})`);
      }
      // `security -i` can report a failed command without a failing exit status.
      if (read(account) !== secret)
        throw new KeychainError(`The Keychain did not store the secret for ${account} as given`);
    },
  };
}

export const systemKeychain: Keychain = keychainWith(execFileSync);

/** For a daemon started without a Keychain (tests): nothing to read, and writing is refused. */
export const unavailableKeychain: Keychain = {
  read: () => null,
  write: (account) => {
    throw new KeychainError(
      `No Keychain is available to this daemon, so ${account} was not written`,
    );
  },
};

/** Enough to tell two secrets apart, never enough to recover one. */
export function secretFingerprint(secret: string): string {
  return `sha256:${createHash('sha256').update(secret).digest('hex').slice(0, FINGERPRINT_HEX)}`;
}
```

```ts
/** The login environment without any billing switch, nested-session marker or 10db variable. */
export function stripBillingSwitches(
  base: Readonly<Record<string, string>>,
): Record<string, string> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(base).filter(
      ([name]) => !name.startsWith(OWN_PREFIX) && !NESTED.includes(name) && !isBillingSwitch(name),
    ),
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the launcher**

`apps/daemon/src/environment/launch.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountKind,
  AccountProblem,
  ApiKeySettings,
  BedrockSettings,
  GatewaySettings,
  SubscriptionSettings,
  type AccountId,
  type SupportedAccountKind,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
import type { AccountRow } from '@10db/db';
import { secretFingerprint, type Keychain } from './keychain';
import { stripBillingSwitches } from './session-env';

/** The Claude Code version the account variables and `accountInfo()` fields were verified on (spike 12). */
export const ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON = '2.1.302';

/** Variables whose value is a credential: the guard refuses commands that would print them. */
export const SECRET_VARIABLES: readonly string[] = [
  'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY',
  'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN',
  'AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK',
  'AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY',
  'AWS_SESSION_TOKEN',
  'CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN',
];

/** A Bedrock session takes its AWS credentials from its account only, never from the login shell. */
const AWS_LOGIN_VARS = [
  'AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID',
  'AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY',
  'AWS_SESSION_TOKEN',
  'AWS_PROFILE',
  'AWS_DEFAULT_PROFILE',
  'AWS_REGION',
  'AWS_DEFAULT_REGION',
] as const;
const CLIENT_APP_VAR = 'CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK_CLIENT_APP';

/** What a session on an account must report before its prompt (accounts spec §5). */
export interface AccountExpectation {
  readonly accountId: AccountId;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly kind: SupportedAccountKind;
  /** The email (subscription) or organization (keyed) Claude Code must report; null compares none. */
  readonly identity: string | null;
}

export interface SessionLaunch {
  readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  /** The alias, or the channel's model ID from the account's model map. */
  readonly model: string;
  readonly expect: AccountExpectation;
}

export interface ProbeLaunch {
  readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  readonly expect: AccountExpectation;
}

/** The account can't start a session as it stands; the message names the account, never a value. */
export class AccountLaunchError extends Error {
  override readonly name = 'AccountLaunchError';
  readonly accountId: AccountId;
  readonly problem: AccountProblem;
  constructor(accountId: AccountId, problem: AccountProblem, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.accountId = accountId;
    this.problem = problem;
  }
}

/** The one owner of account variables (accounts spec §3). */
export interface Launcher {
  /** The login env with every billing switch stripped: for the gate and the repo's MCP servers. */
  projectEnv(): Record<string, string>;
  /** A session on `account` asking for `alias`; reads a keyed account's secret now and caches nothing. */
  session(account: AccountRow, alias: string): SessionLaunch;
  /** The same environment, for reading who the account is without a prompt. */
  probe(account: AccountRow): ProbeLaunch;
  /** Where the builder's own login lives when the login shell moves it: an absolute directory, or null for Claude Code's default. */
  loginConfigDir(): string | null;
  /** Why the login shell's directory was ignored (it is relative), naming the variable and never its value; null otherwise. */
  loginConfigDirWarning(): string | null;
}

function quoted(text: string): string {
  return `'${text.replaceAll("'", `'\\''`)}'`;
}

/** Signs a subscription in with the unmodified `claude`; the path is one shell word even with spaces. */
export function signInCommand(configDir: string | null): string {
  return configDir === null
    ? 'claude /login'
    : `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=${quoted(configDir)} claude /login`;
}

export function createLauncher(opts: {
  readonly base: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  readonly clientApp: string;
  readonly keychain: Keychain;
}): Launcher {
  const projectEnv = (): Record<string, string> => stripBillingSwitches(opts.base);

  function secretOf(account: AccountRow): string {
    const missing = new AccountLaunchError(
      account.id,
      AccountProblem.SecretMissing,
      `Account "${account.label}" has no secret set through 10db`,
    );
    if (account.secretRef === null || account.secretFingerprint === null) throw missing;
    const secret = opts.keychain.read(account.secretRef);
    if (secret === null) throw missing;
    if (secretFingerprint(secret) !== account.secretFingerprint)
      throw new AccountLaunchError(
        account.id,
        AccountProblem.SecretChanged,
        `The Keychain secret of account "${account.label}" changed outside 10db`,
      );
    return secret;
  }

  function build(account: AccountRow): {
    env: Record<string, string>;
    modelMap: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  } {
    const env: Record<string, string> = { ...projectEnv(), [CLIENT_APP_VAR]: opts.clientApp };
    // Every kind runs on its own directory, so a keyed session never meets the default account's saved login;
    // null is Claude Code's default directory, which only the default account uses.
    if (account.configDir !== null) env['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR'] = account.configDir;
    switch (account.kind) {
      case AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription: {
        const settings = SubscriptionSettings.parse(account.settings);
        return { env, modelMap: settings.modelMap ?? {} };
      }
      case AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey: {
        const settings = ApiKeySettings.parse(account.settings);
        env['ANTHROPIC_API_KEY'] = secretOf(account);
        return { env, modelMap: settings.modelMap ?? {} };
      }
      case AccountKind.Bedrock: {
        const settings = BedrockSettings.parse(account.settings);
        for (const name of AWS_LOGIN_VARS) delete env[name];
        env['CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK'] = '1';
        env['AWS_REGION'] = settings.region;
        if (settings.awsProfile === null) env['AWS_BEARER_TOKEN_BEDROCK'] = secretOf(account);
        else env['AWS_PROFILE'] = settings.awsProfile;
        return { env, modelMap: settings.modelMap ?? {} };
      }
      case AccountKind.Gateway: {
        const settings = GatewaySettings.parse(account.settings);
        env['ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL'] = settings.baseUrl;
        env['ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN'] = secretOf(account);
        return { env, modelMap: settings.modelMap ?? {} };
      }
    }
  }

  const expectation = (account: AccountRow): AccountExpectation => ({
    accountId: account.id,
    label: account.label,
    kind: account.kind,
    identity: account.expectedIdentity,
  });

  return {
    projectEnv,
    session(account, alias) {
      const { env, modelMap } = build(account);
      return { env, model: modelMap[alias] ?? alias, expect: expectation(account) };
    },
    probe(account) {
      return { env: build(account).env, expect: expectation(account) };
    },
    loginConfigDir() {
      const dir = opts.base['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR'];
      // A relative path depends on the shell's working directory, so it names no one directory.
      return dir !== undefined && isAbsolute(dir) ? dir : null;
    },
    loginConfigDirWarning() {
      const dir = opts.base['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR'];
      if (dir === undefined || dir === '' || isAbsolute(dir)) return null;
      return "The login shell sets CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR to a relative path, which names no one directory, so the default account runs on Claude Code's default directory (~/.claude). Set it to an absolute path and start 10db on a fresh data directory to run it there.";
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Warn about a newer Claude Code**

In `packages/contracts/src/system.ts`, add to `SystemCheckDto`'s object:

```ts
  /** The Claude Code version the account checks were verified on, and a warning on a newer major version (accounts spec §10). */
  claudeCode: z.object({ verifiedOn: z.string(), warning: z.string().nullable() }),
```

In `apps/daemon/src/environment/system-check.ts` (import `ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON` from `./launch`):

```ts
const major = (version: string | null): number | null => {
  const match = /^(\d+)\.\d+\.\d+/.exec(version ?? '');
  return match?.[1] === undefined ? null : Number(match[1]);
};

/** A newer major Claude Code may rename the variables or fields the account checks rely on. */
export function claudeCodeWarning(version: string | null): string | null {
  const installed = major(version);
  const verified = major(ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON);
  if (installed === null || verified === null || installed <= verified) return null;
  return `Claude Code ${version ?? ''} is a newer major version than ${ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON}, the version 10db's account checks were verified on. Sessions that report unexpected credentials are refused until the live check is rerun on it.`;
}
```

and `systemCheck` builds `claudeCode: { verifiedOn: ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON, warning: claudeCodeWarning(tools.find((t) => t.name === ToolName.Claude)?.version ?? null) }`. In `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`, `noSystemCheck` returns `claudeCode: { verifiedOn: ACCOUNT_NAMES_VERIFIED_ON, warning: null }` (import it from `../environment`).

In `apps/desktop/src/test/daemon.tsx`, the `/system` handler's report gains `claudeCode: { verifiedOn: '2.1.302', warning: null }`. In `apps/desktop/src/shell/system-alert.tsx`, read `const versionWarning = check.claudeCode.warning;`, include it in the early-return test, build the alert:

```tsx
const versionAlert = versionWarning ? (
  <Alert color="yellow" icon={icon} title="This Claude Code is newer than 10db's account checks">
    {versionWarning}
  </Alert>
) : null;
```

and render `{versionAlert}` after the login-shell alert inside the `Stack`.

and add one render test: a `/system` handler whose `claudeCode.warning` is set shows the alert titled "This Claude Code is newer than 10db's account checks".

---

### Task 4: Scheduling per account: lane caps, pauses and the monthly cap

The new `accounts/` module takes over from provider pauses. Lanes are capped per account. A usage limit pauses only the account that hit it. A metered account stops starting sessions at its monthly cap: in the scheduler before a launch, and in the runner before each further session in a lane (accounts spec §6).

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/month.ts`, `cap.ts`, `cards.ts`, `pauses.ts`, `runtime.ts`, `card-answers.ts`, `index.ts`
- Create: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/month.test.ts`, `pauses.test.ts`, `runtime.test.ts`
- Delete: `apps/daemon/src/scheduler/provider-pauses.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/scheduler/provider-pauses.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/scheduler/scheduler.ts`, `scheduler.test.ts`, `scheduler/index.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/engine.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/fixtures.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/inbox/service.ts` (`reword`), `packages/db/src/store/inbox.ts` (`body` in the patch), `packages/db/src/store/workstreams.ts` (`countLanes` drops `byProvider`)
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts` (wiring; `caps` loses `perProvider`), and any daemon test that passes `caps: { global, perProvider }` (`grep -rn perProvider apps/daemon`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `getAccountRow`, `AccountRow`, `accountSpendUsd`, `UsageWindow`, `LaneCounts.byAccount`, `listAccounts`, `seedAccount`; Task 3's `signInCommand`; Task 1's `AccountPauseReason`, `AccountProblem`, `AccountRefusal`, `ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL`, `AccountCardDetail`, `AccountCardChoice`, `BedrockSettings`, `isMetered`, `Month`.
- Produces (from `accounts/index.ts`):
  - `interface MonthWindow { month: string; start: Date; end: Date }`, `monthOf(at: Date): MonthWindow`, `monthWindow(month: string): MonthWindow`, `usageWindow(window: MonthWindow): UsageWindow`.
  - `interface CapStatus { spentUsd: number; capUsd: number; window: MonthWindow }`, `capStatus(db: DbReader, account: AccountRow, now: Date): CapStatus | null`.
  - `interface AccountCard { title; body; foldKey; detail: Record<string, Json>; options?: readonly InboxOption[] }`, `accountFoldKey(id: AccountId, ...parts: string[]): string`, `usageLimitCard`, `capCard`, `refusedCard(account: AccountRow, refusal: AccountRefusal): AccountCard`, `fixFor(account: AccountRow, problem: AccountProblem): string`.
  - `interface AccountPause { reason: AccountPauseReason; until: Date | null }`, `interface AccountPauses { pause; pausedFor; resume; restore; stop }`, `createAccountPauses(opts)`.
  - `interface AccountRuntime { blocked(account, projectId): AccountPause | null; rateLimited(accountId, resetsAt, projectId): void; refused(account, projectId, refusal): void; sessionEnded(account, projectId): void; isPaused(accountId): boolean; saved(accountId): void }`, `createAccountRuntime(opts)`.
  - `createAccountCardAnswers(deps: { resume(accountId: AccountId): void }): (item: InboxItemDto, answer: InboxAnswer) => void`. Task 6 adds Confirm.
  - `InboxService.reword(id: InboxItemId, body: string): void` (only an open card).
  - `SchedulerOptions`: `pauses` is replaced by `accounts: Pick<AccountRuntime, 'blocked' | 'isPaused'>`; `caps` is `{ global: number }`.
  - `RunnerOptions`: `onRateLimited` is replaced by `accounts: Pick<AccountRuntime, 'blocked' | 'rateLimited' | 'refused' | 'sessionEnded'>`; `Runner.stopProvider` becomes `stopAccount(accountId: AccountId, reason: StopReason)`; the runner's `Outcome` gains `{ kind: 'held'; until: Date | null; providerSessionId: string | null }`.
  - `apps/daemon/test/fixtures.ts`: `seedSpend(store: Store, project: ProjectDto, costUsd: number): SessionRow` (one session started now on the store's clock).
  - `TestEngine.accounts: AccountRuntime`; `createTestEngine`'s fourth argument `{ account?: AccountInput }` binds the engine's project to a new account; `TestEngine.rateLimited` holds `{ accountId, resetsAt }`.

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/month.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { monthOf, monthWindow, usageWindow } from './index';

describe('months, in local time', () => {
  it.each<[string, Date, string, Date, Date]>([
    [
      'mid-month',
      new Date(2026, 9, 13, 12),
      '2026-10',
      new Date(2026, 9, 1),
      new Date(2026, 10, 1),
    ],
    [
      'the last minute',
      new Date(2026, 9, 31, 23, 59),
      '2026-10',
      new Date(2026, 9, 1),
      new Date(2026, 10, 1),
    ],
    [
      'the first instant',
      new Date(2026, 10, 1, 0, 0),
      '2026-11',
      new Date(2026, 10, 1),
      new Date(2026, 11, 1),
    ],
    [
      'December',
      new Date(2026, 11, 31, 23, 0),
      '2026-12',
      new Date(2026, 11, 1),
      new Date(2027, 0, 1),
    ],
  ])('%s', (_name, at, month, start, end) => {
    expect(monthOf(at)).toEqual({ month, start, end });
  });

  it('opens a named month and refuses a malformed one', () => {
    expect(monthWindow('2026-02')).toEqual({
      month: '2026-02',
      start: new Date(2026, 1, 1),
      end: new Date(2026, 2, 1),
    });
    expect(() => monthWindow('2026-13')).toThrow();
    expect(usageWindow(monthWindow('2026-02'))).toEqual({
      month: '2026-02',
      start: new Date(2026, 1, 1).toISOString(),
      end: new Date(2026, 2, 1).toISOString(),
    });
  });
});
```
