`apps/daemon/src/accounts/pauses.test.ts` (it replaces `provider-pauses.test.ts`):

```ts
import { AccountPauseReason, type AccountId } from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  createTestStore,
  getProject,
  getWorkstream,
  listInbox,
  seedAccount,
  seedProject,
  transitionWorkstream,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedWorkstream } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { createEventBus, createStateStore, type StateStore } from '../events';
import { createInboxService } from '../inbox';
import { createAccountPauses, type AccountCard } from './index';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let state: StateStore;
let clock: ManualClock;
let stopped: AccountId[];
let resumed: number;

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new ManualClock();
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => clock.now()));
  state = createStateStore(store, createEventBus());
  stopped = [];
  resumed = 0;
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

function pauses() {
  return createAccountPauses({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox: createInboxService({ state, onParkedAnswer: () => undefined }),
    stopAccount: (id) => {
      stopped.push(id);
      return Promise.resolve();
    },
    onResume: () => {
      resumed += 1;
    },
  });
}
const card = (id: AccountId, kind: string): AccountCard => ({
  title: 'paused',
  body: 'body',
  foldKey: `account:${id}:${kind}`,
  detail: {},
});
const inMinutes = (n: number) => new Date(clock.now().getTime() + n * 60_000);
/** A workstream of `project` left in PAUSED_LIMIT. */
function pausedWork(project = seedProject(store)) {
  const { workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { project });
  state.write((ctx) => {
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'PAUSED_LIMIT', fence: null });
  });
  return workstream;
}

describe('account pauses', () => {
  it('pauses for a usage limit once, raises one card, stops the account, and re-queues its work at the reset', () => {
    const work = pausedWork();
    const accountId = getProject(state.db, work.projectId).accountId;
    const p = pauses();
    const reset = inMinutes(10);
    const at = { projectId: work.projectId, card: card(accountId, 'usage_limit') };
    p.pause(accountId, { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: reset }, at);
    p.pause(accountId, { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: reset }, at);
    expect(p.pausedFor(accountId)).toEqual({ reason: 'usage_limit', until: reset });
    expect(stopped).toEqual([accountId, accountId]);
    expect(listInbox(state.db, { states: ['OPEN'] })).toHaveLength(1);
    clock.advance(10 * 60_000);
    expect(p.pausedFor(accountId)).toBeNull();
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, work.id).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    expect(resumed).toBe(1);
  });

  it("re-queues only the paused account's work", () => {
    const key = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Client key' });
    const mine = pausedWork(seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id }));
    const other = pausedWork();
    const p = pauses();
    p.pause(
      key.id,
      { reason: AccountPauseReason.Refused, until: null },
      { projectId: mine.projectId, card: card(key.id, 'refused') },
    );
    p.resume(key.id);
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, mine.id).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, other.id).state).toBe('PAUSED_LIMIT');
  });

  it('keeps a pause with no end over one that ends, and the later of two ends', () => {
    const key = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Client key' });
    const projectId = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id }).id;
    const p = pauses();
    const at = { projectId, card: card(key.id, 'x') };
    p.pause(key.id, { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: inMinutes(20) }, at);
    p.pause(key.id, { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: inMinutes(5) }, at);
    expect(p.pausedFor(key.id)?.until).toEqual(inMinutes(20));
    p.pause(key.id, { reason: AccountPauseReason.Refused, until: null }, at);
    p.pause(key.id, { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: inMinutes(60) }, at);
    expect(p.pausedFor(key.id)).toEqual({ reason: 'refused', until: null });
    clock.advance(120 * 60_000);
    expect(p.pausedFor(key.id)?.reason).toBe('refused');
    expect(stopped).toEqual([key.id, key.id, key.id]);
  });

  it('restores a persisted pause after a restart, and resumes one whose end passed while the daemon was down', () => {
    const key = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Client key' });
    const projectId = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id }).id;
    pauses().pause(
      key.id,
      { reason: AccountPauseReason.Refused, until: null },
      { projectId, card: card(key.id, 'r') },
    );
    const restarted = pauses();
    restarted.restore();
    expect(restarted.pausedFor(key.id)).toEqual({ reason: 'refused', until: null });

    const short = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Short' });
    const work = pausedWork(seedProject(store, { accountId: short.id }));
    const before = pauses();
    before.pause(
      short.id,
      { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until: inMinutes(1) },
      { projectId: work.projectId, card: card(short.id, 'u') },
    );
    // The daemon stops before the pause ends, so its own timer never fires.
    before.stop();
    clock.advance(2 * 60_000);
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, work.id).state).toBe('PAUSED_LIMIT');
    const later = pauses();
    later.restore();
    expect(later.pausedFor(short.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, work.id).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    expect(resumed).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/runtime.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL,
  AccountCardChoice,
  EventType,
  type AccountDto,
  type ProjectDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  createTestStore,
  getAccountRow,
  getWorkstream,
  listEventsAfter,
  listInbox,
  patchAccount,
  seedAccount,
  seedProject,
  transitionWorkstream,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedSpend, seedWorkstream } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { createEventBus, createStateStore, type StateStore } from '../events';
import { createInboxService, type InboxService } from '../inbox';
import {
  createAccountCardAnswers,
  createAccountPauses,
  createAccountRuntime,
  type AccountPauses,
  type AccountRuntime,
} from './index';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let state: StateStore;
let clock: ManualClock;
let inbox: InboxService;
let pauses: AccountPauses;
let runtime: AccountRuntime;
let key: AccountDto;
let project: ProjectDto;

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new ManualClock(new Date(2026, 9, 20, 12));
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => clock.now()));
  state = createStateStore(store, createEventBus());
  inbox = createInboxService({ state, onParkedAnswer: () => undefined });
  pauses = createAccountPauses({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    stopAccount: () => Promise.resolve(),
    onResume: () => undefined,
  });
  runtime = createAccountRuntime({ state, clock, inbox, pauses, defaultPauseMs: 30 * 60_000 });
  key = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Client key', monthlyCapUsd: 5 });
  project = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id });
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

const row = () => getAccountRow(state.db, key.id);
const openCards = () => listInbox(state.db, { states: ['OPEN'] });

describe('the monthly cap', () => {
  it('lets a metered account under its cap, and never caps a subscription', () => {
    seedSpend(store, project, 4.99);
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)).toBeNull();
    const onDefault = seedProject(store);
    seedSpend(store, onDefault, 1_000);
    expect(runtime.blocked(getAccountRow(state.db, onDefault.accountId), onDefault.id)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('pauses at the cap until the next local month, with one card and account.cap_reached', () => {
    seedSpend(store, project, 5);
    const after = listEventsAfter(state.db, 0, 1000).at(-1)?.seq ?? 0;
    const pause = runtime.blocked(row(), project.id);
    expect(pause).toEqual({ reason: 'monthly_cap', until: new Date(2026, 10, 1) });
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)).toEqual(pause);
    expect(openCards()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(openCards()[0]?.body).toMatch(/\$5\.00 of its \$5\.00 cap/);
    const types = listEventsAfter(state.db, after, 100).map((e) => e.type);
    expect(types).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([EventType.AccountPaused, EventType.AccountCapReached]),
    );

    const { workstream } = seedWorkstream(store, { project });
    state.write((ctx) => {
      transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
      transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'PAUSED_LIMIT', fence: null });
    });
    clock.advance(new Date(2026, 10, 1).getTime() - clock.now().getTime());
    expect(runtime.isPaused(key.id)).toBe(false);
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, workstream.id).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('rewrites the cap card with the overshoot as running sessions end', () => {
    seedSpend(store, project, 5);
    runtime.blocked(row(), project.id);
    seedSpend(store, project, 2);
    runtime.sessionEnded(row(), project.id);
    expect(openCards()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(openCards()[0]?.body).toMatch(/\$7\.00 of its \$5\.00 cap.*\$2\.00 over/);
  });

  it('resumes when the cap is raised', () => {
    seedSpend(store, project, 5);
    runtime.blocked(row(), project.id);
    state.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, key.id, { monthlyCapUsd: 10 }));
    runtime.saved(key.id);
    expect(runtime.isPaused(key.id)).toBe(false);
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('refusals and usage limits', () => {
  it('pauses a refused account with no end, outranking a usage limit, until it is saved', () => {
    runtime.refused(row(), project.id, {
      problem: 'secret_changed',
      message: 'The Keychain secret of account "Client key" changed outside 10db',
      seenIdentity: null,
    });
    runtime.rateLimited(key.id, new Date(clock.now().getTime() + 60_000), project.id);
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)).toEqual({ reason: 'refused', until: null });
    const refusal = openCards().find((c) => c.title.includes('refused'));
    expect(refusal?.body).toContain('PUT /accounts/');
    expect(refusal?.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['Resume', 'Later']);
    runtime.saved(key.id);
    expect(runtime.isPaused(key.id)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('leaves a usage-limit pause alone when the account is saved', () => {
    runtime.rateLimited(key.id, null, project.id);
    runtime.saved(key.id);
    expect(runtime.blocked(row(), project.id)?.reason).toBe('usage_limit');
    clock.advance(30 * 60_000);
    expect(runtime.isPaused(key.id)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('resumes a refused account when its card is answered Resume', () => {
    runtime.refused(row(), project.id, {
      problem: 'cannot_bill',
      message: 'x',
      seenIdentity: null,
    });
    const answers = createAccountCardAnswers({
      resume: (id) => {
        pauses.resume(id);
      },
    });
    const item = openCards()[0];
    if (!item) throw new Error('the refusal raised no card');
    expect(item.detail?.[ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]).toMatchObject({
      accountId: key.id,
      reason: 'refused',
      problem: 'cannot_bill',
    });
    answers(item, { choice: AccountCardChoice.Resume });
    expect(runtime.isPaused(key.id)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { Month } from '@10db/contracts';
import type { UsageWindow } from '@10db/db';

export interface MonthWindow {
  readonly month: string;
  readonly start: Date;
  readonly end: Date;
}

const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** The calendar month containing `at`, in the machine's local time (accounts spec §6). */
export function monthOf(at: Date): MonthWindow {
  const start = new Date(at.getFullYear(), at.getMonth(), 1);
  const end = new Date(at.getFullYear(), at.getMonth() + 1, 1);
  return { month: `${String(start.getFullYear())}-${pad(start.getMonth() + 1)}`, start, end };
}

/** A `YYYY-MM` month, in local time. */
export function monthWindow(month: string): MonthWindow {
  const valid = Month.parse(month);
  return monthOf(new Date(Number(valid.slice(0, 4)), Number(valid.slice(5, 7)) - 1, 1));
}

export function usageWindow(window: MonthWindow): UsageWindow {
  return { month: window.month, start: window.start.toISOString(), end: window.end.toISOString() };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/cap.ts`:

```ts
import { isMetered } from '@10db/contracts';
import { accountSpendUsd, type AccountRow, type DbReader } from '@10db/db';
import { monthOf, usageWindow, type MonthWindow } from './month';

export interface CapStatus {
  readonly spentUsd: number;
  readonly capUsd: number;
  readonly window: MonthWindow;
}

/** A metered account's spend this month against its cap; null when it has no cap (accounts spec §6). */
export function capStatus(db: DbReader, account: AccountRow, now: Date): CapStatus | null {
  if (account.monthlyCapUsd === null || !isMetered(account.kind)) return null;
  const window = monthOf(now);
  return {
    spentUsd: accountSpendUsd(db, account.id, usageWindow(window)),
    capUsd: account.monthlyCapUsd,
    window,
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/cards.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL,
  AccountCardChoice,
  AccountKind,
  AccountPauseReason,
  AccountProblem,
  BedrockSettings,
  type AccountId,
  type AccountRefusal,
  type InboxOption,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import type { AccountRow, Json } from '@10db/db';
import { signInCommand } from '../environment';
import type { CapStatus } from './cap';

export interface AccountCard {
  readonly title: string;
  readonly body: string;
  readonly foldKey: string;
  readonly detail: Record<string, Json>;
  readonly options?: readonly InboxOption[];
}

/** Cards about an account fold per project on what they are about. */
export function accountFoldKey(id: AccountId, ...parts: readonly string[]): string {
  return ['account', id, ...parts].join(':');
}

const money = (usd: number): string => `$${usd.toFixed(2)}`;

function detail(
  account: AccountRow,
  reason: AccountPauseReason | null,
  problem: AccountProblem | null,
  seenIdentity: string | null,
): Record<string, Json> {
  return { [ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]: { accountId: account.id, reason, problem, seenIdentity } };
}

function signInAgain(account: AccountRow): string {
  if (account.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription)
    return `Sign in again with: ${signInCommand(account.configDir)}`;
  if (account.kind === AccountKind.Bedrock) {
    const settings = BedrockSettings.safeParse(account.settings);
    if (settings.success && settings.data.awsProfile !== null)
      return `Sign in again with: aws sso login --profile ${settings.data.awsProfile}`;
  }
  return `Check the account's key, then set it again with PUT /accounts/${account.id}/secret.`;
}

/** The exact step that fixes the problem for this account (accounts spec §5 "Expired credentials"). */
export function fixFor(account: AccountRow, problem: AccountProblem): string {
  switch (problem) {
    case AccountProblem.SignedOut:
      return `${signInAgain(account)} Then answer Resume.`;
    case AccountProblem.Unconfirmed:
      return `Confirm the identity ${account.label} runs as, or set it with PATCH /accounts/${account.id}.`;
    case AccountProblem.IdentityMismatch:
      return account.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
        ? `If that is the right account, confirm it; otherwise ${signInAgain(account).toLowerCase()} and answer Resume.`
        : `Check the account's key and its expected organization (PATCH /accounts/${account.id}), then answer Resume.`;
    case AccountProblem.WrongCredentials:
      return "10db strips every other credential, so a newer Claude Code may read a different variable; the system check names the version 10db's checks were verified on.";
    case AccountProblem.SecretMissing:
    case AccountProblem.SecretChanged:
      return `Set the secret with PUT /accounts/${account.id}/secret; the account resumes when it is saved.`;
    case AccountProblem.CannotBill:
      return "Check the account's billing with its provider, then answer Resume.";
  }
}

export function usageLimitCard(account: AccountRow, until: Date): AccountCard {
  return {
    title: `${account.label} usage limit reached`,
    body: `Every lane on ${account.label} is paused until ${until.toISOString()}. Work resumes automatically.`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit),
    detail: detail(account, AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, null, null),
  };
}

/** The card's body is rewritten as running sessions end, so it shows the final overshoot. */
export function capCard(account: AccountRow, cap: CapStatus): AccountCard {
  const over = cap.spentUsd - cap.capUsd;
  return {
    title: `${account.label} reached its monthly cap`,
    body: `${money(cap.spentUsd)} of its ${money(cap.capUsd)} cap is spent in ${cap.window.month}${over > 0 ? `, ${money(over)} over` : ''}. New sessions on ${account.label} wait until ${cap.window.end.toISOString()} or until the cap is raised. Sessions already running finish, and this card shows what they add.`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap),
    detail: detail(account, AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap, null, null),
  };
}

/** A refusal names what Claude Code reported in the body and the detail only; never in logs. */
export function refusedCard(account: AccountRow, refusal: AccountRefusal): AccountCard {
  const seen =
    refusal.seenIdentity === null ? '' : ` Claude Code reported ${refusal.seenIdentity}.`;
  return {
    title: `${account.label}: sessions refused`,
    body: `${refusal.message}.${seen} ${account.label}'s work waits until the account is fixed. ${fixFor(account, refusal.problem)}`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountPauseReason.Refused, refusal.problem),
    detail: detail(account, AccountPauseReason.Refused, refusal.problem, refusal.seenIdentity),
    options: [
      { label: AccountCardChoice.Resume, description: 'I fixed it: try the account again' },
      { label: AccountCardChoice.Later, description: 'Keep its work waiting' },
    ],
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the pauses, the runtime and the card answers**

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/pauses.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountPauseReason,
  EventType,
  WorkstreamState,
  type AccountId,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  deleteAppSetting,
  getAppSetting,
  getProject,
  listAccounts,
  listWorkstreams,
  setAppSetting,
  transitionWorkstream,
  type Json,
} from '@10db/db';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Clock, Timer } from '../clock';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { InboxService } from '../inbox';
import type { AccountCard } from './cards';

export interface AccountPause {
  readonly reason: AccountPauseReason;
  /** When it ends; null until the account is saved or its card answered. */
  readonly until: Date | null;
}

export interface AccountPauses {
  /** Pauses the account unless a pause that outlasts this one holds it; the card is raised either way (it folds). */
  pause(
    accountId: AccountId,
    pause: AccountPause,
    at: {
      readonly projectId: ProjectId;
      readonly card: AccountCard;
      readonly data?: Record<string, Json>;
    },
  ): void;
  pausedFor(accountId: AccountId): AccountPause | null;
  resume(accountId: AccountId): void;
  /** Re-arms pauses persisted before a restart. */
  restore(): void;
  stop(): void;
}

const PauseSetting = z.object({
  reason: z.enum(AccountPauseReason),
  until: z.iso.datetime().nullable(),
});
const settingKey = (id: AccountId): string => `account_pause.${id}`;

/** A pause with no end outranks one that ends; otherwise the later end wins. */
function outlasts(next: AccountPause, current: AccountPause): boolean {
  if (next.until === null) return current.until !== null;
  if (current.until === null) return false;
  return next.until > current.until;
}

/** One pause per account (accounts spec §3: a usage-limit pause applies to the account that hit it). */
export function createAccountPauses(opts: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly inbox: InboxService;
  stopAccount(accountId: AccountId): Promise<void>;
  onResume(): void;
}): AccountPauses {
  const paused = new Map<AccountId, AccountPause>();
  const timers = new Map<AccountId, Timer>();

  function arm(id: AccountId, pause: AccountPause): void {
    timers.get(id)?.cancel();
    timers.delete(id);
    paused.set(id, pause);
    if (pause.until === null) return;
    timers.set(
      id,
      opts.clock.setTimeout(
        () => {
          resume(id);
        },
        Math.max(0, pause.until.getTime() - opts.clock.now().getTime()),
      ),
    );
  }

  function resume(id: AccountId): void {
    timers.get(id)?.cancel();
    timers.delete(id);
    const was = paused.get(id);
    paused.delete(id);
    opts.state.write((ctx) => {
      deleteAppSetting(ctx, settingKey(id));
      for (const ws of listWorkstreams(ctx.tx, { states: [WorkstreamState.PausedLimit] })) {
        if (getProject(ctx.tx, ws.projectId).accountId !== id) continue;
        transitionWorkstream(ctx, ws.id, {
          to: WorkstreamState.Queued,
          fence: null,
          patch: { pausedUntil: null },
          reason: 'the account resumed',
        });
      }
      ctx.emit({
        type: EventType.AccountResumed,
        entityType: 'account',
        entityId: id,
        data: { reason: was?.reason ?? null },
      });
    });
    opts.onResume();
  }

  return {
    pause(id, pause, at) {
      const current = paused.get(id);
      if (!current || outlasts(pause, current)) {
        arm(id, pause);
        opts.state.write((ctx) => {
          const until = pause.until?.toISOString() ?? null;
          setAppSetting(ctx, settingKey(id), { reason: pause.reason, until });
          const data = { reason: pause.reason, until, ...at.data };
          ctx.emit({ type: EventType.AccountPaused, entityType: 'account', entityId: id, data });
          if (pause.reason === AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap)
            ctx.emit({
              type: EventType.AccountCapReached,
              entityType: 'account',
              entityId: id,
              data,
            });
        });
      }
      opts.inbox.flag({
        projectId: at.projectId,
        workstreamId: null,
        title: at.card.title,
        body: at.card.body,
        detail: at.card.detail,
        foldKey: at.card.foldKey,
        ...(at.card.options ? { options: at.card.options } : {}),
      });
      // Only a usage limit stops running sessions; a capped or refused account lets them finish.
      if (pause.reason === AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit) void opts.stopAccount(id);
    },
    pausedFor: (id) => paused.get(id) ?? null,
    resume(id) {
      if (paused.has(id)) resume(id);
    },
    restore() {
      for (const account of listAccounts(opts.state.db, { includeArchived: true })) {
        const parsed = PauseSetting.safeParse(getAppSetting(opts.state.db, settingKey(account.id)));
        if (!parsed.success) continue;
        const until = parsed.data.until === null ? null : new Date(parsed.data.until);
        if (until !== null && until <= opts.clock.now()) resume(account.id);
        else arm(account.id, { reason: parsed.data.reason, until });
      }
    },
    stop() {
      for (const timer of timers.values()) timer.cancel();
      timers.clear();
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/runtime.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountPauseReason,
  type AccountId,
  type AccountRefusal,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { findOpenByFoldKey, getAccountRow, type AccountRow } from '@10db/db';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { InboxService } from '../inbox';
import { capStatus } from './cap';
import { accountFoldKey, capCard, refusedCard, usageLimitCard } from './cards';
import type { AccountPause, AccountPauses } from './pauses';

/** What the scheduler and the runner ask about an account before and after a session. */
export interface AccountRuntime {
  /** Why no session may start on it now: a pause, or its monthly cap (which this call pauses it for). */
  blocked(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId): AccountPause | null;
  /** A usage limit: pauses it until the reset (or the default length) and stops its running lanes. */
  rateLimited(accountId: AccountId, resetsAt: Date | null, projectId: ProjectId): void;
  /** A session on it was refused for its identity or credentials: pauses it until it is saved. */
  refused(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId, refusal: AccountRefusal): void;
  /** A session on it ended: an open cap card shows the spend so far. */
  sessionEnded(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId): void;
  isPaused(accountId: AccountId): boolean;
  /** It was saved: a cap or refusal pause ends, and the next check decides again. */
  saved(accountId: AccountId): void;
}

export function createAccountRuntime(opts: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly inbox: InboxService;
  readonly pauses: AccountPauses;
  readonly defaultPauseMs: number;
}): AccountRuntime {
  const db = opts.state.db;
  return {
    blocked(account, projectId) {
      const paused = opts.pauses.pausedFor(account.id);
      if (paused) return paused;
      const cap = capStatus(db, account, opts.clock.now());
      if (!cap || cap.spentUsd < cap.capUsd) return null;
      const pause: AccountPause = { reason: AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap, until: cap.window.end };
      opts.pauses.pause(account.id, pause, {
        projectId,
        card: capCard(account, cap),
        data: { month: cap.window.month, spentUsd: cap.spentUsd, capUsd: cap.capUsd },
      });
      return pause;
    },
    rateLimited(accountId, resetsAt, projectId) {
      const now = opts.clock.now();
      const until =
        resetsAt && resetsAt > now ? resetsAt : new Date(now.getTime() + opts.defaultPauseMs);
      opts.pauses.pause(
        accountId,
        { reason: AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, until },
        { projectId, card: usageLimitCard(getAccountRow(db, accountId), until) },
      );
    },
    refused(account, projectId, refusal) {
      opts.pauses.pause(
        account.id,
        { reason: AccountPauseReason.Refused, until: null },
        { projectId, card: refusedCard(account, refusal), data: { problem: refusal.problem } },
      );
    },
    sessionEnded(account, projectId) {
      if (opts.pauses.pausedFor(account.id)?.reason !== AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap) return;
      const cap = capStatus(db, account, opts.clock.now());
      const open = findOpenByFoldKey(
        db,
        projectId,
        accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountPauseReason.MonthlyCap),
      );
      if (cap && open) opts.inbox.reword(open.id, capCard(account, cap).body);
    },
    isPaused: (accountId) => opts.pauses.pausedFor(accountId) !== null,
    saved(accountId) {
      const paused = opts.pauses.pausedFor(accountId);
      if (paused && paused.reason !== AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit) opts.pauses.resume(accountId);
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/card-answers.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL,
  AccountCardChoice,
  AccountCardDetail,
  type AccountId,
  type InboxAnswer,
  type InboxItemDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';

/** Acts on an account card's answer before the inbox records it; other cards pass through untouched. */
export function createAccountCardAnswers(deps: {
  resume(accountId: AccountId): void;
}): (item: InboxItemDto, answer: InboxAnswer) => void {
  return (item, answer) => {
    const card = AccountCardDetail.safeParse(item.detail?.[ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]);
    if (!card.success) return;
    if (answer.choice === AccountCardChoice.Resume) deps.resume(card.data.accountId);
  };
}
```

```ts
}
```

In `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`:

- `RunnerOptions` drops `onRateLimited` and gains `readonly accounts: Pick<AccountRuntime, 'blocked' | 'rateLimited' | 'refused' | 'sessionEnded'>;`.
- `Control.provider` becomes `readonly accountId: AccountId;`, set in `launch` from `getProject(db, getWorkstreamRow(db, id).projectId).accountId`. `Runner.stopProvider` becomes `stopAccount(accountId: AccountId, reason: StopReason): Promise<void>`, filtering `active` by `c.accountId === accountId` (the `no-unnecessary-condition` disable goes).
- Both `opts.onRateLimited(project.provider, …, project.id)` calls become `opts.accounts.rateLimited(ctl.accountId, …, project.id)`.
- Add to `Outcome`:

```ts
  | {
      /** The account can take no session now: it is paused or at its monthly cap. */
      readonly kind: 'held';
      readonly until: Date | null;
      readonly providerSessionId: string | null;
    }
```

and add `'held'` to the kinds `finishSession`'s `parkedAfterWait` treats like `paused`.

- In `run`, after the `verifying` branch and before `runSession`:

```ts
const lane = getProject(db, getWorkstreamRow(db, id).projectId);
// The scheduler checked before the launch; a lane checks again before each further session (accounts spec §6).
const block = opts.accounts.blocked(getAccountRow(db, ctl.accountId), lane.id);
if (block) {
  settle(id, ctl, { kind: 'held', until: block.until, providerSessionId: null });
  return;
}
```

- In `settle`, add:

```ts
case 'held':
  opts.state.write((ctx) => {
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.PausedLimit,
      fence: ctl.fence,
      // The next action is kept: the lane resumes exactly where it stopped once the account does.
      patch: {
        pausedUntil: outcome.until?.toISOString() ?? null,
        ...(outcome.providerSessionId
          ? {
              nextAction: {
                kind: 'resume',
                providerSessionId: outcome.providerSessionId,
                reason: 'the account resumed',
              },
            }
          : {}),
      },
      reason: 'the account is paused',
    });
    clearLease(ctx, id);
  });
  return;
```

- At the end of `runSession`, after `finishSession` and before `afterSession`: `opts.accounts.sessionEnded(getAccountRow(db, ctl.accountId), project.id);`.

### Task 5: Sessions verify their account before the prompt

Sessions launch through the launcher and prove their account (accounts spec §5).

- **Before the prompt:** `accountInfo()` must show the account's identity and the credential its kind needs.
- **At the first `init`:** the key source and the model are checked again.
- **Mid-session:** a credential error ends the session.

An account refusal sends no prompt (or stops at the first turn), pauses the account and keeps the work waiting. The gate and the repo's MCP servers get the project env, which never holds the account's credentials. `billingMode` stops being read anywhere in the daemon.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/sessions/account-check.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/sessions/account-check.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/sessions/types.ts`, `claude-adapter.ts`, `claude-mapping.ts`, `claude-test-support.ts`, `fake-adapter.ts`, `unavailable-adapter.ts`, `index.ts`, and their tests (`claude-adapter.test.ts`, `claude-mapping.test.ts`, `fake-adapter.test.ts`, `unavailable-adapter.test.ts`, `claude-adapter.live.test.ts`)
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/engine.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/fixtures.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/environment/session-env.ts`, `session-env.test.ts`, `environment/index.ts` (the billing-mode env goes)
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/main.ts`, and any daemon test passing `sessionEnv` (`grep -rn sessionEnv apps/daemon`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 3's `Launcher` (with `loginConfigDir`), `createLauncher`, `AccountLaunchError`, `AccountExpectation`, `SessionLaunch`, `Keychain`, `unavailableKeychain`, `systemKeychain`, `secretFingerprint`; Task 4's `AccountRuntime.refused` and the runner's `held` outcome; Task 2's `getAccountRow`, `recordAccountSecret`, `accountSecretRef`, `seedDefaultAccount` (`configDir`), `listAccounts`; Task 1's `AccountProblem`, `AccountRefusal`, `MODEL_ALIAS_PATTERN`.
- Produces:
  - `sessions/account-check.ts`: `interface SeenAccount { email; organization; subscriptionType; tokenSource; apiKeySource; apiProvider }` (each `string | undefined`), `seenFrom(info: AccountInfo-like): SeenAccount`, `checkAccount(expect: AccountExpectation, seen: SeenAccount): AccountRefusal | null`, `missingAccountRefusal(expect: AccountExpectation, why: string): AccountRefusal | null` (`signed_out` for a subscription whose `accountInfo()` gave nothing, null for a keyed kind), `checkInitCredentials(expect, apiKeySource: string | undefined, preflight: SeenAccount): AccountRefusal | null`, `modelMatches(requested: string, reported: string): boolean`, `credentialProblem(error: string | undefined): AccountProblem | null`.
  - `SessionSpec.account: AccountExpectation` replaces `billingMode`.
  - `SessionEndReason.AccountRefused = 'account_refused'`; the `ended` event gains `readonly refusal?: AccountRefusal`; `turn_complete` gains `cachedTokens`.
  - `SessionAdapter.probeAccount(probe: AccountProbeSpec): Promise<SeenAccount>`, with `interface AccountProbeSpec { cwd: string; env: Readonly<Record<string, string>> }` and `class AccountProbeError`.
  - `FakeAdapter.seen: (spec: SessionSpec) => Partial<SeenAccount> & { model?: string }`, `FakeAdapter.probeSeen: SeenAccount`, `FakeAdapter.probes: AccountProbeSpec[]`, `FAKE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL = 'builder@example.com'`, and the fake step `{ do: 'auth_error', error }`.
  - `RunnerOptions.launcher: Pick<Launcher, 'projectEnv' | 'session'>` replaces `sessionEnv`; the runner's `held` outcome gains `refusal: AccountRefusal | null`.
  - `DaemonOptions.loginEnv?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>` and `keychain?: Keychain` replace `sessionEnv`. `startDaemon` seeds the default account on `launcher.loginConfigDir()`.
  - `apps/daemon/test/fixtures.ts`: `createMemoryKeychain(): Keychain`. `createTestEngine`'s setup gains `secret?: string` (written to the memory Keychain and recorded); `TestEngine.keychain: Keychain`.

`apps/daemon/src/sessions/account-check.test.ts`:

```ts
import { mintId, type AccountProblem, type SupportedAccountKind } from '@10db/contracts';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { AccountExpectation } from '../environment';
import {
  checkAccount,
  checkInitCredentials,
  credentialProblem,
  missingAccountRefusal,
  modelMatches,
  type SeenAccount,
} from './account-check';

const NONE: SeenAccount = {
  email: undefined,
  organization: undefined,
  subscriptionType: undefined,
  tokenSource: undefined,
  apiKeySource: undefined,
  apiProvider: undefined,
};
const expect_ = (
  kind: SupportedAccountKind,
  identity: string | null = null,
): AccountExpectation => ({
  accountId: mintId('Account'),
  label: 'Company A',
  kind,
  identity,
});
const SUB: Partial<SeenAccount> = {
  email: 'a@example.com',
  subscriptionType: 'Claude Max',
  apiProvider: 'firstParty',
  apiKeySource: 'none',
};

describe('checkAccount', () => {
  it.each<{
    name: string;
    expected: AccountExpectation;
    seen: Partial<SeenAccount>;
    problem: AccountProblem | null;
    seenIdentity?: string | null;
  }>([
    {
      name: 'the confirmed subscription',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: SUB,
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'the same email in another case',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'A@Example.com'),
      seen: SUB,
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'a subscription with no identity yet',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription'),
      seen: SUB,
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'another subscription in the Keychain',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: { ...SUB, email: 'b@example.com' },
      problem: 'identity_mismatch',
      seenIdentity: 'b@example.com',
    },
    {
      name: 'a signed-out config directory',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: { apiProvider: 'firstParty' },
      problem: 'signed_out',
    },
    {
      name: 'an API key where the subscription should be',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: { ...SUB, apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' },
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'a gateway token where the subscription should be',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: { ...SUB, tokenSource: 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN' },
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'Bedrock where the subscription should be',
      expected: expect_('claude_subscription', 'a@example.com'),
      seen: { ...SUB, apiProvider: 'bedrock' },
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'an API key',
      expected: expect_('anthropic_api_key'),
      seen: { apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', apiProvider: 'firstParty' },
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'an API key from a key helper',
      expected: expect_('anthropic_api_key'),
      seen: { apiKeySource: 'apiKeyHelper', apiProvider: 'firstParty' },
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'an API key in the expected organization',
      expected: expect_('anthropic_api_key', 'org-1'),
      seen: { apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', apiProvider: 'firstParty', organization: 'org-1' },
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'an API key reporting no organization when one is expected',
      expected: expect_('anthropic_api_key', 'org-1'),
      seen: { apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', apiProvider: 'firstParty' },
      problem: 'identity_mismatch',
      seenIdentity: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'Bedrock',
      expected: expect_('bedrock'),
      seen: { apiProvider: 'bedrock' },
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'Bedrock that fell back to the first-party login',
      expected: expect_('bedrock'),
      seen: SUB,
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'a gateway as spike 12 saw it',
      expected: expect_('gateway'),
      seen: {
        apiProvider: 'firstParty',
        apiKeySource: 'none',
        tokenSource: 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN',
      },
      problem: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'a gateway that fell back to the saved login',
      expected: expect_('gateway'),
      seen: SUB,
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
    {
      name: 'a gateway reporting a provider spike 12 never saw',
      expected: expect_('gateway'),
      seen: { apiProvider: 'gateway', apiKeySource: 'none', tokenSource: 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN' },
      problem: 'wrong_credentials',
    },
  ])('$name', ({ expected, seen, problem, seenIdentity }) => {
    const refusal = checkAccount(expected, { ...NONE, ...seen });
    expect(refusal?.problem ?? null).toBe(problem);
    if (seenIdentity !== undefined) expect(refusal?.seenIdentity ?? null).toBe(seenIdentity);
    // A refusal's message is logged, so it never carries an email or organization.
    expect(refusal?.message ?? '').not.toMatch(/@|org-1/);
    if (refusal) expect(refusal.message).toContain('Company A');
  });

  it('re-checks the key source at init with the provider the preflight saw', () => {
    const expected = expect_('anthropic_api_key');
    const preflight: SeenAccount = {
      ...NONE,
      apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY',
      apiProvider: 'firstParty',
    };
    expect(checkInitCredentials(expected, 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY', preflight)).toBeNull();
    expect(checkInitCredentials(expected, 'none', preflight)?.problem).toBe('wrong_credentials');
  });
});

describe('modelMatches', () => {
  it.each<[string, string, boolean]>([
    ['haiku', 'claude-haiku-5-5', true],
    ['sonnet', 'claude-sonnet-5-5[1m]', true],
    ['sonnet', 'us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5-5-v1:0', true],
    ['haiku', '~anthropic/claude-haiku-latest', true],
    ['opus', 'claude-sonnet-5-5', false],
    ['~anthropic/claude-haiku-latest', '~anthropic/claude-haiku-latest', true],
    ['us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5-5-v1:0', 'claude-sonnet-5-5', false],
  ])('%s against %s: %s', (requested, reported, ok) => {
    expect(modelMatches(requested, reported)).toBe(ok);
  });
});

describe('missingAccountRefusal', () => {
  it.each<[SupportedAccountKind, AccountProblem | null]>([
    ['claude_subscription', 'signed_out'],
    ['anthropic_api_key', null],
    ['bedrock', null],
    ['gateway', null],
  ])('a %s whose accountInfo() gave nothing: %s', (kind, problem) => {
    const refusal = missingAccountRefusal(
      expect_(kind),
      'Claude Code reported no account within 15 s',
    );
    expect(refusal?.problem ?? null).toBe(problem);
    if (refusal) expect(refusal.message).toContain('Company A');
  });
});

describe('credentialProblem', () => {
  it.each<[string | undefined, AccountProblem | null]>([
    ['authentication_failed', 'signed_out'],
    ['oauth_org_not_allowed', 'signed_out'],
    ['cloud_credential_error', 'signed_out'],
    ['billing_error', 'cannot_bill'],
    ['account_on_hold', 'cannot_bill'],
    ['rate_limit', null],
    ['server_error', null],
    [undefined, null],
  ])('%s → %s', (error, problem) => {
    expect(credentialProblem(error)).toBe(problem);
  });
});
```

In `apps/daemon/src/sessions/claude-adapter.test.ts`:

- `spec()` replaces `billingMode: 'subscription'` with `account: { accountId: mintId('Account'), label: 'Claude plan', kind: 'claude_subscription', identity: 'builder@example.com' }`.
- `fakeQuery`'s default `accountInfo()` answer gains `email: 'builder@example.com'`.
- In `claude-test-support.ts`, `initMessage` takes a third parameter `model = 'claude-sonnet-5-5'` and puts it in `model` (the default spec asks for `sonnet`).
- The tests `refuses to start, without sending a prompt, when the billing preflight times out` and `… when accountInfo() rejects` become one table, because a subscription that answers nothing now reads as signed out and pauses its account, while a keyed account still fails its workstream:

```ts
describe('a preflight that gets no account', () => {
  const noAnswer = {
    'never answers': () => new Promise<AccountInfo>(() => undefined),
    rejects: () => Promise.reject(new Error('control channel closed')),
  } as const;
  it.each<{
    how: keyof typeof noAnswer;
    kind: 'claude_subscription' | 'anthropic_api_key';
    reason: string;
    message: RegExp;
  }>([
    {
      how: 'never answers',
      kind: 'claude_subscription',
      reason: 'account_refused',
      message:
        /^Claude Code reported no account within 15 s for account "Claude plan".*No prompt was sent/,
    },
    {
      how: 'rejects',
      kind: 'claude_subscription',
      reason: 'account_refused',
      message: /^Claude Code reported no account.*No prompt was sent/,
    },
    {
      how: 'never answers',
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      reason: 'billing_refused',
      message:
        /^Account check failed: Claude Code reported no account within 15 s.*No prompt was sent/,
    },
    {
      how: 'rejects',
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      reason: 'billing_refused',
      message: /^Account check failed: Claude Code reported no account.*No prompt was sent/,
    },
  ])('$kind: accountInfo() $how, so no prompt is sent', async ({ how, kind, reason, message }) => {
    const clock = new ManualClock();
    const { adapter, record } = adapterFor(
      { messages: [initMessage()], then: 'hang', account: noAnswer[how]() },
      1_000,
      clock,
    );
    const account = {
      accountId: mintId('Account'),
      label: 'Claude plan',
      kind,
      identity: kind === 'claude_subscription' ? 'builder@example.com' : null,
    };
    const done = collect(adapter.start(spec({ account })).events);
    clock.advance(BILLING_PREFLIGHT_MS);
    const events = await done;
    expect(events.map((e) => e.type)).toEqual(['ended']);
    expect(events[0]).toMatchObject({ type: 'ended', reason });
    if (reason === 'account_refused')
      expect(events[0]).toMatchObject({ refusal: { problem: 'signed_out', seenIdentity: null } });
    expect(endMessage(events[0])).toMatch(message);
    expect(record.inputs).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- The test `never sends the prompt when the billing preflight shows Bedrock on a subscription project` now expects `reason: 'account_refused'`, `refusal: { problem: 'wrong_credentials' }` and a message matching `/needs a Claude subscription.*No prompt was sent/`. `accepts a Bedrock session when the project bills to Bedrock` passes `account: { …, kind: 'bedrock', identity: null }` instead of `billingMode`. `reports running token totals and the cumulative cost` expects `cachedTokens: 0` on each event. Every other test that names `billingMode` uses the default `account`.

```ts
  it('refuses before the prompt when Claude Code reports another email', async () => {

    ]);
  });
});

describe('probeAccount', () => {
  it('reads the account without a prompt, without project settings or MCP servers, and ends the process', async () => {
    const fake = fakeQuery({
      messages: [],
      then: 'hang',
      account: {
        apiProvider: 'firstParty',
        subscriptionType: 'Claude Max',
        email: 'b@example.com',
      },
    });
    const adapter = createClaudeAdapter({
      claudePath: '/usr/local/bin/claude',
      pluginDir: '/harness',
      logger: silentLogger,
      query: fake.query,
    });
    const seen = await adapter.probeAccount({
      cwd: '/data',
      env: { PATH: '/bin', CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: '/data/accounts/a/claude' },
    });
    expect(seen).toMatchObject({
      email: 'b@example.com',
      apiProvider: 'firstParty',
      subscriptionType: 'Claude Max',
    });
    expect(fake.record.inputs).toEqual([]);
    expect(fake.record.mcpCalls).toEqual([]);
    expect(fake.record.options).toMatchObject({
      cwd: '/data',
      settingSources: [],
      strictMcpConfig: true,
    });
    expect(fake.record.options?.abortController?.signal.aborted).toBe(true);
  });

  it('fails with AccountProbeError when Claude Code reports no account in time', async () => {
    const clock = new ManualClock();
    const fake = fakeQuery({
      messages: [],
      then: 'hang',
      account: new Promise<never>(() => undefined),
    });
    const adapter = createClaudeAdapter({
      claudePath: '/c',
      pluginDir: '/h',
      logger: silentLogger,
      query: fake.query,
      clock,
    });
    const probe = adapter.probeAccount({ cwd: '/data', env: {} });
    clock.advance(15_000);
    await expect(probe).rejects.toThrow(AccountProbeError);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the account check**

`apps/daemon/src/sessions/account-check.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountKind,
  AccountProblem,
  MODEL_ALIAS_PATTERN,
  type AccountRefusal,
  type SupportedAccountKind,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import type { AccountExpectation } from '../environment';

/** What Claude Code reports about the account a session runs on (`accountInfo()`). */
export interface SeenAccount {
  readonly email: string | undefined;
  readonly organization: string | undefined;
  readonly subscriptionType: string | undefined;
  readonly tokenSource: string | undefined;
  readonly apiKeySource: string | undefined;
  readonly apiProvider: string | undefined;
}

export function seenFrom(info: {
  readonly email?: string;
  readonly organization?: string;
  readonly subscriptionType?: string;
  readonly tokenSource?: string;
  readonly apiKeySource?: string;
  readonly apiProvider?: string;
}): SeenAccount {
  return {
    email: info.email,
    organization: info.organization,
    subscriptionType: info.subscriptionType,
    tokenSource: info.tokenSource,
    apiKeySource: info.apiKeySource,
    apiProvider: info.apiProvider,
  };
}

const NEEDS: Readonly<Record<SupportedAccountKind, string>> = {
  [AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription]: 'a Claude subscription',
  [AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey]: 'an Anthropic API key',
  [AccountKind.Bedrock]: 'Bedrock',
  [AccountKind.Gateway]: 'a gateway token',
};

const noKey = (source: string | undefined): boolean => source === undefined || source === 'none';

/** Key sources and providers only: never an email or organization, so it can be logged. */
function described(seen: SeenAccount): string {
  return `apiKeySource ${seen.apiKeySource ?? 'unreported'}, apiProvider ${seen.apiProvider ?? 'unreported'}, tokenSource ${seen.tokenSource ?? 'unreported'}, subscription ${seen.subscriptionType ? 'yes' : 'no'}`;
}

/**
 * Whether Claude Code bills the credential the kind needs. Only values a real session has shown are
 * accepted (spike 12 for the gateway), so a renamed variable fails loudly instead of billing someone else.
 */
function credentialsMatch(kind: SupportedAccountKind, seen: SeenAccount): boolean {
  switch (kind) {
    case AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription:
      // A plan's name is never compared; a gateway also looks first-party, so its token source rules it out.
      return (
        noKey(seen.apiKeySource) &&
        seen.apiProvider === 'firstParty' &&
        Boolean(seen.subscriptionType) &&
        seen.tokenSource !== 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN'
      );
    case AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey:
      return seen.apiKeySource === 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' && seen.apiProvider === 'firstParty';
    case AccountKind.Bedrock:
      return seen.apiProvider === 'bedrock';
    case AccountKind.Gateway:
      return (
        seen.tokenSource === 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN' &&
        seen.apiProvider === 'firstParty' &&
        noKey(seen.apiKeySource)
      );
  }
}

const same = (a: string, b: string): boolean => a.trim().toLowerCase() === b.trim().toLowerCase();

/** Before the prompt (accounts spec §5): who Claude Code is, and the credential it bills. */
export function checkAccount(expect: AccountExpectation, seen: SeenAccount): AccountRefusal | null {
  const who = `account "${expect.label}"`;
  if (
    expect.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription &&
    !seen.email &&
    !seen.subscriptionType &&
    noKey(seen.apiKeySource)
  )
    return {
      problem: AccountProblem.SignedOut,
      message: `Claude Code is not signed in for ${who}`,
      seenIdentity: null,
    };
  if (!credentialsMatch(expect.kind, seen))
    return {
      problem: AccountProblem.WrongCredentials,
      message: `${who} needs ${NEEDS[expect.kind]} but Claude Code reported ${described(seen)}`,
      seenIdentity: null,
    };
  if (expect.identity === null) return null;
  const subscription = expect.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription;
  const reported = subscription ? seen.email : seen.organization;
  if (reported !== undefined && same(reported, expect.identity)) return null;
  return {
    problem: AccountProblem.IdentityMismatch,
    message: `Claude Code reported a different ${subscription ? 'email' : 'organization'} than the one confirmed for ${who}`,
    seenIdentity: reported ?? null,
  };
}

/**
 * `accountInfo()` rejected or never answered. A signed-out config directory may report nothing at all,
 * so on a subscription that reads as signed out and the account waits for its sign-in; a keyed account
 * has no such reason, and null leaves its workstream to fail.
 */
export function missingAccountRefusal(
  expect: AccountExpectation,
  why: string,
): AccountRefusal | null {
  return expect.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
    ? {
        problem: AccountProblem.SignedOut,
        message: `${why} for account "${expect.label}", which looks signed out`,
        seenIdentity: null,
      }
    : null;
}

/** At the first init: the key source the CLI actually used, with the provider and plan the preflight saw. */
export function checkInitCredentials(
  expect: AccountExpectation,
  apiKeySource: string | undefined,
  preflight: SeenAccount,
): AccountRefusal | null {
  const seen = { ...preflight, apiKeySource };
  return credentialsMatch(expect.kind, seen)
    ? null
    : {
        problem: AccountProblem.WrongCredentials,
        message: `account "${expect.label}" needs ${NEEDS[expect.kind]} but Claude Code started with ${described(seen)}`,
        seenIdentity: null,
      };
}

/** An alias matches the model ID Claude Code resolved it to; a model-map ID must come back unchanged. */
export function modelMatches(requested: string, reported: string): boolean {
  if (requested === reported) return true;
  return (
    MODEL_ALIAS_PATTERN.test(requested) && reported.toLowerCase().includes(`claude-${requested}`)
  );
}

const CREDENTIAL_ERRORS: Readonly<Record<string, AccountProblem>> = {
  authentication_failed: AccountProblem.SignedOut,
  oauth_org_not_allowed: AccountProblem.SignedOut,
  cloud_credential_error: AccountProblem.SignedOut,
  billing_error: AccountProblem.CannotBill,
  account_on_hold: AccountProblem.CannotBill,
};

/** An assistant error that is the account's, not the work's. */
export function credentialProblem(error: string | undefined): AccountProblem | null {
  return error === undefined ? null : (CREDENTIAL_ERRORS[error] ?? null);
}
```

```ts
if (!account) {
  const why = `Claude Code reported no account within ${String(BILLING_PREFLIGHT_MS / 1_000)} s`;
  // A signed-out subscription may answer nothing: the account waits for its sign-in, the lane doesn't fail.
  const signedOut = missingAccountRefusal(spec.account, why);
  if (signedOut)
    refuse(SessionEndReason.AccountRefused, `${signedOut.message}. No prompt was sent.`, signedOut);
  else
    refuse(
      SessionEndReason.BillingRefused,
      `Account check failed: ${why}. No prompt was sent. ${fixHint}`,
    );
  return;
}
const refusal = checkAccount(spec.account, seenFrom(account));
if (refusal) {
  refuse(SessionEndReason.AccountRefused, `${refusal.message}. No prompt was sent.`, refusal);
  return;
}
```

```ts
    `${credentials.message}. The session stopped at its first turn, before any tool ran.`,

}
if (!modelMatches(spec.model, init.model)) {

    `Model check failed: 10db asked for ${spec.model} but Claude Code runs ${init.model}. The session stopped at its first turn, before any tool ran; that turn's first request may have been billed.`,

}
resolveVerified(true);
```

- Add the probe, and return it from `createClaudeAdapter` as `probeAccount: (probe) => probeAccount(probe, opts, runQuery)`:

```ts
/** How long an identity probe waits for `accountInfo()`. */
const IDENTITY_PROBE_MS = 15_000;

/** Ends the stream so the process exits; anything it throws after the abort means nothing here. */
async function drain(q: AsyncIterable<unknown>): Promise<void> {
  try {
    for await (const message of q) if (message === undefined) break;
  } catch {
    return;
  }
}

/** Claude Code started only to say who it is: no prompt, no project settings, no MCP servers, no tools. */
async function probeAccount(
  probe: AccountProbeSpec,
  opts: ClaudeAdapterOptions,
  runQuery: QueryFn,
): Promise<SeenAccount> {
  const clock = opts.clock ?? systemClock;
  const input = createChannel<SDKUserMessage>();
  const abortController = new AbortController();
  const q = runQuery({
    prompt: input,
    options: {
      cwd: probe.cwd,
      env: { ...probe.env },
      pathToClaudeCodeExecutable: opts.claudePath,
      settingSources: [],
      strictMcpConfig: true,
      permissionMode: 'default',
      canUseTool: () =>
        Promise.resolve({ behavior: 'deny', message: 'An identity probe runs no tools.' }),
      abortController,
    },
  });
  try {
    const info = await settleWithin(clock, q.accountInfo(), IDENTITY_PROBE_MS);
    if (!info)
      throw new AccountProbeError(
        `Claude Code reported no account within ${String(IDENTITY_PROBE_MS / 1_000)} s`,
      );
    return seenFrom(info);
  } finally {
    input.end();
    abortController.abort();
    void drain(q);
  }
}
```

```ts
/** The email the fake reports for a subscription that expects none, and for an identity probe. */
export const FAKE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL = 'builder@example.com';

const NOTHING_SEEN: SeenAccount = {
  email: undefined,
  organization: undefined,
  subscriptionType: undefined,
  tokenSource: undefined,
  apiKeySource: undefined,
  apiProvider: undefined,
};

/** What a well-configured session of each kind reports: exactly what it expects. */
function expectedSeen(expect: AccountExpectation): SeenAccount {
  switch (expect.kind) {
    case AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription:
      return {
        ...NOTHING_SEEN,
        email: expect.identity ?? FAKE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL,
        subscriptionType: 'Claude Max',
        apiProvider: 'firstParty',
        apiKeySource: 'none',
      };
    case AccountKind.AnthropicApiKey:
      return {
        ...NOTHING_SEEN,
        apiKeySource: 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY',
        apiProvider: 'firstParty',
        organization: expect.identity ?? undefined,
      };
    case AccountKind.Bedrock:
      return {
        ...NOTHING_SEEN,
        apiProvider: 'bedrock',
        organization: expect.identity ?? undefined,
      };
    case AccountKind.Gateway:
      return {
        ...NOTHING_SEEN,
        apiProvider: 'firstParty',
        apiKeySource: 'none',
        tokenSource: 'ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN',
      };
  }
}
```

```ts
  /** What a session reports for its account, over what it expects; tests set it to stage a mismatch. */

    email: FAKE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL,
    subscriptionType: 'Claude Max',
    apiProvider: 'firstParty',
    apiKeySource: 'none',
  };
  readonly probes: AccountProbeSpec[] = [];
```

### Task 6: Unready accounts, identity confirmation, staggered starts and offered aliases

An account that can't run a session yet never launches. That means a subscription with no confirmed identity, or a keyed account with no secret. Its work waits `QUEUED`, and the preparer raises one card. An unready account is prepared, never refused or paused: a lane that finds its account unready before a further session goes back to `QUEUED` and asks the preparer.

- **The default account:** the preparer records its identity from the first probe of its directory, the builder's current login, and raises a card that holds nothing: "Claude plan runs as <email>".
- **Any other subscription:** the card offers Confirm, with the email Claude Code reported. While that card is open, the preparer doesn't probe the account again.
- **Signed out:** a probe that sees no login, or gets no answer at all, raises the sign-in card with the account's sign-in command.
- **Staggering:** on a subscription account, sessions and probes start one at a time (the spec's fallback while the refresh race is open).
- **Aliases:** an account's `aliases` limit what the router and escalation pick.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/readiness.ts`, `start-gate.ts`, `preparer.ts`, and `preparer.test.ts`, `start-gate.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/cards.ts`, `card-answers.ts`, `index.ts`, `runtime.test.ts` (its new Confirm case, and `confirm` on Task 4's `createAccountCardAnswers` call)
- Create: `apps/daemon/src/routing/offered.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/routing/offered.test.ts`; Modify: `apps/daemon/src/routing/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/team/steps.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/team/steps.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/scheduler/scheduler.ts`, `scheduler.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `runner.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/engine.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 5's `SessionAdapter.probeAccount`, `SeenAccount`, `AccountProbeError`, `checkAccount`, `FAKE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`; Task 4's `AccountPauses`, `AccountRuntime.saved`, `accountFoldKey`, `refusedCard`, `createAccountCardAnswers`; Task 3's `Launcher.probe`, `signInCommand`; Task 2's `patchAccount`, `getAccount`, `defaultAccountId`, `seedDefaultAccount` (`configDir`); `findOpenByFoldKey` from `@10db/db`, as Task 4's runtime uses it.
- Produces:
  - `unready(account: AccountRow): AccountRefusal | null`: `unconfirmed` for a subscription with no `expectedIdentity`, `secret_missing` for a keyed account with no recorded fingerprint.
  - `interface StartGate { acquire(key: string): Promise<() => void>; waiting(key: string): number }`, `createStartGate(): StartGate`.
  - `PREPARE_RETRY_MS = 60_000`; `interface AccountPreparer { prepare(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId): void; idle(): Promise<void> }`; `createAccountPreparer(opts)`. The preparer knows the default account by `defaultAccountId`, whatever its label or `configDir`.
  - `confirmCard`, `signInCard(account, why?: string)`, `secretCard`, `recordedCard(account, email)` in `cards.ts`; `refusedCard` adds Confirm when it names a seen subscription email.
  - `createAccountCardAnswers(deps: { resume(accountId: AccountId): void; confirm(accountId: AccountId, identity: string): void })`: `confirm` is required, so Task 4's callers (`runtime.test.ts`, `start.ts`, `engine.ts`) pass it here.
  - `SchedulerOptions.accounts` adds `Pick<AccountPreparer, 'prepare'>`; `RunnerOptions.accounts` adds the same, and `RunnerOptions.startGate: StartGate`; the runner's `Outcome` gains `{ kind: 'unready'; refusal: AccountRefusal; providerSessionId: null }`.
  - `offeredProfiles(profiles: readonly ModelProfileDto[], aliases: readonly string[] | undefined): ModelProfileDto[]` (from `routing/index.ts`).
  - `TestEngine.startGate: StartGate`; `TestEngine.prepared: { accountId: AccountId; projectId: ProjectId }[]` (what the runner asked the preparer).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createStartGate } from './index';

describe('the start gate', () => {
  it('lets one start through per key, in order, and keys apart', async () => {
    const gate = createStartGate();
    const order: string[] = [];
    const first = await gate.acquire('a');
    const second = gate.acquire('a').then((release) => {
      order.push('second');
      return release;
    });
    const other = await gate.acquire('b');
    order.push('other');
    expect(gate.waiting('a')).toBe(1);
    order.push('first done');
    first();
    first();
    (await second)();
    other();
    expect(order).toEqual(['other', 'first done', 'second']);
    expect(gate.waiting('a')).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/preparer.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL, AccountCardChoice, type AccountDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  createTestStore,
  getAccount,
  getAccountRow,
  listInbox,
  seedAccount,
  seedDefaultAccount,
  seedProject,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createMemoryKeychain } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { createLauncher } from '../environment';
import { createEventBus, createStateStore, type StateStore } from '../events';
import { createInboxService, type InboxService } from '../inbox';
import { silentLogger } from '../log';
import { AccountProbeError, FakeAdapter } from '../sessions';
import {
  createAccountCardAnswers,
  createAccountPreparer,
  createStartGate,
  PREPARE_RETRY_MS,
  type AccountPreparer,
} from './index';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let state: StateStore;
let clock: ManualClock;
let inbox: InboxService;
let adapter: FakeAdapter;
let preparer: AccountPreparer;
let ready: number;

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new ManualClock();
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => clock.now()));
  state = createStateStore(store, createEventBus());
  inbox = createInboxService({ state, onParkedAnswer: () => undefined });
  adapter = new FakeAdapter({});
  ready = 0;
  preparer = createAccountPreparer({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    adapter,
    launcher: createLauncher({
      base: { PATH: '/bin' },
      clientApp: 'test',
      keychain: createMemoryKeychain(),
    }),
    startGate: createStartGate(),
    dataDir: '/data',
    logger: silentLogger,
    onReady: () => {
      ready += 1;
    },
  });
});
afterEach(() => {
  close();
});

const openCards = () => listInbox(state.db, { states: ['OPEN'] });
/** The default account with no identity yet, as a fresh daemon seeds it, and a project on it. */
function freshDefault(configDir: string | null = null): {
  defaultAccount: AccountDto;
  projectId: ReturnType<typeof seedProject>['id'];
} {
  const defaultAccount = store.write((ctx) =>
    seedDefaultAccount(ctx, { expectedIdentity: null, configDir }),
  ).value;
  if (!defaultAccount) throw new Error('the store already had an account');
  return { defaultAccount, projectId: seedProject(store).id };
}

describe('the preparer', () => {
  it.each<[string, string | null]>([
    ["Claude Code's default directory", null],
    ["the login shell's own directory", '/h/b/.claude-work'],
  ])(
    "records the default account's identity from its first probe of %s, with a card that holds nothing, then asks for a tick",
    async (_where, configDir) => {
      const { defaultAccount, projectId } = freshDefault(configDir);
      adapter.probeSeen = { ...adapter.probeSeen, email: 'me@example.com' };
      preparer.prepare(getAccountRow(state.db, defaultAccount.id), projectId);
      await preparer.idle();
      expect(getAccount(state.db, defaultAccount.id).expectedIdentity).toBe('me@example.com');
      expect(adapter.probes).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(adapter.probes[0]?.env['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR']).toBe(configDir ?? undefined);
      expect(ready).toBe(1);
      const cards = openCards();
      expect(cards.map((c) => c.title)).toEqual(['Claude plan runs as me@example.com']);
      expect(cards[0]?.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual([]);
      expect(cards[0]?.body).toContain(configDir === null ? 'claude /login' : configDir);
      expect(cards[0]?.detail?.[ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]).toMatchObject({
        accountId: defaultAccount.id,
        seenIdentity: 'me@example.com',
      });
    },
  );

  it('raises one sign-in card when the default directory is signed out, and probes again only after a minute', async () => {
    const { defaultAccount, projectId } = freshDefault();
    adapter.probeSeen = { ...adapter.probeSeen, email: undefined, subscriptionType: undefined };
    const row = getAccountRow(state.db, defaultAccount.id);
    preparer.prepare(row, projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    preparer.prepare(row, projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(adapter.probes).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(openCards().map((c) => c.body)).toEqual([expect.stringContaining('claude /login')]);
    clock.advance(PREPARE_RETRY_MS);
    preparer.prepare(row, projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(adapter.probes).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(openCards()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('asks the builder to confirm an account on its own directory, and Confirm records it', async () => {
    seedProject(store);
    const sub = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Company A' });
    const projectId = seedProject(store, { accountId: sub.id }).id;
    adapter.probeSeen = { ...adapter.probeSeen, email: 'a@company.example' };
    preparer.prepare(getAccountRow(state.db, sub.id), projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(getAccount(state.db, sub.id).expectedIdentity).toBeNull();
    const card = openCards()[0];
    expect(card?.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['Confirm', 'Later']);
    expect(card?.detail?.[ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]).toMatchObject({
      accountId: sub.id,
      problem: 'unconfirmed',
      seenIdentity: 'a@company.example',
    });
    expect(adapter.probes[0]?.env['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR']).toBe(sub.configDir);

    const confirmed: string[] = [];
    const answers = createAccountCardAnswers({
      resume: () => undefined,
      confirm: (_id, identity) => {
        confirmed.push(identity);
      },
    });
    if (!card) throw new Error('no confirm card');
    answers(card, { choice: AccountCardChoice.Confirm });
    expect(confirmed).toEqual(['a@company.example']);
  });

  it('probes no more while a Confirm card waits on the builder', async () => {
    seedProject(store);
    const sub = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Company A' });
    const projectId = seedProject(store, { accountId: sub.id }).id;
    const row = getAccountRow(state.db, sub.id);
    preparer.prepare(row, projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    clock.advance(PREPARE_RETRY_MS);
    preparer.prepare(row, projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(adapter.probes).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(openCards().map((c) => c.title)).toEqual(['Confirm the account Company A']);
  });

  it('asks for the secret of a keyed account that has none, without probing', async () => {
    seedProject(store);
    const key = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'anthropic_api_key', label: 'Client key' });
    const projectId = seedProject(store, { accountId: key.id }).id;
    preparer.prepare(getAccountRow(state.db, key.id), projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(adapter.probes).toEqual([]);
    expect(openCards()[0]?.body).toContain(`PUT /accounts/${key.id}/secret`);
  });

  it('asks the default account to sign in when its probe gets no account in time', async () => {
    const { defaultAccount, projectId } = freshDefault();
    adapter.probeAccount = () =>
      Promise.reject(new AccountProbeError('Claude Code reported no account within 15 s'));
    preparer.prepare(getAccountRow(state.db, defaultAccount.id), projectId);
    await preparer.idle();
    const cards = openCards();
    expect(cards.map((c) => c.title)).toEqual(['Sign in the account Claude plan']);
    expect(cards[0]?.body).toMatch(/reported no account within 15 s.*claude \/login/);
    expect(getAccount(state.db, defaultAccount.id).expectedIdentity).toBeNull();
  });

  it('does nothing for a ready account', async () => {
    // seedProject's default account is confirmed for builder@example.com.
    const project = seedProject(store);
    preparer.prepare(getAccountRow(state.db, project.accountId), project.id);
    await preparer.idle();
    expect(adapter.probes).toEqual([]);
    expect(openCards()).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write readiness, the start gate and the cards**

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/readiness.ts`:

```ts
import { AccountKind, AccountProblem, type AccountRefusal } from '@10db/contracts';
import type { AccountRow } from '@10db/db';

/** Why the account can't start a session yet, from its row alone; null when it may try. */
export function unready(account: AccountRow): AccountRefusal | null {
  if (account.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription && account.expectedIdentity === null)
    return {
      problem: AccountProblem.Unconfirmed,
      message: `Account "${account.label}" has no confirmed identity yet`,
      seenIdentity: null,
    };
  if (account.secretRef !== null && account.secretFingerprint === null)
    return {
      problem: AccountProblem.SecretMissing,
      message: `Account "${account.label}" has no secret set through 10db`,
      seenIdentity: null,
    };
  return null;
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/start-gate.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * One start at a time per key. On a subscription account, concurrent sessions may race an OAuth refresh
 * (Claude Code issues 54443 and 61923; spike 12's refresh check is unfinished), so its starts and probes
 * queue here until the previous one reports `started` or ends.
 */
export interface StartGate {
  /** Resolves with a release once every earlier holder of `key` has released; releasing twice is harmless. */
  acquire(key: string): Promise<() => void>;
  /** How many callers wait for `key` now, the holder excluded. */
  waiting(key: string): number;
}

export function createStartGate(): StartGate {
  const tails = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
  const queued = new Map<string, number>();
  return {
    acquire(key) {
      const before = tails.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve();
      let open: () => void = () => undefined;
      const mine = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        open = resolve;
      });
      const tail = before.then(() => mine);
      tails.set(key, tail);
      queued.set(key, (queued.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
      void tail.then(() => {
        if (tails.get(key) === tail) tails.delete(key);
      });
      return before.then(() => {
        queued.set(key, (queued.get(key) ?? 1) - 1);
        let released = false;
        return () => {
          if (released) return;
          released = true;
          open();
        };
      });
    },
    // Callers whose acquire has not resolved yet; the holder's already has.
    waiting: (key) => queued.get(key) ?? 0,
  };
}
```

In `apps/daemon/src/accounts/cards.ts`, add (import `signInCommand` is already there):

```ts
/** For an account on its own directory: the builder confirms the email Claude Code reported (spec §5). */
export function confirmCard(account: AccountRow, email: string): AccountCard {
  return {
    title: `Confirm the account ${account.label}`,
    body: `Claude Code in ${account.configDir ?? "Claude Code's default directory"} is signed in as ${email}. Confirm it, and ${account.label}'s sessions run only while they report this email.`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountProblem.Unconfirmed),
    detail: detail(account, null, AccountProblem.Unconfirmed, email),
    options: [
      { label: AccountCardChoice.Confirm, description: `Run ${account.label} as ${email}` },
      { label: AccountCardChoice.Later, description: 'Keep its work waiting' },
    ],
  };
}

/**
 * A subscription with no login. `why` is what the probe met when it got no answer at all: a signed-out
 * directory may report nothing, so that gets the sign-in command too.
 */
export function signInCard(account: AccountRow, why: string | null = null): AccountCard {
  const seen =
    why === null
      ? `Claude Code is not signed in for ${account.label}`
      : `${why}, so ${account.label} may be signed out`;
  return {
    title: `Sign in the account ${account.label}`,
    body: `${seen}. Its work waits; 10db tries again in a minute. Sign in with: ${signInCommand(account.configDir)}`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountProblem.SignedOut),
    detail: detail(account, null, AccountProblem.SignedOut, null),
  };
}

/**
 * The default account's identity is recorded from its first probe without a Confirm (it is the builder's current
 * login), so this card says which login that was. It holds no work.
 */
export function recordedCard(account: AccountRow, email: string): AccountCard {
  return {
    title: `${account.label} runs as ${email}`,
    body: `10db recorded ${email}, the login in ${account.configDir ?? "Claude Code's default directory"}, as ${account.label}'s identity, and runs its sessions only while they report it. If that is the wrong login, sign in as the right one with: ${signInCommand(account.configDir)}. The next session is then refused, and its card offers Confirm.`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, 'recorded'),
    detail: detail(account, null, null, email),
  };
}

export function secretCard(account: AccountRow): AccountCard {
  return {
    title: `Set the secret of ${account.label}`,
    body: `${account.label} has no secret yet, so its work waits. ${fixFor(account, AccountProblem.SecretMissing)}`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountProblem.SecretMissing),
    detail: detail(account, null, AccountProblem.SecretMissing, null),
  };
}
```

```ts
],
```
