- [ ] **Step 4: Write the preparer**

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/preparer.ts`:

```ts
import { AccountKind, AccountProblem, type AccountId, type ProjectId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { defaultAccountId, findOpenByFoldKey, patchAccount, type AccountRow } from '@10db/db';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import type { Launcher } from '../environment';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { InboxService } from '../inbox';
import type { Logger } from '../log';
import type { SessionAdapter, SeenAccount } from '../sessions';
import {
  accountFoldKey,
  confirmCard,
  recordedCard,
  secretCard,
  signInCard,
  type AccountCard,
} from './cards';
import { unready } from './readiness';
import type { StartGate } from './start-gate';

/** How long an unready account waits before it is probed again. */
export const PREPARE_RETRY_MS = 60_000;

export interface AccountPreparer {
  /** Works toward making an unready account ready: at most one probe a minute, and one card per problem. */
  prepare(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId): void;
  /** Settles once every probe started so far has finished (tests). */
  idle(): Promise<void>;
}

export function createAccountPreparer(opts: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly inbox: InboxService;
  readonly adapter: Pick<SessionAdapter, 'probeAccount'>;
  readonly launcher: Pick<Launcher, 'probe'>;
  readonly startGate: StartGate;
  readonly dataDir: string;
  readonly logger: Logger;
  /** An account became ready: the scheduler looks again. */
  onReady(): void;
}): AccountPreparer {
  const running = new Map<AccountId, Promise<void>>();
  const lastTried = new Map<AccountId, number>();

  const raise = (projectId: ProjectId, card: AccountCard): void => {
    opts.inbox.flag({
      projectId,
      workstreamId: null,
      title: card.title,
      body: card.body,
      detail: card.detail,
      foldKey: card.foldKey,
      ...(card.options ? { options: card.options } : {}),
    });
  };

  async function confirmIdentity(account: AccountRow, projectId: ProjectId): Promise<void> {
    let seen: SeenAccount;
    try {
      const release = await opts.startGate.acquire(account.id);
      try {
        seen = await opts.adapter.probeAccount({
          cwd: opts.dataDir,
          env: opts.launcher.probe(account).env,
        });
      } finally {
        release();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      // Probe errors name a timeout or a missing claude, never a credential. A signed-out directory
      // may answer nothing at all, so the card carries the sign-in command either way.
      raise(projectId, signInCard(account, error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
      return;
    }
    if (!seen.email || !seen.subscriptionType) {
      raise(projectId, signInCard(account));
      return;
    }
    if (defaultAccountId(opts.state.db) === account.id) {
      // The default account's directory holds the builder's current login (accounts spec §3); the card names it.
      const email = seen.email;
      opts.state.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, account.id, { expectedIdentity: email }));
      opts.logger.info({ accountId: account.id }, 'recorded the default account identity');
      raise(projectId, recordedCard(account, email));
      opts.onReady();
      return;
    }
    raise(projectId, confirmCard(account, seen.email));
  }

  return {
    prepare(account, projectId) {
      const problem = unready(account);
      if (!problem || running.has(account.id)) return;
      // A Confirm card already waits on the builder; probing again would only raise it again.
      const confirming = findOpenByFoldKey(
        opts.state.db,
        projectId,
        accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountProblem.Unconfirmed),
      );
      if (confirming) return;
      const now = opts.clock.now().getTime();
      const last = lastTried.get(account.id);
      if (last !== undefined && now - last < PREPARE_RETRY_MS) return;
      lastTried.set(account.id, now);
      if (
        problem.problem !== AccountProblem.Unconfirmed ||
        account.kind !== AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
      ) {
        raise(projectId, secretCard(account));
        return;
      }
      const job = confirmIdentity(account, projectId)
        .catch((error: unknown) => {
          opts.logger.error(
            { accountId: account.id, err: String(error) },
            'preparing the account failed',
          );
        })
        .finally(() => running.delete(account.id));
      running.set(account.id, job);
    },
    idle: async () => {
      await Promise.all([...running.values()]);
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/index.ts` adds `unready`, `createStartGate`, `type StartGate`, `createAccountPreparer`, `PREPARE_RETRY_MS`, `type AccountPreparer`.

```ts
case 'unready': {
  const lane = getProject(db, row.projectId);
  opts.state.write((ctx) => {
    // The state machine has no shorter way back to the queue; the next action is kept as it was.
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.PausedLimit,
      fence: ctl.fence,
      reason: `account not ready: ${outcome.refusal.problem}`,
    });
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.Queued,
      fence: ctl.fence,
      reason: 'waiting for the account to be ready',
    });
    clearLease(ctx, id);
  });
  // The scheduler holds it there until the preparer makes the account ready (accounts spec §5 "Signing in").
  opts.accounts.prepare(getAccountRow(db, lane.accountId), lane.id);
  return;
}
```

```ts
import type { ModelProfileDto } from '@10db/contracts';

/** The profiles an account offers: those whose alias it lists, or every one when it lists none (spec §3). */
export function offeredProfiles(
  profiles: readonly ModelProfileDto[],
  aliases: readonly string[] | undefined,
): ModelProfileDto[] {
  return aliases === undefined ? [...profiles] : profiles.filter((p) => aliases.includes(p.model));
}
```

exported from `routing/index.ts`. In `runner.ts`, `escalated` passes the same filtered list to `escalateStep`.

### Task 7: The guard keeps agents away from credentials

Practical isolation (accounts spec §3). In every step kind the guard refuses:

- Keychain commands;
- commands that print the environment;
- references to a credential variable;
- any tool or shell path under 10db's data directory, which holds every other account's config directory;
- the default account's own sign-in outside it: `~/.claude`, `~/.claude.json` and the login shell's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (accounts spec §1 success 5).

The rules sit beside the batch plan's `delivery` and `gh` rules.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/guard/account-rules.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/guard/judge.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/guard/paths.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/guard/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/guard/judge.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/mcp/tools.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/mcp/server.ts` (if `McpDeps` lives there), `apps/daemon/src/mcp/mcp.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`, `apps/daemon/test/engine.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 3's `SECRET_VARIABLES` and `Launcher.loginConfigDir()`; the guard's `splitCommand`, `stripCommandPrefixes`, `resolveThroughLinks`.
- Produces:
  - `interface ProtectedPath { path: string; refusal: string }`; `protectedPaths(opts: { dataDir: string; home: string; loginConfigDir: string | null }): ProtectedPath[]`: the data directory, `<home>/.claude`, `<home>/.claude.json` and the login shell's config directory when it sets one (exported from `guard/index.ts`).
  - `GuardInput.protectedPaths: readonly ProtectedPath[]` (required).
  - `guard/paths.ts`: `isWithin(dir: string, target: string): boolean` (the path itself counts, a file included; links are followed as a write would follow them).
  - `guard/account-rules.ts`: `keychainRefusal(words)`, `envDumpRefusal(written, command)`, `secretReferenceRefusal(raw)`, `mentionsPath(text, path)` (a mention ends at the end of the text, `/`, whitespace, `=`, `:`, a quote or a shell operator, so `~/.claude-work` is not `~/.claude`), `pathRefusal(value, repoPath, paths): string | null`, `segmentPathRefusal(raw, words, repoPath, paths): string | null`.
  - `RunnerOptions.protectedPaths` and `McpDeps.protectedPaths`: `readonly ProtectedPath[]`, built once in `startDaemon`.

```ts
  });

  it.each([
    ['env running a command', 'env FOO=1 git status'],
    ['set -e', 'set -e'],
    ['an export that sets', 'export FOO=1'],
    ['ps aux', 'ps aux'],
    ['searching code for a variable name', 'grep -rn ANTHROPIC_API_KEY src'],
    ['a harmless variable', 'echo $HOME'],
    ["the repo's own .claude directory", 'cat .claude/settings.json'],
    ["a sibling of the default account's directory", 'ls ~/.claude-other'],
    ['a sibling of the data directory', `ls "${data.path}-backup"`],
  ])('allows %s in an implement step', (_name, command) => {
    expect(run(command, false)).toEqual({ decision: 'allow' });
  });

  it.each<[string, Record<string, unknown>]>([
    ['Read', { file_path: join(CONFIG, '.claude.json') }],
    ['Read through a repo link', { file_path: 'link-data/accounts/acc_1/claude/.claude.json' }],
    ['Glob', { pattern: '**/*', path: '<data>' }],
    ['Grep', { pattern: 'token', path: '<data>' }],
    ['Write', { file_path: join(CONFIG, 'settings.json'), content: '{}' }],
    ["Read the default sign-in file", { file_path: join(homedir(), '.claude.json') }],
    ["Read in the login shell's config directory", { file_path: '<login>/.claude.json' }],
  ])('denies %s on a protected path in both step kinds', (toolName, input) => {
    const resolved = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(input).map(([k, v]) => [
        k,
        v === '<data>' ? data.path : typeof v === 'string' ? v.replace('<login>', login.path) : v,
      ]),
    );
    const tool = toolName.split(' ')[0] ?? toolName;
    for (const readOnly of [false, true])
      expect(judge(tool, resolved, [{ kind: 'area', name: 'api' }], readOnly)).toMatchObject({
        decision: 'deny',
        reason: expect.stringMatching(/data directory|sign-in/),
      });
  });

  it('still lets a tool read inside the repo', () => {
    expect(judge('Read', { file_path: 'src/api/a.ts' })).toEqual({ decision: 'allow' });
  });
});
```

In `apps/daemon/src/guard/paths.ts`, export a containment check beside `resolveInRepo`:

```ts
/** Whether `target` is `dir` itself (a file counts) or inside it, following links as a write would; a path that can't be read contains nothing. */
export function isWithin(dir: string, target: string): boolean {
  try {
    const root = fold(realpathSync.native(dir));
    const resolved = fold(resolveThroughLinks(resolve(target)));
    const rel = relative(root, resolved);
    return rel === '' || (rel !== '..' && !rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`) && !isAbsolute(rel));
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
```

(`fold` is the file's existing case folding; move its declaration above `isWithin` if needed.)

`apps/daemon/src/guard/account-rules.ts`:

```ts
import { realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import { homedir } from 'node:os';
import { basename, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { SECRET_VARIABLES } from '../environment';
import { isWithin } from './paths';

const KEYCHAIN = 'Keychain commands are never run by agents: 10db keeps account secrets there.';
const ENV_DUMP =
  'Printing the environment would show account credentials. Read the one variable you need, if it holds no credential.';
const secret = (name: string): string =>
  `${name} holds an account credential; agents never read it.`;

/** A path no agent may touch, and the reason the guard gives. */
export interface ProtectedPath {
  readonly path: string;
  readonly refusal: string;
}

/**
 * 10db's data directory, which holds every other account's config directory, and the default
 * account's own sign-in outside it: Claude Code's default directory and file, and the login shell's own directory.
 */
export function protectedPaths(opts: {
  readonly dataDir: string;
  readonly home: string;
  readonly loginConfigDir: string | null;
}): ProtectedPath[] {
  const login = (path: string): ProtectedPath => ({
    path,
    refusal: `${path} holds Claude Code's own sign-in, which the default account runs on; agents never read or write it.`,
  });
  return [
    {
      path: opts.dataDir,
      refusal: `${opts.dataDir} is 10db's data directory, which holds every account's sign-in; agents never read or write it.`,
    },
    login(join(opts.home, '.claude')),
    login(join(opts.home, '.claude.json')),
    ...(opts.loginConfigDir === null ? [] : [login(opts.loginConfigDir)]),
  ];
}

/** `security` in any form, and AppleScript that names the Keychain. */
export function keychainRefusal(command: readonly string[]): string | null {
  const [head = '', ...args] = command;
  if (head === 'security') return KEYCHAIN;
  if (head === 'osascript' && args.some((a) => /keychain|\bsecurity\b/i.test(a))) return KEYCHAIN;
  return null;
}

const isSecretName = (word: string): boolean => SECRET_VARIABLES.includes(word.split('=')[0] ?? '');

/**
 * Commands that print the environment, or one credential in it. `written` is the segment's words as
 * written (with `env` and assignments); `command` is what runs once those prefixes are stripped.
 */
export function envDumpRefusal(
  written: readonly string[],
  command: readonly string[],
): string | null {
  if (command.length === 0 && written.some((w) => basename(w) === 'env')) return ENV_DUMP;
  const [head = '', ...args] = command;
  const names = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith('-'));
  switch (head) {
    case 'printenv':
      return ENV_DUMP;
    case 'set':
      return args.length === 0 ? ENV_DUMP : null;
    case 'export':
    case 'declare':
    case 'typeset':
    case 'readonly': {
      const credential = names.find(isSecretName);
      if (credential) return secret(credential.split('=')[0] ?? credential);
      return names.length === 0 ? ENV_DUMP : null;
    }
    case 'compgen':
      return args.some((a) => /^-[a-zA-Z]*[ev]/.test(a)) ? ENV_DUMP : null;
    case 'ps':
      // macOS ps prints each process's environment with -e or -E, or `e` in a BSD-style word.
      return args.some((a) => (a.startsWith('-') ? /^-[a-zA-Z]*[eE]/.test(a) : /e/.test(a)))
        ? ENV_DUMP
        : null;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** `$NAME` or `${NAME…}` of a credential variable anywhere in the segment. */
export function secretReferenceRefusal(raw: string): string | null {
  const name = SECRET_VARIABLES.find((n) => new RegExp(`\\$\\{?${n}(?![A-Za-z0-9_])`).test(raw));
  return name ? secret(name) : null;
}

function expandHome(text: string): string {
  const home = homedir();
  return text
    .replaceAll('${HOME}', home)
    .replaceAll('$HOME', home)
    .replace(/(^|[\s=:])~(?=\/|$)/gm, `$1${home}`);
}

/** The path as given and as the file system resolves it (a temp directory is often a link). */
function forms(path: string): string[] {
  try {
    return [path, realpathSync.native(path)];
  } catch {
    return [path];
  }
}

/**
 * What may follow a path where the text names it: the end, a deeper path, whitespace, an assignment or
 * option separator, a quote or a shell operator. A sibling such as `~/.claude-work` or `…/10db-backup`
 * goes on with another character, so it is not the path.
 */
const PATH_END = /^(?:$|[\s/=:'"`;|&)<>])/;

/** Whether text names the path however it is quoted or escaped: `\ `, quotes, `~` or `$HOME`. */
export function mentionsPath(text: string, path: string): boolean {
  const plain = expandHome(text.replaceAll('\\ ', ' ').replace(/["']/g, '')).toLowerCase();
  return forms(path).some((form) => {
    const needle = form.toLowerCase();
    for (let at = plain.indexOf(needle); at !== -1; at = plain.indexOf(needle, at + 1))
      if (PATH_END.test(plain.slice(at + needle.length))) return true;
    return false;
  });
}

/** The refusal for a path, or a word that may be one, landing on a protected path from the repo. */
export function pathRefusal(
  value: string,
  repoPath: string,
  paths: readonly ProtectedPath[],
): string | null {
  if (value.startsWith('-')) return null;
  const target = resolve(repoPath, expandHome(value));
  return (
    paths.find((p) => isWithin(p.path, target) || mentionsPath(value, p.path))?.refusal ?? null
  );
}

/** A shell segment naming a protected path anywhere in its text, or in a word that resolves onto one. */
export function segmentPathRefusal(
  raw: string,
  words: readonly string[],
  repoPath: string,
  paths: readonly ProtectedPath[],
): string | null {
  const named = paths.find((p) => mentionsPath(raw, p.path));
  if (named) return named.refusal;
  for (const word of words) {
    const refusal = pathRefusal(word, repoPath, paths);
    if (refusal) return refusal;
  }
  return null;
}
```

In `apps/daemon/src/guard/judge.ts`:

```ts
const accountRule =
  keychainRefusal(words) ??
  envDumpRefusal(segment.words, words) ??
  secretReferenceRefusal(segment.raw) ??
  segmentPathRefusal(segment.raw, segment.words, input.repoPath, input.protectedPaths);
if (accountRule) return deny(accountRule);
```

```ts
// Every tool, in every step kind: no path under the data directory or the default account's sign-in (accounts spec §3).

  const value = stringField(input.toolInput, field);
  const refusal = value === null ? null : pathRefusal(value, input.repoPath, input.protectedPaths);

}
```

### Task 8: The account service and its HTTP routes

The routes of accounts spec §7, behind the UI token:

- create, change and archive accounts;
- set a secret;
- read the sign-in command, a live identity probe and a month's usage;
- move a project to another account.

A secret goes in through `PUT` and never comes back out, in any response, event, card or log line.

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/service.ts`; Modify: `apps/daemon/src/accounts/index.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/api/services.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/api/app.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/api/app.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/daemon/services.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`
- Create: `apps/daemon/test/accounts-api.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `insertAccount`, `patchAccount`, `archiveAccount`, `recordAccountSecret`, `listAccounts`, `getAccount`, `getAccountRow`, `accountUsage`, `patchProject` (`accountId`); Task 3's `Keychain`, `KeychainError`, `secretFingerprint`, `signInCommand`, `Launcher.probe`, `AccountLaunchError`; Task 4's `monthOf`, `monthWindow`, `usageWindow`, `AccountRuntime.saved`; Task 5's `checkAccount`, `SessionAdapter.probeAccount`; Task 6's `StartGate`.
- Produces:
  - `interface AccountService { list(): AccountDto[]; create(input: AccountConfig): AccountCreatedDto; patch(id, patch: AccountPatch): AccountDto; archive(id): AccountDto; setSecret(id, input: AccountSecretInput): AccountDto; signIn(id): AccountSignInDto; identity(id): Promise<AccountIdentityDto>; usage(id, month: string | null): AccountUsageDto }`, `createAccountService(deps)`.
  - `ApiServices` gains `listAccounts`, `createAccount`, `patchAccount`, `archiveAccount`, `setAccountSecret`, `accountSignIn`, `accountIdentity`, `accountUsage` (the same signatures with `AccountId` arguments).
  - Routes: `GET /accounts`, `POST /accounts` (201, `AccountCreatedDto`), `PATCH /accounts/:accountId`, `POST /accounts/:accountId/archive`, `PUT /accounts/:accountId/secret`, `GET /accounts/:accountId/signin`, `GET /accounts/:accountId/identity`, `GET /accounts/:accountId/usage?month=YYYY-MM`. `PATCH /projects/:projectId` already takes `accountId` (Task 2).

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import {
  AccountCreatedDto,
  AccountDto,
  AccountIdentityDto,
  AccountSignInDto,
  AccountUsageDto,
  ProjectDto,
  TaskDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { startDaemon, type RunningDaemon } from '../src/daemon';
import { KeychainError } from '../src/environment';
import { FakeAdapter } from '../src/sessions';
import { apiClient } from './api-client';
import { createMemoryKeychain, createTempRepo, tempDir } from './fixtures';

const SECRET = 'sk-or-v1-0123456789abcdef-NEVER-SHOWN';
const QUIET = { caffeinate: () => null, watch: false } as const;

let data: { path: string; cleanup(): void };
let daemon: RunningDaemon | null;
beforeEach(() => {
  data = tempDir('accounts api');
  daemon = null;
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await daemon?.stop();
  data.cleanup();
});

async function start(keychain = createMemoryKeychain()) {
  daemon = await startDaemon({
    dataDir: data.path,
    port: 0,
    adapter: new FakeAdapter({}),
    logLevel: 'debug',
    keychain,
    ...QUIET,
  });
  return {
    api: apiClient(daemon.url, daemon.token),
    keychain,
    url: daemon.url,
    token: daemon.token,
  };
}

describe('accounts over HTTP', () => {
  it('takes a secret into the Keychain and never returns, emits or logs it', async () => {
    const { api, keychain } = await start();
    const { account, signIn } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
      kind: 'gateway',
      label: 'OpenRouter',
      monthlyCapUsd: 100,
      settings: { baseUrl: 'https://openrouter.ai/api', aliases: ['haiku'] },
    });
    expect(signIn).toBeNull();
    expect(account.secretSet).toBe(false);
    const stored = await api.put(`/accounts/${account.id}/secret`, AccountDto, { secret: SECRET });
    expect(stored.secretSet).toBe(true);
    expect(keychain.read(`account:${account.id}`)).toBe(SECRET);

    const seen = [
      JSON.stringify(stored),
      JSON.stringify(await api.get('/accounts', z.array(AccountDto))),
      JSON.stringify(await api.patch(`/accounts/${account.id}`, AccountDto, { payer: 'Client B' })),
      JSON.stringify(await api.get(`/accounts/${account.id}/identity`, AccountIdentityDto)),
      JSON.stringify(await api.get(`/accounts/${account.id}/usage`, AccountUsageDto)),
      JSON.stringify(await api.get('/events?after=0&limit=1000', z.unknown())),
      JSON.stringify(await api.get('/inbox', z.unknown())),
      readFileSync(join(data.path, 'logs', 'daemon.log'), 'utf8'),
    ];
    for (const text of seen) expect(text).not.toContain('NEVER-SHOWN');
  });

  it('creates a subscription with a private config directory and a sign-in command quoting its path', async () => {
    const { api } = await start();
    const { account, signIn } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
      kind: 'claude_subscription',
      label: 'Company A',
    });
    const configDir = join(data.path, 'accounts', account.id, 'claude');
    expect(account.configDir).toBe(configDir);
    expect(statSync(configDir).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o700);
    expect(statSync(join(data.path, 'accounts')).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o700);
    expect(signIn?.command).toBe(`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR='${configDir}' claude /login`);
    expect(await api.get(`/accounts/${account.id}/signin`, AccountSignInDto)).toEqual(signIn);
  });

  it('gives a keyed account its own empty config directory, and no sign-in command', async () => {
    const { api } = await start();
    const { account, signIn } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      label: 'Client key',
    });
    const configDir = join(data.path, 'accounts', account.id, 'claude');
    expect(account.configDir).toBe(configDir);
    expect(statSync(configDir).mode & 0o777).toBe(0o700);
    expect(readdirSync(configDir)).toEqual([]);
    expect(signIn).toBeNull();
  });

  it('answers 409 when the Keychain refuses the secret, and records nothing', async () => {
    const { api } = await start({
      read: () => null,
      write: (account) => {
        throw new KeychainError(
          `Writing the Keychain item ${account} failed (security exit status 36)`,
        );
      },
    });
    const { account } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      label: 'Client key',
    });
    await expect(
      api.put(`/accounts/${account.id}/secret`, AccountDto, { secret: SECRET }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/409/);
    expect(
      (await api.get('/accounts', z.array(AccountDto))).find((a) => a.id === account.id)?.secretSet,
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it.each<[string, string, string, unknown]>([
    ['a reserved kind', 'POST', '/accounts', { kind: 'openai_api_key', label: 'x' }],
    ['a short secret', 'PUT', '/accounts/{id}/secret', { secret: 'short' }],
    ['a malformed month', 'GET', '/accounts/{id}/usage?month=2026-13', undefined],
    ['sign-in for a keyed account', 'GET', '/accounts/{id}/signin', undefined],
  ])('answers 400 for %s', async (_name, method, path, body) => {
    const { api, url, token } = await start();
    const { account } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      label: 'K',
    });
    const res = await fetch(`${url}${path.replace('{id}', account.id)}`, {
      method,
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        ...(body === undefined ? {} : { 'content-type': 'application/json' }),
      },
      ...(body === undefined ? {} : { body: JSON.stringify(body) }),
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  it('moves a project to another account, with work in flight too', async () => {
    const repo = createTempRepo();
    try {
      const { api } = await start();
      const project = await api.post('/projects', ProjectDto, {
        name: 'demo',
        repoPath: repo.path,
        gateCommand: 'true',
        areas: [{ name: 'src', globs: ['src/**'] }],
      });
      const accounts = await api.get('/accounts', z.array(AccountDto));
      expect(accounts.map((a) => [a.label, a.isDefault])).toEqual([['Claude plan', true]]);
      expect(project.accountId).toBe(accounts[0]?.id);
      const { account } = await api.post('/accounts', AccountCreatedDto, {
        kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
        label: 'K',
      });
      const moved = await api.patch(`/projects/${project.id}`, ProjectDto, {
        accountId: account.id,
      });
      expect(moved.accountId).toBe(account.id);
      await api.post(`/projects/${project.id}/tasks`, TaskDto, {
        title: 'T',
        acceptanceCriteria: ['x'],
        areas: ['src'],
      });
      const task = (await api.get(`/projects/${project.id}/tasks`, z.array(TaskDto)))[0];
      if (!task) throw new Error('no task');
      await api.post(`/tasks/${task.id}/start`, z.unknown());
      const defaultId = accounts[0]?.id ?? '';
      // The task waits on the keyed account, which has no secret; the move takes it along.
      const back = await api.patch(`/projects/${project.id}`, ProjectDto, { accountId: defaultId });
      expect(back.accountId).toBe(defaultId);
    } finally {
      repo.cleanup();
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
} from '@10db/db';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import {
  AccountLaunchError,
  KeychainError,
  secretFingerprint,
  signInCommand,

  type Launcher,

import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import { checkAccount, type SeenAccount, type SessionAdapter } from '../sessions';
import { monthOf, monthWindow, usageWindow } from './month';
import type { AccountRuntime } from './runtime';
import type { StartGate } from './start-gate';

export interface AccountService {
  list(): AccountDto[];
  create(input: AccountConfig): AccountCreatedDto;
  patch(id: AccountId, patch: AccountPatch): AccountDto;
  archive(id: AccountId): AccountDto;
  setSecret(id: AccountId, input: AccountSecretInput): AccountDto;
  signIn(id: AccountId): AccountSignInDto;
  identity(id: AccountId): Promise<AccountIdentityDto>;
  usage(id: AccountId, month: string | null): AccountUsageDto;
}

/** The directory and every level 10db made above it: readable by the builder only (accounts spec §4). */
function makePrivate(root: string, dir: string): void {
  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  // mkdir's mode passes through the umask, so each level is set explicitly.
  for (const path of [root, dirname(dir), dir]) chmodSync(path, 0o700);
}

const nothingSeen = (id: AccountId): Omit<AccountIdentityDto, 'refusal'> => ({
  accountId: id,
  email: null,
  organization: null,
  apiProvider: null,
  apiKeySource: null,
  tokenSource: null,
  subscription: false,
});

export function createAccountService(deps: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly dataDir: string;
  readonly keychain: Keychain;
  readonly launcher: Pick<Launcher, 'probe'>;
  readonly adapter: Pick<SessionAdapter, 'probeAccount'>;
  readonly startGate: StartGate;
  readonly runtime: Pick<AccountRuntime, 'saved'>;
  /** An account changed: the scheduler looks again. */
  onSaved(): void;
}): AccountService {
  const db = deps.state.db;
  const root = join(deps.dataDir, 'accounts');
  const saved = (id: AccountId): void => {
    deps.runtime.saved(id);
    deps.onSaved();
  };
  const signInOf = (account: AccountDto): AccountSignInDto | null =>
    account.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
      ? {
          accountId: account.id,
          configDir: account.configDir,
          command: signInCommand(account.configDir),
        }
      : null;

  return {
    list: () => listAccounts(db, { includeArchived: true }),
    create(input) {
      const account = deps.state.write((ctx) => {
        const created = insertAccount(ctx, input, {
          configDirFor: (id) => join(root, id, 'claude'),
        });
        // Every created account, keyed kinds too, gets one. Inside the transaction: a directory that
        // can't be made leaves no row behind.
        if (created.configDir !== null) makePrivate(root, created.configDir);
        return created;
      });
      return { account, signIn: signInOf(account) };
    },
    patch(id, patch) {
      const account = deps.state.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, id, patch));
      saved(id);
      return account;
    },
    archive: (id) => deps.state.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, id)),
    setSecret(id, input) {
      const { secret } = AccountSecretInput.parse(input);
      const row = getAccountRow(db, id);
      if (row.archived) throw new ConflictError(`Account "${row.label}" is archived`);
      if (row.secretRef === null) throw new InvalidError(`Account "${row.label}" takes no secret`);
      try {
        deps.keychain.write(row.secretRef, secret);
      } catch (error) {
        // Keychain messages name the item and the status, never the value.
        if (error instanceof KeychainError) throw new ConflictError(error.message);
        throw error;
      }
      const account = deps.state.write((ctx) =>
        recordAccountSecret(ctx, id, secretFingerprint(secret)),
      );
      saved(id);
      return account;
    },
    signIn(id) {
      const signIn = signInOf(getAccount(db, id));
      if (!signIn)
        throw new InvalidError('Only a Claude subscription account signs in with claude /login');
      return signIn;
    },
    async identity(id) {
      const row = getAccountRow(db, id);
      let launch;
      try {
        launch = deps.launcher.probe(row);
      } catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof AccountLaunchError)) throw error;
        return {
          ...nothingSeen(id),
          refusal: { problem: error.problem, message: error.message, seenIdentity: null },
        };
      }
      let seen: SeenAccount;
      try {
        const release =
          row.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
            ? await deps.startGate.acquire(id)
            : () => undefined;
        try {
          seen = await deps.adapter.probeAccount({ cwd: deps.dataDir, env: launch.env });
        } finally {
          release();
        }
      } catch (error) {
        throw new ConflictError(
          `Couldn't read the account ${row.label}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
        );
      }
      const unconfirmed =
        row.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription && row.expectedIdentity === null
          ? {
              problem: AccountProblem.Unconfirmed,
              message: `Account "${row.label}" has no confirmed identity yet`,
              seenIdentity: seen.email ?? null,
            }
          : null;
      return {
        accountId: id,
        email: seen.email ?? null,
        organization: seen.organization ?? null,
        apiProvider: seen.apiProvider ?? null,
        apiKeySource: seen.apiKeySource ?? null,
        tokenSource: seen.tokenSource ?? null,
        subscription: Boolean(seen.subscriptionType),
        refusal: checkAccount(launch.expect, seen) ?? unconfirmed,
      };
    },
    usage(id, month) {
      getAccountRow(db, id);
      const window = month === null ? monthOf(deps.clock.now()) : monthWindow(month);
      return accountUsage(db, id, usageWindow(window));
    },
  };
}
```

Export `createAccountService` and `type AccountService` from `accounts/index.ts`.

```ts
    .post(
```

### Task 9: Lanes follow their project to another account

From Task 2 on, `PATCH /projects/:id` moves a project whatever work it has. This task makes the work follow (decisions 25 and 26):

- A running session finishes on the account it started on. The runner reads the project's account before each session of a lane, so the move applies at the lane's next session, and `insertSession` records the account the session really launched on.
- Claude Code keeps a conversation in its account's config directory, so a lane whose next action would resume one from another account starts a fresh session instead. The decision reuses the crash path's: a `resume` becomes what a failed resume becomes, from the step's latest handoff or with "inspect git status first". A gate failure the lane was fixing comes back as `fix`, and an `answer` reaches the fresh session with the handoff before it.

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/next-action.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/next-action.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/index.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/briefing/briefing.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/db/src/store/sessions.ts`, `packages/db/src/store/accounts.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `insertSession`, `patchProject` (`accountId`, no refusal), `seedAccount`, `patchAccount`; Task 4's `Control.accountId`, the `blocked` check in `run` and the runner's `held` outcome; Task 6's `unready` outcome; the runner's `nextActionAfterCrash`, `latestHandoffForStep`, `getSessionByProviderId`, `listEvidence`.
- Produces:
  - `nextActionOnAnotherAccount(previous: NextAction, latest: HandoffDto | null, failedGate: EvidenceId | null): NextAction` (exported from `runner/index.ts`).
  - `NewSession.accountId?: AccountId`: the account the session launches on; absent, the project's current account.
  - `Control.accountId` is mutable: the account of the lane's current session, or of its next one, read from the project before each session.
  - A fresh `answer` session (its `providerSessionId` null) is briefed from the step's latest handoff before the answer, and counts as briefed.

In `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.test.ts` (import `seedAccount`, `patchAccount`, `patchProject`, `transitionWorkstream` from `@10db/db` and `type AccountId` from `@10db/contracts`), add:

```ts
describe('moving accounts', () => {
  /** A second subscription, confirmed, so a session on it is ready and reports what it expects. */
  const secondPlan = () => {
    const account = seedAccount(engine.store, {
      kind: 'claude_subscription',
      label: 'Second plan',
    });
    engine.state.write((ctx) =>
      patchAccount(ctx, account.id, { expectedIdentity: 'second@example.com' }),
    );
    return account;
  };
  const moveTo = (accountId: AccountId) =>
    engine.state.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, engine.project.id, { accountId }));
  /** What the scheduler does once the new account isn't paused. */
  const requeue = (id: Parameters<typeof getWorkstream>[1]) =>
    engine.state.write((ctx) => transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, { to: 'QUEUED', fence: null }));
  const accountsOf = (id: Parameters<typeof getWorkstream>[1]) =>
    listSessions(engine.store.db, id).map((s) => s.accountId);

  it('starts a fresh session from the handoff when the project moved off the account its conversation ran on', async () => {
    engine = await createTestEngine({
      Alpha: [
        [
          {
            do: 'mcp',
            tool: 'write_handoff',
            args: handoffArgs('op-move-0001', 'Write the second file'),
          },
          { do: 'rate_limit', status: 'rejected', resetsInSeconds: 600 },
        ],
        [write('src/api/b.txt'), commit('op-move-0002', ['src/api/b.txt']), turn],
      ],
    });
    const { workstream } = engine.addTask('Alpha');
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id)).toMatchObject({
      state: 'PAUSED_LIMIT',
      nextAction: { kind: 'resume' },
    });

    const second = secondPlan();
    moveTo(second.id);
    requeue(workstream.id);
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).state).toBe('DONE');
    expect(accountsOf(workstream.id)).toEqual([engine.project.accountId, second.id]);
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[1]).toMatchObject({ resume: null });
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[1]?.prompt).toContain('Next action: Write the second file');
  });

  it("brings the builder's answer to a fresh session after the project moved", async () => {
    engine = await createTestEngine({
      Alpha: [
        [askShip, turn],
        [write('src/api/a.txt'), commit('op-moveask-01', ['src/api/a.txt']), turn],
      ],
    });
    const { workstream } = engine.addTask('Alpha');
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    const card = await waitFor(openCard);
    engine.clock.advance(10 * 60_000);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).nextAction).toMatchObject({ kind: 'answer', inboxId: card.id });

    moveTo(secondPlan().id);
    engine.inbox.answer(card.id, { choice: 'Yes' });
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).state).toBe('DONE');
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[1]).toMatchObject({ resume: null });
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[1]?.prompt).toContain('Answer: Yes');
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[1]?.prompt).toContain('inspect git status first');
  });

  it('keeps the gate failure a lane was fixing when the project moved', async () => {
    engine = await createTestEngine(
      {
        Alpha: [
          [turn],
          [{ do: 'rate_limit', status: 'rejected', resetsInSeconds: 600 }],
          [write('src/api/fixed.txt'), commit('op-movefix-01', ['src/api/fixed.txt']), turn],
        ],
      },
      { gateCommand: 'test -f src/api/fixed.txt || { echo MISSING-FIXED-FILE; exit 2; }' },
    );
    const { workstream } = engine.addTask('Alpha');
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).nextAction).toMatchObject({ kind: 'fix' });
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id)).toMatchObject({
      state: 'PAUSED_LIMIT',
      nextAction: { kind: 'resume' },
    });

    moveTo(secondPlan().id);
    requeue(workstream.id);
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).state).toBe('DONE');
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[2]).toMatchObject({ resume: null });
    expect(engine.adapter.prompts[2]?.prompt).toContain('MISSING-FIXED-FILE');
  });

  it("runs a lane's next session on the account its project moved to while the first one ran", async () => {
    const moved: { to: AccountId | null } = { to: null };
    engine = await createTestEngine(
      {
        Alpha: [
          [
            { do: 'context', percentage: 75 },
            { do: 'await_message', contains: 'write_handoff' },
            {
              do: 'mcp',
              tool: 'write_handoff',
              args: handoffArgs('op-movelane-1', 'Write the second file'),
            },
            turn,
          ],
          [write('src/api/b.txt'), commit('op-movelane-2', ['src/api/b.txt']), turn],
        ],
      },
      {},
      {
        // The builder moves the project while the first session runs; it ends on the old account.
        afterSession: () => {
          if (moved.to === null) {
            moved.to = secondPlan().id;
            moveTo(moved.to);
          }
          return Promise.resolve();
        },
      },
    );
    const { workstream } = engine.addTask('Alpha');
    engine.launch(workstream.id);
    await engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(workstream.id).state).toBe('DONE');
    expect(accountsOf(workstream.id)).toEqual([engine.project.accountId, moved.to]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `pnpm --filter @10db/daemon exec vitest run src/runner src/briefing && pnpm --filter @10db/db exec vitest run src/store/accounts.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL.

- `next-action.test.ts` can't import `nextActionOnAnotherAccount`.
- The briefing case finds no handoff in the fresh answer's prompt.
- The db case's session is recorded as the project's subscription, so `costUsd` is null, not 0.
- In the runner suite, the first three cases resume the old conversation: `prompts[n].resume` is the first session's provider ID, not null. The fourth records both sessions on the default account, because `ctl.accountId` was read once at launch.

- [ ] **Step 3: Decide the next action on another account**

In `apps/daemon/src/runner/next-action.ts` (import `type EvidenceId` beside the others):

```ts
/**
 * Before a session on the project's account, when the conversation the lane would resume ran on
 * another one: Claude Code keeps a conversation in its account's config directory, so the lane
 * starts fresh as after a failed resume, from the step's latest handoff. The builder's answer still
 * reaches the fresh session, and so does a gate failure the lane was fixing.
 */
export function nextActionOnAnotherAccount(
  previous: NextAction,
  latest: HandoffDto | null,
  failedGate: EvidenceId | null,
): NextAction {
  switch (previous.kind) {
    case 'answer':
      return { ...previous, providerSessionId: null };
    case 'resume':
      return failedGate
        ? { kind: 'fix', evidenceId: failedGate }
        : nextActionAfterCrash(previous, null, latest);
    case 'start':
    case 'continue_from_handoff':
    case 'fix':
    case 'verify':
    case 'revise':
      return previous;
  }
}
```

Export it from `apps/daemon/src/runner/index.ts` beside `nextActionAfterCrash`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Brief a fresh answer session from the handoff**

In `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, `actionSection`'s `answer` case becomes:

```ts
case 'answer': {
  const answer = input.answer
    ? `The builder answered your question.\nQuestion: ${input.answer.question}\nAnswer: ${input.answer.answer}${input.answer.note ? `\nNote: ${input.answer.note}` : ''}\nContinue.`
    : 'The builder answered your question; continue.';
  if (input.action.providerSessionId !== null) return answer;
  // No conversation to resume: the session starts from the handoff, as a continuation would.
  const brief = input.handoff
    ? `Continue from the handoff below. Do not redo finished work.\n\nHandoff:\n${renderHandoff(input.handoff)}`
    : 'The previous session left no handoff, so inspect git status first.';
  return `${brief}\n\n${answer}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Record the account a session launches on**

In `packages/db/src/store/sessions.ts`, `NewSession` gains `/** The account the session launches on; absent, the project's current account. */ readonly accountId?: AccountId;`. Task 2's owner lookup in `insertSession` uses it when it is given, and the project's account otherwise:

```ts
// A project can move while a session waits for its start, so the runner names the account it launched on.
const owner =
  input.accountId === undefined
    ? projectOwner(ctx, input.workstreamId)
    : { accountId: input.accountId, kind: getAccountRow(ctx.tx, input.accountId).kind };
```

where `projectOwner` is Task 2's join (workstream, project, account) moved into a function that throws `NotFoundError('workstream', …)` as before. The row's `accountId` comes from `owner` after `...input`, so the spread can't put back an `undefined`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Read the account before each session, and start fresh across accounts**

In `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`:

- `Control.accountId` loses `readonly`, with the comment `/** The account of the lane's current session, or of its next one: read from the project before each session, so a move applies at the lane's next session. */`.
- In `run`, where Task 4 checks `blocked` before each session, take the project's account first:

```ts
const lane = getProject(db, getWorkstreamRow(db, id).projectId);
// A running session finished on the account it started on; the next one runs where the project is now.
ctl.accountId = lane.accountId;
const block = opts.accounts.blocked(getAccountRow(db, ctl.accountId), lane.id);
```

- Add, beside `freshAction`:

```ts
/**
 * A conversation lives in the config directory of the account its session ran on, so once the
 * project has moved the lane can't resume it: its next action becomes a fresh start on the
 * project's account (accounts spec §6).
 */
function leaveOtherAccount(id: WorkstreamId, ctl: Control): void {
  const row = getWorkstreamRow(db, id);
  const action = row.nextAction;
  const resumeId =
    action.kind === 'resume' || action.kind === 'answer' ? action.providerSessionId : null;
  if (resumeId === null) return;
  const ranOn = getSessionByProviderId(db, id, resumeId)?.accountId;
  if (ranOn === undefined || ranOn === ctl.accountId) return;
  // The lane's latest gate run failed and none has passed since: it was fixing that failure.
  const lastGate = listEvidence(db, id)
    .filter((e) => e.kind === EvidenceKind.Gate)
    .at(-1);
  const nextAction = nextActionOnAnotherAccount(
    action,
    latestHandoffForStep(db, row),
    lastGate && lastGate.exitCode !== 0 ? lastGate.id : null,
  );
  opts.state.write((ctx) => {
    patchWorkstream(ctx, id, ctl.fence, { nextAction });
  });
  opts.logger.info(
    { workstreamId: id, from: ranOn, to: ctl.accountId },
    'the project moved accounts; the lane starts a fresh session',
  );
}
```

  (import `nextActionOnAnotherAccount` from `./next-action`, `EvidenceKind` from `@10db/contracts`).
- `runSession` calls `leaveOtherAccount(id, ctl);` as its first line, before it reads the row.
- `insertSession` gets `accountId: ctl.accountId`.
- A fresh answer session is briefed when its prompt carries the handoff:

```ts
const briefed = resumeId
  ? (resumed?.briefed ?? false)
  : contextPack.handoff !== null &&
    (action.kind === 'continue_from_handoff' || action.kind === 'answer');
```

  (`resumeId` is null here, so an `answer` has no conversation to resume.)

### Task 10: Move a project from its usage-limit card, and offer the move back

The usage-limit card offers "Continue on <account>" for each account the project could move to now, plus Later (decision 27). `PATCH /projects/:id` and the card both go through one move operation, `AccountMoves.move`. A move off an account because of its usage limit is remembered on the project (`moved_from_account_id`, `move_back_at`). When that limit resets, one card per project asks whether to move it back, and nothing moves until the builder answers (decision 28).

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/moves.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountCardChoice,
  InvalidError,
  type AccountDto,
  type InboxAnswer,
  type InboxItemDto,
  type ProjectDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  createTestStore,
  getProject,
  listInbox,
  seedAccount,
  seedProject,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { createEventBus, createStateStore, type StateStore } from '../events';
import { createInboxService, type InboxService } from '../inbox';
import {
  createAccountCardAnswers,
  createAccountMoves,
  createAccountPauses,
  createAccountRuntime,
  createMoveBack,
  MoveCause,
  type AccountMoves,
  type AccountPauses,
  type AccountRuntime,
  type MoveBack,
} from './index';

const DEFAULT_PAUSE_MS = 30 * 60_000;

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let state: StateStore;
let clock: ManualClock;
let inbox: InboxService;
let pauses: AccountPauses;
let runtime: AccountRuntime;
let moveBack: MoveBack;
let moves: AccountMoves;
let answerCard: (item: InboxItemDto, answer: InboxAnswer) => void;
let ticks: number;
let project: ProjectDto;
let second: AccountDto;

/** The daemon's account runtime over the store; a restart builds it again. */
function build(): void {
  pauses = createAccountPauses({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    stopAccount: () => Promise.resolve(),
    onResume: () => undefined,
  });
  runtime = createAccountRuntime({ state, clock, inbox, pauses, defaultPauseMs: DEFAULT_PAUSE_MS });
  moveBack = createMoveBack({ state, clock, inbox, pauses });
  moves = createAccountMoves({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    pauses,
    moveBack,
    defaultPauseMs: DEFAULT_PAUSE_MS,
    onMoved: () => {
      ticks += 1;
    },
  });
  answerCard = createAccountCardAnswers({
    resume: () => undefined,
    confirm: () => undefined,
    move: (projectId, to, why, answering) => {
      moves.move(projectId, to, why, { answering });
    },
    stay: (projectId) => {
      moves.stay(projectId);
    },
  });
}

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new ManualClock(new Date(2026, 9, 20, 12));
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => clock.now()));
  state = createStateStore(store, createEventBus());
  // As in the daemon: a card's answer acts before the inbox records it.
  inbox = createInboxService({
    state,
    onParkedAnswer: () => undefined,
    onFlagAnswer: (item, answer) => {
      answerCard(item, answer);
    },
  });
  ticks = 0;
  build();
  project = seedProject(store);
  second = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Second plan' });
});
afterEach(() => {
  moveBack.stop();
  pauses.stop();
  close();
});

const inMinutes = (n: number) => new Date(clock.now().getTime() + n * 60_000);
const openCards = () => listInbox(state.db, { states: ['OPEN'] });
function card(title: string): InboxItemDto {
  const found = openCards().find((c) => c.title === title);
  if (!found) throw new Error(`no open card "${title}"`);
  return found;
}
const LIMIT = 'Claude plan usage limit reached';
const BACK = 'Claude plan is available again: move demo back?';

describe('moving a project', () => {
  it('offers to continue on each account the project could move to, and moves it when one is chosen', () => {
    const paused = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Paused plan' });
    pauses.pause(
      paused.id,
      { reason: 'refused', until: null },
      { projectId: project.id, card: { title: 'refused', body: 'b', foldKey: 'k', detail: {} } },
    );
    runtime.rateLimited(project.accountId, inMinutes(60), project.id);
    const limit = card(LIMIT);
    expect(limit.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['Continue on Second plan', 'Later']);

    inbox.answer(limit.id, { choice: 'Continue on Second plan' });
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: second.id,
      movedFromAccountId: project.accountId,
      moveBackAt: inMinutes(60).toISOString(),
    });
    expect(ticks).toBe(1);
  });

  it('asks once the limit resets whether to move back, and moves back on Move back', () => {
    const plan = project.accountId;
    runtime.rateLimited(plan, inMinutes(60), project.id);
    // A move by PATCH while the account is paused for its usage limit is remembered too.
    moves.move(project.id, second.id, { cause: MoveCause.Builder });
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id).movedFromAccountId).toBe(plan);
    // The usage-limit card offered a move the builder has made another way.
    expect(openCards()).toEqual([]);

    clock.advance(60 * 60_000);
    const back = card(BACK);
    expect(back.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['Move back', 'Stay']);
    inbox.answer(back.id, { choice: AccountCardChoice.MoveBack });
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: plan,
      movedFromAccountId: null,
      moveBackAt: null,
    });
  });

  it('stays where it is on Stay, and forgets the move back', () => {
    runtime.rateLimited(project.accountId, inMinutes(60), project.id);
    inbox.answer(card(LIMIT).id, { choice: 'Continue on Second plan' });
    clock.advance(60 * 60_000);
    inbox.answer(card(BACK).id, { choice: AccountCardChoice.Stay });
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: second.id,
      movedFromAccountId: null,
      moveBackAt: null,
    });
  });

  it('waits while the account it left is still paused, and asks when that pause ends', () => {
    const stayer = seedProject(store, { name: 'stayer' });
    runtime.rateLimited(project.accountId, inMinutes(60), project.id);
    inbox.answer(card(LIMIT).id, { choice: 'Continue on Second plan' });
    // A session of the project still on the account meets the limit again, later.
    runtime.rateLimited(project.accountId, inMinutes(120), stayer.id);
    clock.advance(60 * 60_000);
    expect(openCards().map((c) => c.title)).not.toContain(BACK);
    clock.advance(60 * 60_000);
    expect(card(BACK).projectId).toBe(project.id);
  });

  it('raises a move back that came due while the daemon was down', () => {
    runtime.rateLimited(project.accountId, inMinutes(60), project.id);
    inbox.answer(card(LIMIT).id, { choice: 'Continue on Second plan' });
    moveBack.stop();
    pauses.stop();
    clock.advance(90 * 60_000);
    build();
    pauses.restore();
    moveBack.restore();
    expect(card(BACK).projectId).toBe(project.id);
  });

  it('remembers nothing for a plain move, and moves nothing when another field of the change is refused', () => {
    moves.move(
      project.id,
      second.id,
      { cause: MoveCause.Builder },
      { patch: { accountId: second.id, maxLanes: 3 } },
    );
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: second.id,
      maxLanes: 3,
      movedFromAccountId: null,
    });
    expect(() =>
      moves.move(
        project.id,
        project.accountId,
        { cause: MoveCause.Builder },
        { patch: { accountId: project.accountId, nudgePct: 10 } },
      ),
    ).toThrow(InvalidError);
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id).accountId).toBe(second.id);
  });
});
```

```ts
const moving = changes.accountId !== undefined && changes.accountId !== current.accountId;

  .update(schema.projects)
```

- Add:

```ts
/** Records the account a project left for its usage limit and when to offer the way back; null forgets it. */
export function setMoveBack(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: ProjectId,
  moveBack: { readonly accountId: AccountId; readonly at: string } | null,
): ProjectDto {
  getProject(ctx.tx, id);
  ctx.tx
    .update(schema.projects)
    .set({
      movedFromAccountId: moveBack?.accountId ?? null,
      moveBackAt: moveBack?.at ?? null,
      updatedAt: ctx.now,
    })
    .where(eq(schema.projects.id, id))
    .run();
  ctx.emit({ type: EventType.ProjectChanged, entityType: 'project', entityId: id });
  return getProject(ctx.tx, id);
}

/** The projects 10db is to offer the way back to an account they left. */
export function listMoveBacks(db: DbReader): ProjectDto[] {
  return listProjects(db).filter((p) => p.moveBackAt !== null);
}
```

```ts
/** The fold key part of the card that offers a project the way back. */
export const MOVE_BACK_CARD = 'available';

/**
 * A usage limit paused every lane on the account. The card offers to continue the project on each
 * account it could move to now (accounts spec §6); a lane in flight starts there from its handoff.
 */
export function usageLimitCard(
  account: AccountRow,
  until: Date,
  project: ProjectDto,
  targets: readonly AccountDto[],
): AccountCard {
  const moves = targets.map((t) => ({
    choice: `Continue on ${accountDisplayName(t)}`,
    accountId: t.id,
  }));
  return {
    title: `${account.label} usage limit reached`,
    body: `Every lane on ${account.label} is paused until ${until.toISOString()}. Work resumes automatically.${
      moves.length > 0
        ? ` Or continue ${project.name} on another account now: each lane's next session runs there from its latest handoff, and 10db asks whether to move it back once ${account.label} is available again.`
        : ''
    }`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(account.id, AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit),
    detail: detail(account, AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit, null, null, { moves }),
    ...(moves.length > 0
      ? {
          options: [
            ...moves.map((m) => ({
              label: m.choice,
              description: `Move ${project.name} there until ${account.label} is available again`,
            })),
            { label: AccountCardChoice.Later, description: `Wait for ${account.label}` },
          ],
        }
      : {}),
  };
}

/** The account a project left for its usage limit is free again; nothing moves until the builder says so. */
export function moveBackCard(
  project: ProjectDto,
  from: AccountRow,
  current: AccountRow,
): AccountCard {
  return {
    title: `${from.label} is available again: move ${project.name} back?`,
    body: `${project.name} moved to ${current.label} when ${from.label} reached its usage limit, and that limit has reset. Move it back, and each lane's next session runs on ${from.label} from its latest handoff; or stay on ${current.label}.`,
    foldKey: accountFoldKey(from.id, MOVE_BACK_CARD),
    detail: detail(from, null, null, null, {
      moves: [{ choice: AccountCardChoice.MoveBack, accountId: from.id }],
      moveBack: true,
    }),
    options: [
      {
        label: AccountCardChoice.MoveBack,
        description: `Run ${project.name} on ${accountDisplayName(from)} again`,
      },
      {
        label: AccountCardChoice.Stay,
        description: `Keep ${project.name} on ${accountDisplayName(current)}`,
      },
    ],
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/moves.ts`:

```ts
import {
  AccountPauseReason,
  type AccountDto,
  type AccountId,
  type InboxItemId,
  type ProjectDto,
  type ProjectId,
  type ProjectPatch,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  findOpenByFoldKey,
  getProject,
  listAccounts,
  patchProject,
  setMoveBack,
  type DbReader,
} from '@10db/db';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { InboxService } from '../inbox';
import { accountFoldKey, MOVE_BACK_CARD } from './cards';
import type { MoveBack } from './move-back';
import type { AccountPauses } from './pauses';

export const MoveCause = { Builder: 'builder', UsageLimit: 'usage_limit' } as const;
export type MoveCause = (typeof MoveCause)[keyof typeof MoveCause];

/** Why a project moves: the builder chose to, or its account reached its usage limit. */
export type MoveWhy =
  | { readonly cause: typeof MoveCause.Builder }
  | { readonly cause: typeof MoveCause.UsageLimit; readonly resetsAt: Date | null };

export interface AccountMoves {
  /**
   * Moves the project, with `patch`'s other fields in the same write. Work in flight follows at each
   * lane's next session. A move off an account at its usage limit, or because of it, is remembered
   * for the move back. `answering` is the card whose answer this is, which the inbox closes itself.
   */
  move(
    projectId: ProjectId,
    to: AccountId,
    why: MoveWhy,
    opts?: { readonly patch?: ProjectPatch; readonly answering?: InboxItemId },
  ): ProjectDto;
  /** The builder keeps the project where it is: the move back is forgotten. */
  stay(projectId: ProjectId): void;
}

/** The accounts a project could move to now: not archived, not its own, not paused. */
export function moveTargets(
  db: DbReader,
  project: ProjectDto,
  isPaused: (id: AccountId) => boolean,
): AccountDto[] {
  return listAccounts(db).filter((a) => a.id !== project.accountId && !isPaused(a.id));
}

export function createAccountMoves(opts: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly inbox: InboxService;
  readonly pauses: Pick<AccountPauses, 'pausedFor'>;
  readonly moveBack: Pick<MoveBack, 'arm'>;
  /** How long a usage limit with no reported reset lasts: the runtime's default pause. */
  readonly defaultPauseMs: number;
  /** A project moved: the scheduler looks again, and re-queues work only the old account held. */
  onMoved(): void;
}): AccountMoves {
  const db = opts.state.db;

  /** A move makes the project's cards that offered one moot. */
  function withdrawMoot(
    project: ProjectDto,
    from: AccountId,
    answering: InboxItemId | undefined,
  ): void {
    const keys = [
      accountFoldKey(from, AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit),
      ...(project.movedFromAccountId === null
        ? []
        : [accountFoldKey(project.movedFromAccountId, MOVE_BACK_CARD)]),
    ];
    for (const key of keys) {
      const open = findOpenByFoldKey(db, project.id, key);
      if (open && open.id !== answering) opts.inbox.withdraw(open.id);
    }
  }

  return {
    move(projectId, to, why, { patch = {}, answering } = {}) {
      const project = getProject(db, projectId);
      const from = project.accountId;
      if (to === from)
        return opts.state.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, projectId, { ...patch, accountId: to }));
      const paused = opts.pauses.pausedFor(from);
      const limitEnds = paused?.reason === AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit ? paused.until : null;
      const forLimit = why.cause === MoveCause.UsageLimit || limitEnds !== null;
      const backAt =
        limitEnds ??
        (why.cause === MoveCause.UsageLimit ? why.resetsAt : null) ??
        new Date(opts.clock.now().getTime() + opts.defaultPauseMs);
      const moved = opts.state.write((ctx) => {
        patchProject(ctx, projectId, { ...patch, accountId: to });
        // Leaving for a limit is temporary: remember where from, and when that limit resets.
        return forLimit
          ? setMoveBack(ctx, projectId, { accountId: from, at: backAt.toISOString() })
          : getProject(ctx.tx, projectId);
      });
      withdrawMoot(project, from, answering);
      if (forLimit) opts.moveBack.arm(projectId);
      opts.onMoved();
      return moved;
    },
    stay(projectId) {
      opts.state.write((ctx) => setMoveBack(ctx, projectId, null));
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/move-back.ts`:

```ts
import { AccountPauseReason, type ProjectId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { getAccountRow, getProject, listMoveBacks, setMoveBack } from '@10db/db';
import type { Clock, Timer } from '../clock';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { InboxService } from '../inbox';
import { moveBackCard } from './cards';
import type { AccountPauses } from './pauses';

export interface MoveBack {
  /** Arms the reminder of a project that left an account for its usage limit, at its `moveBackAt`. */
  arm(projectId: ProjectId): void;
  /** Re-arms every reminder after a restart; one that came due meanwhile raises its card now. */
  restore(): void;
  stop(): void;
}

/** Asks, once the limit a project left for has reset, whether to move it back (accounts spec §6). */
export function createMoveBack(opts: {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly inbox: InboxService;
  readonly pauses: Pick<AccountPauses, 'pausedFor'>;
}): MoveBack {
  const db = opts.state.db;
  const timers = new Map<ProjectId, Timer>();

  function schedule(projectId: ProjectId, at: Date): void {
    timers.get(projectId)?.cancel();
    const wait = at.getTime() - opts.clock.now().getTime();
    if (wait <= 0) {
      timers.delete(projectId);
      due(projectId);
      return;
    }
    timers.set(
      projectId,
      opts.clock.setTimeout(() => {
        timers.delete(projectId);
        due(projectId);
      }, wait),
    );
  }

  function due(projectId: ProjectId): void {
    const project = getProject(db, projectId);
    if (project.movedFromAccountId === null) return;
    const from = getAccountRow(db, project.movedFromAccountId);
    if (from.archived || from.id === project.accountId) {
      opts.state.write((ctx) => setMoveBack(ctx, projectId, null));
      return;
    }
    const paused = opts.pauses.pausedFor(from.id);
    const stillLimited = paused?.reason === AccountPauseReason.UsageLimit ? paused.until : null;
    if (stillLimited !== null && stillLimited > opts.clock.now()) {
      // The limit was met again after the move: ask when it really ends.
      opts.state.write((ctx) =>
        setMoveBack(ctx, projectId, { accountId: from.id, at: stillLimited.toISOString() }),
      );
      schedule(projectId, stillLimited);
      return;
    }
    const card = moveBackCard(project, from, getAccountRow(db, project.accountId));
    opts.inbox.flag({
      projectId,
      workstreamId: null,
      title: card.title,
      body: card.body,
      detail: card.detail,
      foldKey: card.foldKey,
      ...(card.options ? { options: card.options } : {}),
    });
  }

  return {
    arm(projectId) {
      const at = getProject(db, projectId).moveBackAt;
      if (at !== null) schedule(projectId, new Date(at));
    },
    restore() {
      for (const project of listMoveBacks(db))
        if (project.moveBackAt !== null) schedule(project.id, new Date(project.moveBackAt));
    },
    stop() {
      for (const timer of timers.values()) timer.cancel();
      timers.clear();
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 11: A fallback account takes over at a usage threshold

A project may name a fallback account and a usage threshold (decisions 29 and 30). Claude Code's rate-limit event says how much of the account's limit is used and which limit it is; the daemon now carries both to the runner. When a session reports usage at or above a project's threshold, once per crossing:

- a project with a usable fallback has its lanes on the account nudged to hand off, moves to the fallback, and gets a card that says so;
- a project without one gets a card asking where to move it.

At the hard limit itself, a project with a usable fallback moves at once.

- Consumes: Task 9's lanes that follow a move; Task 10's `AccountMoves`, `MoveCause`, `MoveWhy`, `moveTargets`, `MOVE_BACK_CARD`, the card answers' `move`; Task 4's `AccountPauses`, `AccountRuntime.rateLimited` and the runner's `paused` outcome; Task 6's readiness (a fallback is prepared like any account before work runs on it); the runner's `HANDOFF_NUDGE` and `HANDOFF_NUDGE_READ_ONLY`.
- Produces:

  - db: `insertProject` and `patchProject` check a fallback when one is set (`InvalidError`); `archiveAccount` refuses an account a project names as its fallback (`ConflictError`).
  - Sessions: the `rate_limit` event gains `utilization: number | null` and `limitType: string | null`. The fake's `rate_limit` step takes optional `utilization` and `limitType`.
  - `accounts/limits.ts`: `interface UsageReport { status: 'allowed' | 'allowed_warning' | 'rejected'; utilization: number | null; limitType: string | null; resetsAt: Date | null }`; `interface AccountLimits { usageReported(accountId: AccountId, report: UsageReport): void }`; `createAccountLimits(opts)`.
  - `accounts/moves.ts`: `usableFallback(db, project: ProjectDto, isPaused: (id: AccountId) => boolean): AccountDto | null`. `MoveWhy`'s usage-limit variant gains `handOff: boolean`. `createAccountMoves` takes `handOff(projectId: ProjectId, accountId: AccountId, reason: string): void`.
  - `cards.ts`: `usageHighCard(project, account: AccountRow, targets: readonly AccountDto[], report: UsageReport)`, `movedCard(project, account: AccountRow, fallback: AccountDto, report: UsageReport)`, `limitName(limitType: string | null): string`, `USAGE_HIGH_CARD = 'usage_high'`, `MOVED_CARD = 'moved'`.
  - `AccountPauses.pause`'s `at.card` is `AccountCard | null`; `AccountRuntime.rateLimited` raises no card for a project no longer on the account.
  - Runner: `Runner.handOff(projectId: ProjectId, accountId: AccountId, reason: string): void`; `RunnerOptions.accounts` adds `Pick<AccountLimits, 'usageReported'>`.
  - `TestEngine.usageReports: { accountId: AccountId; report: UsageReport }[]`; the engine runs the real limits, so a usage report can move its project.

```ts
describe('fallback accounts', () => {
  it.each<{ name: string; fallback: () => AccountId; message: RegExp }>([
    {
      name: 'its own account',
      fallback: () => defaultAccountId(store.db) ?? mintId('Account'),
      message: /another account than its own/,
    },
    {
      name: 'an archived account',
      fallback: () => {
        const old = apiKey('Old key', 100);
        store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, old.id));
        return old.id;
      },
      message: /archived/,
    },
    {
      name: 'a metered account with no monthly cap',
      fallback: () => apiKey('Uncapped', null).id,
      message: /Set a monthly cap on "Uncapped"/,
    },
  ])('refuses a fallback that is $name', ({ fallback, message }) => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const id = fallback();
    expect(() =>
      store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { fallbackAccountId: id })),
    ).toThrow(message);
  });

  it('keeps the fallback while the project runs on it, and refuses to archive it', () => {
    const fallback = apiKey('Bedrock', 200);
    const project = seedProject(store, { fallbackAccountId: fallback.id });
    expect(project).toMatchObject({ fallbackAccountId: fallback.id, usageHandoffAt: 0.95 });
    store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: fallback.id }));
    expect(getProject(store.db, project.id).fallbackAccountId).toBe(fallback.id);
    store.write((ctx) => patchProject(ctx, project.id, { accountId: project.accountId }));
    expect(() => store.write((ctx) => archiveAccount(ctx, fallback.id))).toThrow(
      /fallback of "demo"/,
    );
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/accounts/limits.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL,
  AccountCardChoice,
  type AccountDto,
  type AccountId,
  type InboxAnswer,
  type InboxItemDto,
  type ProjectId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  createTestStore,
  getProject,
  listInbox,
  patchAccount,
  seedAccount,
  seedProject,
  type Store,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { createEventBus, createStateStore, type StateStore } from '../events';
import { createInboxService, type InboxService } from '../inbox';
import {
  createAccountCardAnswers,
  createAccountLimits,
  createAccountMoves,
  createAccountPauses,
  createMoveBack,
  type AccountLimits,
  type AccountPauses,
  type MoveBack,
  type UsageReport,
} from './index';

let store: Store;
let close: () => void;
let state: StateStore;
let clock: ManualClock;
let inbox: InboxService;
let pauses: AccountPauses;
let moveBack: MoveBack;
let limits: AccountLimits;
let answerCard: (item: InboxItemDto, answer: InboxAnswer) => void;
let handOffs: { projectId: ProjectId; accountId: AccountId }[];
let second: AccountDto;

beforeEach(() => {
  clock = new ManualClock(new Date(2026, 9, 20, 12));
  ({ store, close } = createTestStore(() => clock.now()));
  state = createStateStore(store, createEventBus());
  inbox = createInboxService({
    state,
    onParkedAnswer: () => undefined,
    onFlagAnswer: (item, answer) => {
      answerCard(item, answer);
    },
  });
  handOffs = [];
  pauses = createAccountPauses({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    stopAccount: () => Promise.resolve(),
    onResume: () => undefined,
  });
  moveBack = createMoveBack({ state, clock, inbox, pauses });
  const moves = createAccountMoves({
    state,
    clock,
    inbox,
    pauses,
    moveBack,
    defaultPauseMs: 30 * 60_000,
    handOff: (projectId, accountId) => {
      handOffs.push({ projectId, accountId });
    },
    onMoved: () => undefined,
  });
  limits = createAccountLimits({ state, inbox, pauses, moves });
  answerCard = createAccountCardAnswers({
    resume: () => undefined,
    confirm: () => undefined,
    move: (projectId, to, why, answering) => {
      moves.move(projectId, to, why, { answering });
    },
    stay: () => undefined,
  });
  second = seedAccount(store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Second plan' });
});
afterEach(() => {
  moveBack.stop();
  pauses.stop();
  close();
});

const inMinutes = (n: number) => new Date(clock.now().getTime() + n * 60_000);
const openCards = () => listInbox(state.db, { states: ['OPEN'] });
const report = (
  utilization: number | null,
  status: UsageReport['status'] = 'allowed_warning',
): UsageReport => ({ status, utilization, limitType: 'five_hour', resetsAt: inMinutes(120) });

describe('usage thresholds', () => {
  it('hands off and moves a project with a fallback once its threshold is crossed', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store, { fallbackAccountId: second.id });
    const plan = project.accountId;
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.9));
    expect(handOffs).toEqual([]);
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.96));
    expect(handOffs).toEqual([{ projectId: project.id, accountId: plan }]);
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: second.id,
      movedFromAccountId: plan,
      moveBackAt: inMinutes(120).toISOString(),
    });
    const [moved] = openCards();
    expect(moved?.title).toBe(
      'demo moved to Second plan: Claude plan is at 96% of its 5-hour limit',
    );
    expect(moved?.options).toEqual([]);
    expect(moved?.detail?.[ACCOUNT_CARD_DETAIL]).toMatchObject({
      accountId: plan,
      usage: { limitType: 'five_hour', utilization: 0.96 },
    });
  });

  it('asks once per crossing when the project has no usable fallback, and moves it when the builder answers', () => {
    const project = seedProject(store);
    const plan = project.accountId;
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.96));
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.97));
    const [asked] = openCards();
    if (!asked) throw new Error('no card asked');
    expect(openCards()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(asked.title).toBe(
      'Claude plan is at 96% of its 5-hour limit: hand off and move demo to Second plan?',
    );
    expect(asked.options.map((o) => o.label)).toEqual(['Move to Second plan', 'Later']);
    inbox.answer(asked.id, { choice: AccountCardChoice.Later });
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.98));
    expect(openCards()).toEqual([]);

    // The window reset and usage climbed again: a new crossing.
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.2));
    limits.usageReported(plan, report(0.95));
    const [again] = openCards();
    if (!again) throw new Error('no card asked again');
    inbox.answer(again.id, { choice: 'Move to Second plan' });
    expect(handOffs).toEqual([{ projectId: project.id, accountId: plan }]);
    expect(getProject(state.db, project.id)).toMatchObject({
      accountId: second.id,
      movedFromAccountId: plan,
    });
  });

  it('takes no fallback that is paused or metered without a cap, and asks instead', () => {
    const key = seedAccount(store, {
      kind: 'anthropic_api_key',
      label: 'Client key',
      monthlyCapUsd: 50,
    });
    const capless = seedProject(store, { name: 'capless', fallbackAccountId: key.id });
    state.write((ctx) => patchAccount(ctx, key.id, { monthlyCapUsd: null }));
    const held = seedProject(store, { name: 'held', fallbackAccountId: second.id });
    pauses.pause(
      second.id,
      { reason: 'refused', until: null },
      { projectId: held.id, card: null },

    limits.usageReported(capless.accountId, report(0.96));
    expect(handOffs).toEqual([]);

  it('moves a project with a fallback at the hard limit without a handoff, and leaves the rest to the usage-limit card', () => {
    const leaving = seedProject(store, { name: 'leaving', fallbackAccountId: second.id });
    const staying = seedProject(store, { name: 'staying' });
    limits.usageReported(leaving.accountId, report(null, 'rejected'));

    expect(getProject(state.db, leaving.id).accountId).toBe(second.id);
    expect(getProject(state.db, staying.id).accountId).toBe(leaving.accountId);
    expect(openCards().map((c) => c.title)).toEqual([
      'leaving moved to Second plan: Claude plan reached its 5-hour limit',
    ]);
  });

  it("uses the project's own threshold, and ignores a report with no utilization", () => {
    const early = seedProject(store, { fallbackAccountId: second.id, usageHandoffAt: 0.8 });
    limits.usageReported(early.accountId, report(null));
    expect(getProject(state.db, early.id).accountId).toBe(early.accountId);
    limits.usageReported(early.accountId, report(0.85));
    expect(getProject(state.db, early.id).accountId).toBe(second.id);
  });
});
```
